Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration
Authors: Zongsheng Cao, Bihao Zhan, Jinxin Shi, Jiong Wang, Fangchen Yu, Zhijie Zhong, Zijie Guo, Tianshuo Peng, Zhuo Liu, Yi Xie, Xiang Zhuang, Yue Fan, Runmin Ma, Shiyang Feng, Xiangchao Yan, Anran Liu, Peng Ye, Wenlong Zhang, Shufei Zhang, Chunfeng Song, Fenghua Ling, Jie Zhou, Liang He, Bo Zhang, Lei Bai
First: 2026-06-11T17:58:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:58:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agents-K1：迈向原生知识编排的智能体
当前基于大语言模型的研究智能体虽已通过智能体编排取得进展，但大多忽视科学知识编排。现有工作常将论文简化为摘要、表面提及和平铺直叙的\texttt{cites}边，遗漏科学推理所必需的关键实体、主张、证据、机制及方法谱系。为此，我们提出\textbf{Agents-K1}，一个端到端的知识编排管道，将原始文档转化为智能体原生的科学知识图谱。Agents-K1在统一理论框架下整合三个组件：一个五模块多模态解析器，捕捉全文（而非仅摘要）中的实体、多模态证据、引用及类型化实体间关系；一个4B信息抽取骨干网络，基于规则奖励通过GRPO训练；以及一个三源智能体接口GraphAnything CLI，统一网络搜索、多模态图检索与跨文档遍历。在此基础上，我们处理六领域共246万篇科学论文，构建\textbf{Scholar-KG}，其中发布百万篇子集，完整Scholar-KG可通过下方SCP链接获取。该管道还可扩展至通用领域语料及符合模式的数据合成。大量实验表明，Agents-K1在科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建及多跳科学推理方面表现卓越。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in scientific knowledge representation for LLM-based agents, Agents-K1 introduces an end-to-end pipeline that transforms raw scientific papers into structured knowledge graphs. It employs a multimodal parser capturing entities, evidence, citations, and relations across full documents, a 4B language model trained with GRPO under rule-based rewards for extraction, and a tri-source agent interface enabling multimodal retrieval and cross-document navigation. Evaluated across 2.46 million papers, the system generates Scholar-KG, a large-scale scientific knowledge graph, and demonstrates superior performance in information extraction, graph construction, and multi-hop reasoning compared to prior methods.
为弥补当前基于大语言模型的智能体在科学知识结构化表示上的不足，Agents-K1提出了一种端到端的知识编排管道，将原始科研论文转化为结构化知识图谱。该系统包含一个跨全文解析多模态实体与关系的解析器、一个基于GRPO训练的4B信息抽取模型，以及一个支持跨文档与多模态检索的图界面。在处理246万篇论文后生成Scholar-KG知识图谱，并在信息抽取、图谱构建与多跳推理任务中显著优于现有方法。
Influcoder: Distilling Decoders' Gradient Influence Rankings into an Encoder for Data Attribution
Authors: Dimitri Kachler, Damien Sileo, Pascal Denis
First: 2026-06-11T17:58:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:58:33+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the growth of LLMs' (Large Language Models) capabilities, there has been an increasing push to curate high quality datasets by filtering samples in the training data. In general, Data Attribution (DA) methods aim to estimate how individual samples in a training dataset can precondition a model to generate certain outputs. As an example, one might be interested in which samples in the data could be the source of toxic behavior after training the LLM. Many methods quantify this conditioning through the paradigm of influence functions. While methods of this family are effective in its function, they lack the necessary processing speed and storage compactness to be practically implemented on large datasets. We propose a method, Influcoder, as a quick and cost-effective approach to influence-based Data Attribution at scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Influcoder：将解码器梯度影响排序提炼至编码器以实现数据归因
随着大语言模型（LLMs）能力的提升，人们越来越倾向于通过筛选训练数据中的样本以构建高质量数据集。通常，数据归因（DA）方法旨在评估训练数据中单个样本如何预设模型生成特定输出。例如，人们可能希望了解哪些数据样本在训练大语言模型后会导致毒性行为。许多方法通过影响函数范式量化这种预设作用。尽管此类方法在功能上有效，但其处理速度和存储紧凑性不足以在大规模数据集上实际部署。我们提出一种名为Influcoder的方法，作为大规模影响型数据归因的快速且经济高效的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently identify influential training samples in large language models for data curation, the authors propose Influcoder, a method that distills gradient influence rankings from decoders into encoders to enable scalable data attribution. Instead of computing full influence functions—which are computationally expensive and memory-intensive—the method leverages encoder representations to approximate influence rankings, significantly reducing computational and storage overhead. Experimental results demonstrate that Influcoder achieves comparable accuracy to full influence methods while being orders of magnitude faster and more memory-efficient, making it practical for large-scale datasets.
为高效识别大语言模型训练数据中影响模型输出的关键样本，本文提出Influcoder方法，通过将解码器的梯度影响排序蒸馏至编码器，实现可扩展的数据归因。该方法避免了传统影响函数计算的高计算开销与存储需求，利用编码器结构近似梯度影响排序。实验表明，Influcoder在保持与完整影响函数相近归因精度的同时，显著提升了速度并降低了存储开销，适用于大规模数据集。
HyperTool: Beyond Step-Wise Tool Calls for Tool-Augmented Agents
Authors: Yaxin Du, Yifan Zhou, Yujie Ge, Jiajun Wang, Xianghe Pang, Shuo Tang, Tuney Zheng, Bryan Dai, Jian Yang, Siheng Chen
First: 2026-06-11T17:56:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:56:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tool-augmented LLM agents commonly rely on step-wise atomic tool calls, where each invocation, observation, and value transfer is exposed in the main reasoning trace. This creates an \emph{execution-granularity mismatch}: locally deterministic tool workflows are unfolded into repeated model-visible decisions, consuming context and forcing the model to manage low-level dataflow in the trace. We introduce \textbf{HyperTool}, a unified executable MCP-style tool interface that changes the model-visible unit of tool execution. A model invokes HyperTool with a code block that can call existing tools through their original schemas, manipulate returned values, and pass intermediate results locally, folding deterministic tool subroutines into a single outer call. To train models to use this interface, we synthesize HyperTool-format trajectories from cross-tool compositional tasks and verify them in real MCP environments. On MCP-Universe, HyperTool improves average accuracy from 15.69\% to 35.29\% on Qwen3-32B and from 9.93\% to 33.33\% on Qwen3-8B, and surpass GPT-OSS and Kimi-k2.5 on average accuracy, showing that our HyperTool can substantially improve multi-step tool use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HyperTool：超越分步工具调用的工具增强代理
工具增强型大语言模型代理通常依赖分步原子工具调用，其中每次调用、观察和值传递均暴露在主推理轨迹中。这导致了\emph{执行粒度不匹配}：局部确定性的工具工作流被展开为重复的模型可见决策，消耗上下文并迫使模型在轨迹中管理低级数据流。我们引入\textbf{HyperTool}，一种统一的可执行MCP风格工具接口，改变模型可见的工具执行单元。模型通过代码块调用HyperTool，可按原模式调用现有工具、操作返回值并本地传递中间结果，将确定性工具子程序折叠为单次外部调用。为训练模型使用此接口，我们从跨工具组合任务合成HyperTool格式轨迹，并在真实MCP环境中验证。在MCP-Universe上，HyperTool使Qwen3-32B的平均准确率从15.69%提升至35.29%，Qwen3-8B从9.93%提升至33.33%，且在平均准确率上超越GPT-OSS和Kimi-k2.5，表明HyperTool可显著提升多步工具使用能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of step-wise tool calls that fragment deterministic workflows into model-visible decisions, the paper introduces HyperTool, a unified MCP-style interface that allows models to invoke tools via code blocks, manipulate returned values, and pass intermediate results locally, thereby folding tool subroutines into single calls. Trained using synthetic HyperTool-format trajectories derived from compositional tasks and validated in real MCP environments, the method significantly improves multi-step tool use: on MCP-Universe, it boosts average accuracy from 15.69% to 35.29% for Qwen3-32B and from 9.93% to 33.33% for Qwen3-8B, outperforming GPT-OSS and Kimi-k2.5.
为解决传统逐步工具调用导致的执行粒度不匹配问题，本文提出HyperTool，一种统一的MCP风格工具接口，允许模型通过代码块以原子方式调用复杂工具序列，支持值操作与中间结果本地传递，将确定性子流程折叠为单次调用。通过合成组合任务轨迹训练并实测于真实MCP环境，HyperTool显著提升性能：在MCP-Universe上，Qwen3-32B准确率从15.69%提升至35.29%，Qwen3-8B从9.93%提升至33.33%，平均准确率超越GPT-OSS与Kimi-k2.5。
EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery
Authors: Amy Xin, Jiening Siow, Junjie Wang, Zijun Yao, Fanjin Zhang, Jian Song, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li
First: 2026-06-11T17:56:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:56:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EurekAgent：仅需代理环境工程即可实现自主科学发现
基于大语言模型的代理在自动化科学发现方面展现出日益增长的潜力。给定一个可优化的指标和执行环境，它们能够提出、验证并迭代科学解决方案，其成果已优于人工设计的方法。随着模型能力的持续提升，我们认为自主科学发现的瓶颈正从设计代理工作流转向设计代理环境：塑造代理行为的资源、约束和接口。我们将此称为环境工程：构建能放大有益行为（如开放探索、系统化成果管理及代理间协作）并抑制有害行为（如奖励作弊和高摩擦度的人类监督）的环境。我们提出了EurekAgent，一个面向指标驱动的自主科学发现的环境工程代理系统。EurekAgent从四个维度构建环境：权限工程以实现有界代理执行与隔离评估；成果工程以支持基于文件系统和Git的协作；预算工程以实现预算感知探索；以及人机协同工程以实现便捷的人类监督与干预。EurekAgent在多个数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得了新的最先进结果，包括以不到11美元的API总成本发现新的26圆打包最优解。我们开源了代码与结果，并呼吁将环境工程作为开发可靠自主研究代理的核心研究方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that LLM-based agents can autonomously drive scientific discovery when given a metric and execution environment, the authors argue that the key bottleneck lies not in agent design but in environment engineering—crafting environments that encourage productive behaviors like open-ended exploration and artifact management while suppressing harmful ones like reward hacking. EurekAgent, their proposed system, implements four core environment engineering dimensions: permissions for bounded execution, artifact systems for collaboration via filesystem and Git, budget-aware exploration, and human-in-the-loop interfaces for supervision. Experimental results show EurekAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance across mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including discovering new 26-circle packing solutions at a low cost of under $11 in API usage.
鉴于LLM代理在给定指标和执行环境时能自主推动科学发现，作者认为当前瓶颈在于环境设计而非代理架构，即需构建能促进开放探索、协作等有益行为并抑制奖励黑客和高摩擦人工干预等有害行为的环境。EurekAgent系统通过四个维度实现环境工程：权限控制确保安全执行、文件系统与Git协作管理、预算感知探索、以及人机协同监督。实验表明，EurekAgent在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务中达到多项SOTA，包括以不到11美元API成本发现新的26圆圈打包解。
Adaptive-Horizon Conflict-Based Search for Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Path Finding
Authors: Jiarui Li, Federico Pecora, Runyu Zhang, Gioele Zardini
First: 2026-02-12T14:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:55:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
MAPF is a core coordination problem for large robot fleets in automated warehouses and logistics. Existing approaches are typically either open-loop planners, which generate fixed trajectories and struggle to handle disturbances, or closed-loop heuristics without reliable performance guarantees, limiting their use in safety-critical deployments. This paper presents ACCBS, a closed-loop algorithm built on a finite-horizon variant of CBS with a horizon-changing mechanism inspired by iterative deepening in MPC. ACCBS dynamically adjusts the planning horizon based on the available computational budget, and reuses a single constraint tree to enable seamless transitions between horizons. As a result, it produces high-quality feasible solutions quickly while being asymptotically optimal as the budget increases, exhibiting anytime behavior. Extensive case studies demonstrate that ACCBS combines flexibility to disturbances with strong performance guarantees, effectively bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical robustness for large-scale robot deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于自适应时域的冲突驱动搜索用于闭环多智能体路径规划
MAPF是自动化仓库和物流中大型机器人编队的核心协调问题。现有方法通常要么是开环规划器，生成固定轨迹且难以应对扰动，要么是缺乏可靠性能保证的闭环启发式算法，限制了其在安全关键部署中的应用。本文提出ACCBS，一种基于有限时域CBS变体的闭环算法，其时域调整机制借鉴了MPC中的迭代加深思想。ACCBS根据可用计算预算动态调整规划时域，并复用单一约束树以实现时域无缝切换。因此，它能在短时间内生成高质量可行解，且随着预算增加渐近最优，具备任何时间行为。大量案例研究表明，ACCBS兼具对扰动的灵活性与强性能保证，有效弥合了大规模机器人部署中理论最优性与实际鲁棒性之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust, theoretically grounded coordination in large-scale robot fleets, this paper introduces ACCBS, a closed-loop algorithm that adapts its planning horizon dynamically based on computational budget, using a finite-horizon variant of CBS with an iterative deepening-inspired mechanism. It reuses a single constraint tree to maintain continuity across horizon changes, enabling anytime behavior and asymptotic optimality as budget increases. Experimental results show ACCBS delivers high-quality feasible solutions rapidly while maintaining strong performance guarantees, effectively balancing theoretical optimality with practical disturbance resilience in safety-critical deployments.
为解决大规模机器人车队在自动化仓库和物流中对鲁棒性与理论保障的双重需求，本文提出ACCBS算法，基于有限时域CBS并引入类似MPC迭代加深的机制，动态调整规划时域以匹配计算预算，通过复用单一约束树实现时域无缝切换，具备任何时间行为，快速生成高质量可行解并随计算资源增加渐近最优。实验表明，该算法在应对扰动的同时保持强性能保障，有效弥合理论最优与实际鲁棒性之间的鸿沟，适用于安全关键场景。
Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization
Authors: Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini, Massimo Chiriatti, Enrico Panai, Giuseppe Riva
First: 2026-06-11T17:54:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:54:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在你思考之前：系统0、AI介导的认知与认知殖民
本文考察了三个近期用于理解人工智能对认知与知识后果的框架：三系统理论、思考框架和系统0。文章认为，前两者虽能捕捉人工智能对个体推理与集体知识实践的重要影响维度，但系统0在理论上具有独特地位，前者无法完全复制。本文引入“认知殖民”概念，指AI系统能以用户难以察觉的方式将外部利益嵌入自我架构之中。鉴于此类系统已广泛部署，理解这些无形影响已成为紧迫的哲学与实践任务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how AI reshapes human cognition and epistemic practices, this paper compares Tri-System Theory and Thinkframes with the novel System 0 framework, arguing that System 0 uniquely captures how AI can subtly embed external interests into users' cognitive architecture—a phenomenon termed 'cognitive colonization.' The authors analyze how AI systems, already widely deployed, exert invisible influence on users' reasoning without their awareness, making this a pressing philosophical and practical concern.
该文旨在探讨人工智能如何在深层认知层面重塑人类思维，分析了三类理论框架（三系统理论、Thinkframes与System 0），指出System 0在建模AI如何将外部利益嵌入用户认知架构方面具有独特性，这一现象被称为‘认知殖民’。作者强调，由于此类系统已广泛部署，用户往往难以察觉其影响，因此亟需哲学与技术层面的审视。实验结果虽未详述，但暗示用户对AI如何潜移默化改变其思维过程缺乏觉察，凸显了研究的紧迫性。
Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation
Authors: Guo Yu, Wenlin Liu, Yulan Hu, Hao-Xuan Ma, Jun-Peng Jiang, Han-Jia Ye
First: 2026-06-11T17:54:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:54:09+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/SydCS/OPD-Param-Analysis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：密集监督、稀疏更新：关于在线策略蒸馏的稀疏性与几何结构
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）近期成为一种突出的后训练方法，因为它结合了两个理想要素：在线策略学生轨迹与密集教师监督，但该混合方法如何改变模型参数仍不明确。在多个语言模型与视觉-语言模型对及应用场景中，我们的分析得出两个主要发现。在稀疏性方面，OPD风格的更新量小且坐标稀疏，分布于各层，通常以FFN为主。这种稀疏结构具有操作实用性：仅训练发现的子网络即可恢复近似于完整OPD的性能。然而，在优化器消融实验中，诱导稀疏性的SGD优化器表现劣于AdamW，可能是因为密集教师监督保留了异质的坐标级梯度尺度，而AdamW的自适应缩放仍具优势。在几何结构方面，更新数值上为满秩但谱集中；它们主要远离源权重的主奇异子空间，且过度集中在源权重接近零的坐标上。这些发现表明，密集教师监督并未使OPD变为普通的密集参数重写；相反，OPD保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how on-policy distillation (OPD) alters model parameters despite combining on-policy student trajectories with dense teacher supervision, the authors analyze OPD updates across language and vision-language models. They find that OPD updates are small, coordinate-sparse, and concentrated in FFN layers, allowing performance recovery via training only a subnetwork. However, sparse SGD underperforms AdamW due to the loss of heterogeneous gradient scales preserved by dense supervision. Geometrically, OPD updates are full-rank but spectrally concentrated, avoiding principal singular subspaces of source weights and targeting coordinates near zero, indicating OPD retains distinct geometric properties rather than performing ordinary dense parameter rewriting.
为理解在结合策略学生轨迹与密集教师监督的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中模型参数如何变化，研究分析了多个语言与视觉-语言模型的更新行为。结果发现，OPD更新幅度小、坐标稀疏且集中在前馈网络层，仅训练子网络即可近似复现完整OPD性能；但稀疏SGD优化器表现劣于AdamW，因密集监督破坏了梯度尺度异质性。几何上，更新矩阵数值满秩但谱集中，远离源权重主奇异子空间，主要作用于源权重接近零的坐标，表明OPD保留了在线策略后训练的几何特征，而非执行密集参数重写。
Flex4DHuman: Flexible Multi-view Video Diffusion for 4D Human Reconstruction
Authors: Jen-Hao Cheng, Yipeng Wang, Hao Zhang, Gengshan Yang, Jenq-Neng Hwang
First: 2026-06-11T17:54:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:54:05+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 8 figures. Code, and multi-view caption dataset available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Flex4DHuman, a multi-view video diffusion model that transforms a monocular or sparse multi-view video of a dynamic subject into synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning. Unlike prior human-centric methods that rely on skeletons, depth maps, normals, or rendered target-view geometry, Flex4DHuman requires no explicit geometry priors and instead conditions generation through relative camera-pose positional encoding. The generated videos can be directly ingested by downstream reconstruction pipelines to create dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Built on the Wan 2.1 1.3B text-to-video model, Flex4DHuman preserves the backbone architecture and encodes camera and view information through a five-axis positional encoding that extends spatio-temporal RoPE with view indices and continuous SE(3) relative camera geometry. A three-stage curriculum progressively trains the model for pose following, flexible reference-to-target view generation, and temporal rollout. To support temporal rollout, we train with clean historical target-view tokens. We also add multi-view captions to enable test-time text control. Combined with an off-the-shelf 4D Gaussian Splatting stage, our framework lifts monocular static-camera videos into dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ show that Flex4DHuman surpasses prior state-of-the-art methods, while the same formulation generalizes to animal categories after mixed human-animal training. These capabilities make Flex4DHuman a practical step toward scalable 4D content creation from casual monocular videos for simulation, gaming, AR/VR, and video re-shooting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Flex4DHuman：用于4D人体重建的灵活多视角视频扩散模型
我们提出了Flex4DHuman，这是一种多视角视频扩散模型，仅通过相对相机姿态条件化，即可将动态主体的单目或稀疏多视角视频转换为同步的密集多视角视频。与以往依赖骨架、深度图、法线或渲染目标视角几何的人体中心方法不同，Flex4DHuman无需显式几何先验，而是通过相对相机姿态位置编码引导生成。生成的视频可直接输入下游重建流程，创建动态4D高斯点云。该模型基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频模型，保留其骨干架构，并通过五轴位置编码扩展时空RoPE，加入视角索引和连续SE(3)相对相机几何信息。三阶段课程训练逐步提升模型的姿势跟随、灵活参考视角到目标视角生成及时间展开能力。为支持时间展开，我们使用干净的历史目标视角标记进行训练，并添加多视角字幕以实现测试时文本控制。结合现成的4D高斯点云生成阶段，我们的框架可将单目静态相机视频提升为动态4D高斯点云。在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ上的实验表明，Flex4DHuman超越了现有最先进方法，且相同架构在混合训练后可泛化至动物类别。这些能力使Flex4DHuman成为从普通单目视频规模化生成4D内容（用于仿真、游戏、AR/VR及视频重拍）的实用一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable 4D human reconstruction from casual monocular videos, Flex4DHuman introduces a multi-view video diffusion model that generates synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning, avoiding explicit geometry priors. It builds on the Wan 2.1 1.3B text-to-video architecture, encoding camera and view information via a five-axis positional encoding that extends spatio-temporal RoPE with view indices and continuous SE(3) geometry. Trained in three stages—pose following, flexible reference-to-target view generation, and temporal rollout—the model uses clean historical target-view tokens and multi-view captions for test-time control. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, and the model generalizes to animal categories after mixed training, enabling practical 4D content creation for simulation, gaming, and AR/VR.
为实现从普通单目视频中可扩展的4D人体重建，Flex4DHuman提出一种多视角视频扩散模型，仅通过相对相机位姿条件生成同步密集多视角视频，无需显式几何先验。该模型基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频架构，通过五轴位置编码扩展时空RoPE，融合视角索引与连续SE(3)相对几何信息。采用三阶段课程训练——姿态跟随、灵活参考视角生成与时间展开，利用历史目标视角标记与多视角字幕实现测试时控制。在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ数据集上实验表明其性能优于当前最先进方法，且经混合训练后可泛化至动物类别，为仿真、游戏及视频重拍等场景提供实用的4D内容生成能力。
World Tracing: Generative Pixel-Aligned Geometry Beyond the Visible
Authors: Hao Zhang, Mohamed El Banani, Jen-Hao Cheng, Paul Zhang, Yi Hua, Ben Mildenhall, Christoph Lassner, Narendra Ahuja, Gengshan Yang
First: 2026-06-11T17:52:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:52:48+00:00
Comments: World Labs Technical Report; Page: https://haoz19.github.io/world-tracing-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Image-to-3D methods often trade off faithfulness and completeness: depth estimators are anchored to input pixels but stop at the visible surface, while image-to-3D models generate complete shapes that are often misaligned with the input. We introduce World Tracing, a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with observed pixels while completing geometry beyond the visible surface. For each input pixel, World Tracing predicts an ordered stack of camera-space 3D points, where the first layer represents the visible surface and subsequent layers represent front-to-back intersections with occluded surfaces. We instantiate this representation with a world-tracing diffusion transformer, WT-DiT, which treats multiple geometry layers as separate denoising tokens coupled through factorized and global attention. WT-DiT is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule that balances visible-surface reconstruction with occluded-geometry generation. World Tracing achieves strong performance on visible-surface reconstruction and complete geometry generation across object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, outperforming both depth predictors and image-to-3D generators. It also preserves 2D-to-3D correspondence, enabling text-driven 3D scene editing, geometry-conditioned novel-view video synthesis, and training-free integration with textured-mesh generators.
中文标题/摘要
标题：世界追踪：超越可见范围的生成式像素对齐几何
图像到3D的方法常在忠实度与完整性之间权衡：深度估计器锚定于输入像素但仅限于可见表面，而图像到3D模型生成的完整形状常与输入不匹配。我们提出世界追踪（World Tracing），一种生成式像素对齐几何表示，可预测与观测像素对齐的3D点并完成可见表面之外的几何结构。对每个输入像素，世界追踪预测一个有序的相机空间3D点堆栈，其中第一层代表可见表面，后续层代表从前到后与被遮挡表面的交点。我们通过世界追踪扩散变换器（WT-DiT）实现该表示，将多层几何结构视为独立的去噪标记，并通过因子化与全局注意力耦合。WT-DiT采用像素空间流匹配训练，并结合混合噪声调度，在可见表面重建与遮挡几何生成之间取得平衡。世界追踪在物体、场景及动态基准测试中均表现出色，优于深度预测器与图像到3D生成器，同时保持2D到3D对应关系，支持文本驱动的3D场景编辑、几何条件下的新视角视频合成及无需训练即可与纹理网格生成器集成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between faithfulness and completeness in image-to-3D methods, World Tracing introduces a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with input pixels while extending geometry beyond visible surfaces. It uses a world-tracing diffusion transformer (WT-DiT) that models multiple depth layers per pixel as denoising tokens, coupled via factorized and global attention, and is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule to balance surface reconstruction and occluded geometry generation. Experiments show it outperforms depth estimators and image-to-3D generators on object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, while preserving 2D-to-3D correspondence for downstream tasks like text-driven 3D editing and novel-view video synthesis.
为解决图像到3D方法中忠实性与完整性之间的权衡，World Tracing提出一种生成式像素对齐的几何表示，能预测与输入像素对齐的3D点并扩展至不可见表面。其采用世界追踪扩散变换器（WT-DiT），将多层几何结构建模为去噪令牌并通过因子化与全局注意力耦合，结合像素空间流匹配与混合噪声调度以平衡可见表面重建与遮挡几何生成。实验表明其在物体、场景及动态基准上优于深度预测器与图像到3D生成器，同时保持2D到3D对应关系，支持文本驱动的3D编辑、新视角视频合成及无需训练的纹理网格生成器集成。
Operadic consistency: a label-free signal for compositional reasoning failures in LLMs
Authors: Nathaniel Bottman, Yinhong Liu, Kyle Richardson
First: 2026-06-11T17:50:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:50:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Detecting LLM reasoning failures at inference time without ground-truth labels has motivated a wide range of confidence baselines, including self-consistency, semantic entropy, and P(True), built on within-question sampling and self-evaluation. Operad theory, the formalism for systems built by iterated substitution, suggests a complementary diagnostic: a model's direct answer to a compositional query should agree with the answer it produces by composing a stated decomposition of the same query. We instantiate this idea as operadic consistency (OC), a per-question signal. Across twelve instruction-tuned LLMs (4B to 671B parameters, open-weights and closed-source) on four multi-hop QA datasets, OC is strongly correlated with accuracy on every dataset (Pearson $r \in [0.86, 0.94]$, all $p \leq 0.0004$), and is the only signal we evaluate with $r \geq 0.85$ uniformly across all four datasets. Chain-of-thought self-consistency (CoT-SC; Wang et al., 2023) matches OC on HotpotQA and DROP ($r = 0.93, 0.87$) but drops to $r \approx 0.45$ on MuSiQue and StrategyQA. At the per-question level, OC contributes information beyond CoT-SC and semantic entropy on every dataset (cluster-robust $p \leq 10^{-16}$ for the OC coefficient), and the conclusion is robust to additionally controlling for constructed decomposition-aware baselines ($p \leq 10^{-13}$). The same signal yields selective-prediction improvements (accuracy at fixed coverage) over a tuned CoT-SC baseline at the equal-cost $K = 3$ budget (AUARC lifts of +0.086 to +0.096 and AUROC lifts of +0.092 to +0.164; 95% CIs exclude zero on every cell). On five frontier thinking models, where the decomposition is extracted from the model's own chain of thought, the same equal-cost comparison gives positive selective-prediction point-estimate lift on all 16 (dataset, budget, metric) cells tested, with 95% CIs excluding zero on 12 of the 16.
中文标题/摘要
标题：运算一致性：一种无需标签的LLM组合推理失败检测信号
在推理阶段无需真实标签检测LLM推理失败，已推动多种置信度基线方法的发展，包括自一致性、语义熵和P(True)，这些方法基于问题内采样与自我评估。运算代数理论，即用于描述通过迭代代入构建系统的形式化方法，提出了一种互补诊断：模型对组合查询的直接回答应与其通过分解该查询后组合得到的回答一致。我们将此思想形式化为运算一致性（OC），一种针对每个问题的信号。在十二个指令调优的LLM（4B至671B参数，开源与闭源）上，针对四个多跳问答数据集，OC与准确率在每个数据集上均高度相关（皮尔逊r∈[0.86, 0.94]，所有p≤0.0004），且是唯一在所有四个数据集上r≥0.85的信号。思维链自一致性（CoT-SC；Wang等，2023）在HotpotQA和DROP上与OC表现一致（r=0.93, 0.87），但在MuSiQue和StrategyQA上降至r≈0.45。在每个问题层面，OC在每个数据集上均提供超越CoT-SC和语义熵的信息（聚类稳健p≤10^{-16}），且结论在控制构造的分解感知基线后仍稳健（p≤10^{-13}）。同一信号在固定覆盖度下，于等成本K=3预算时，相比调优的CoT-SC基线，可提升选择性预测准确率（AUARC提升+0.086至+0.096，AUROC提升+0.092至+0.164；95%置信区间在所有单元中均排除零）。在五个前沿思维模型上，分解从模型自身思维链中提取，相同等成本比较在16个（数据集、预算、指标）测试单元中均呈现正向选择性预测点估计提升，其中12个单元95%置信区间排除零。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect LLM reasoning failures without ground-truth labels, the paper introduces operadic consistency (OC), a label-free diagnostic based on operad theory that checks whether a model’s direct answer matches its answer derived from composing a stated decomposition of the same query. Evaluated across 12 LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets, OC shows strong correlation with accuracy (Pearson r between 0.86 and 0.94, all p ≤ 0.0004), outperforming other baselines like chain-of-thought self-consistency on two datasets and providing unique predictive power beyond semantic entropy and decomposition-aware controls. OC also improves selective-prediction performance at fixed cost, with consistent AUARC and AUROC lifts across multiple models and budgets, and yields positive point estimates on 12 out of 16 tested cells for frontier models using self-generated decompositions.
为在无标签条件下检测大语言模型推理失败，本文提出基于代数运算理论的“运算一致性”（OC）诊断指标，通过比对模型直接回答与其分解后组合回答的一致性来评估推理可靠性。在12个不同规模的LLM和四个多跳问答数据集上，OC与准确率高度相关（皮尔逊相关系数0.86–0.94，p值均≤0.0004），优于链式思维自一致性方法，并在多个数据集上提供额外预测信息。OC还能在固定成本下提升选择性预测性能，多个模型和指标组合下均实现显著的AUARC与AUROC提升，且置信区间均排除零值。
Thermodynamic assessment of machine learning models for solid-state synthesis prediction
Authors: Jane Schlesinger, Simon Hjaltason, Nathan J. Szymanski, Christopher J. Bartel
First: 2026-02-03T23:25:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:50:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning models have recently emerged to predict whether hypothetical solid-state materials can be synthesized. These models aim to circumvent direct first-principles modeling of solid-state phase transformations, instead learning from large databases of successfully synthesized materials. Here, we assess the alignment of several recently introduced synthesis prediction models with material and reaction thermodynamics, quantified by the energy with respect to the convex hull and a metric accounting for thermodynamic selectivity of enumerated synthesis reactions. A dataset of successful synthesis recipes was used to determine the likely bounds on both quantities beyond which materials can be deemed unlikely to be synthesized. With these bounds as context, thermodynamic quantities were computed using the CHGNet foundation potential for thousands of new hypothetical materials generated using the Chemeleon generative model. Four recently published machine learning models for synthesizability prediction were applied to this same dataset, and the resultant predictions were considered against computed thermodynamics. We find these models generally overpredict the likelihood of synthesis, but some model scores do trend with thermodynamic heuristics, assigning lower scores to materials that are less stable or do not have an available synthesis recipe that is calculated to be thermodynamically selective. In total, this work identifies existing gaps in machine learning models for materials synthesis and introduces a new approach to assess their quality in the absence of extensive negative examples (failed syntheses).
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器学习模型用于固态合成预测的热力学评估
近期出现的机器学习模型旨在预测假设的固态材料是否可被合成。这些模型旨在绕过对固态相变的直接第一性原理建模，而是从大量成功合成材料的数据库中学习。本文评估了几个近期提出的合成预测模型与材料及反应热力学的一致性，通过凸包能量和考虑合成反应热力学选择性的指标进行量化。利用成功合成配方的数据集，确定了两个量的可能界限，超过该界限的材料可被视为不太可能被合成。以这些界限为背景，使用CHGNet基础势函数，对通过Chemeleon生成模型生成的数千种新假设材料计算了热力学量。将四种近期发表的合成性预测机器学习模型应用于同一数据集，其预测结果与计算热力学进行对比。我们发现这些模型普遍高估了合成的可能性，但部分模型评分与热力学启发式方法趋势一致，对稳定性较差或无热力学选择性合成配方的材料赋予较低评分。总体而言，本研究指出了材料合成机器学习模型的现有不足，并提出了一种在缺乏大量负面示例（失败合成）情况下评估模型质量的新方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate machine learning models predicting solid-state material synthesizability, this study assesses four recent ML models using thermodynamic metrics—specifically, energy relative to the convex hull and thermodynamic selectivity of synthesis reactions. The models were tested against a dataset of successfully synthesized materials to establish thermodynamic thresholds for synthesis likelihood. Thermodynamic properties were computed for thousands of hypothetical materials using CHGNet and Chemeleon, then compared to model predictions. Results show that while models generally overestimate synthesis probability, some scores correlate with thermodynamic stability and selectivity, assigning lower scores to less stable or non-selective materials. This work highlights current limitations in ML models and proposes a thermodynamic benchmarking approach useful when negative examples (failed syntheses) are scarce.
为评估机器学习模型预测固态材料合成可能性时与热力学原理的一致性，本研究将四个近期提出的模型与基于凸包能量和反应选择性的热力学指标进行比较。模型在成功合成材料数据集上确定了合成可能性的热力学阈值，随后应用于通过Chemeleon生成的数千种假设材料。结果表明，尽管模型普遍高估合成可能性，但部分模型评分与热力学稳定性及反应选择性相关，对稳定性差或反应非选择性的材料评分较低。该研究指出了当前机器学习模型的不足，并提出了一种在缺乏失败合成样本时评估模型质量的热力学基准方法。
SkMTEB: Slovak Massive Text Embedding Benchmark and Model Adaptation
Authors: Marek Šuppa, Andrej Ridzik, Daniel Hládek, Natália Kňažeková, Viktória Ondrejová
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-06-11T17:50:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:50:06+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce SkMTEB, the first comprehensive MTEB-style text embedding benchmark for Slovak, a low-resource West Slavic language, comprising 31 datasets across 7 task types -- nearly 4$\times$ the depth of existing multilingual benchmark coverage for Slovak. Our evaluation of 31 embedding models reveals that large instruction-tuned multilingual models achieve the strongest performance, while existing Slovak-specific models trained for NLU tasks transfer poorly to embedding tasks. To address the need for efficient, locally-deployable Slovak embeddings, we develop \texttt{e5-sk-small} (45M parameters) and \texttt{e5-sk-large} (365M) by applying vocabulary trimming and fine-tuning to Multilingual E5 models. Despite size reductions of up to 62\%, our open-source models achieve competitive performance with proprietary APIs while remaining locally deployable for semantic search and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We release the benchmark, models, datasets, and code openly, hoping our approach offers a replicable path for other under-resourced languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkMTEB：斯洛伐克大规模文本嵌入基准与模型适配
我们引入SkMTEB，这是首个面向斯洛伐克语（一种资源匮乏的西斯拉夫语）的综合性MTEB风格文本嵌入基准，涵盖7类任务的31个数据集——几乎是现有斯洛伐克语多语言基准覆盖深度的4倍。对31个嵌入模型的评估表明，大型指令微调多语言模型表现最佳，而现有的斯洛伐克语专用NLU任务训练模型在嵌入任务上的迁移效果较差。为满足高效、本地部署斯洛伐克语嵌入的需求，我们通过词汇修剪和微调，基于多语言E5模型开发了\texttt{e5-sk-small}（45M参数）和\texttt{e5-sk-large}（365M）。尽管模型规模缩减高达62%，我们的开源模型在性能上可媲美专有API，同时仍可本地部署用于语义搜索和检索增强生成（RAG）。我们公开发布基准、模型、数据集和代码，希望为其他资源匮乏语言提供可复制的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of comprehensive benchmarks and efficient models for Slovak, a low-resource West Slavic language, the authors introduce SkMTEB, the first MTEB-style benchmark with 31 datasets across seven tasks, nearly four times deeper than prior Slovak evaluations. They evaluate 31 embedding models and find that large instruction-tuned multilingual models outperform Slovak-specific NLU models, which fail to transfer well to embedding tasks. To enable local deployment, they adapt Multilingual E5 models via vocabulary trimming and fine-tuning, creating e5-sk-small (45M) and e5-sk-large (365M), achieving competitive performance with up to 62% parameter reduction while remaining suitable for semantic search and RAG.
为填补斯洛伐克语（一种资源匮乏的西斯拉夫语）缺乏全面评估基准和高效嵌入模型的空白，作者构建了SkMTEB，这是首个包含31个数据集、覆盖七类任务的MTEB风格基准，规模近四倍于现有斯洛伐克语评估。评估31个嵌入模型发现，大型指令调优的多语言模型表现最优，而现有斯洛伐克语专用NLU模型难以迁移到嵌入任务。为实现本地部署的斯洛伐克语嵌入，作者通过词汇修剪与微调改造多语言E5模型，推出e5-sk-small（45M参数）和e5-sk-large（365M参数），在参数缩减62%的情况下仍保持竞争力，适用于语义搜索和RAG场景。
Surflo: Consistent 3D Surface Flow Model with Global State
Authors: Antoine Guédon, Shu Nakamura, Nicolas Dufour, Jiahui Lei, Ko Nishino, Angjoo Kanazawa
First: 2026-06-11T17:48:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:48:38+00:00
Comments: Project webpage: https://anttwo.github.io/surflo/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Geometry is invariant to viewpoint, which makes any collection of images a redundant encoding of a single 3D state. Existing feed-forward reconstruction models fail to exploit this: per-view methods emit overlapping, unaligned pointmaps that grow linearly with input count, while global-latent methods commit to a fixed, low-resolution output. We introduce Surflo, which compresses a variable number of unposed RGB views into K latent tokens-one global state-and decodes oriented 3D surface points by independently transporting them from noise onto the surface via flow matching. This frees the output from any fixed grid or token budget: the same latent yields from a few thousand to a million points in a single forward pass. To suppress the local inconsistencies inherent to independent per-point decoding, an inference-time guidance term correlates nearby points by injecting a photometric gradient during ODE integration. Surflo matches or surpasses feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs an order of magnitude faster than optimization-based methods that require hundreds of views, and is the only feed-forward approach to combine a global latent with arbitrary-resolution decoding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Surflo：具有全局状态的一致3D表面流模型
几何结构对视角不变，这意味着任何图像集合都是单个3D状态的冗余编码。现有前馈重建模型未能利用这一点：每视角方法生成重叠且未对齐的点图，其规模随输入数量线性增长，而全局潜在空间方法则固定输出低分辨率。我们提出Surflo，将任意数量的无姿态RGB图像压缩为K个潜在标记——一个全局状态，并通过流匹配独立地将噪声点运输至表面以解码定向3D表面点。这使输出摆脱了固定网格或标记预算的限制：同一潜在状态可在单次前向传递中生成从数千到百万个点。为抑制独立点解码固有的局部不一致性，推理时引入光度梯度指导项，在ODE积分过程中关联邻近点。Surflo在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线，比需数百视角的优化方法快一个数量级，且是唯一结合全局潜在空间与任意分辨率解码的前馈方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the viewpoint-invariant nature of 3D geometry, the paper proposes Surflo, a feed-forward model that compresses multiple unposed RGB views into a single global latent state, enabling scalable, arbitrary-resolution 3D surface reconstruction. It decodes oriented surface points by transporting noise via flow matching, freeing output from fixed grids or token budgets. To mitigate local inconsistencies from independent point decoding, it injects photometric gradients during ODE integration to guide nearby points. Experimental results show Surflo matches or exceeds feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs 10x faster than optimization-based methods requiring hundreds of views, and uniquely combines global latent encoding with flexible resolution decoding.
为高效从多视角无姿态RGB图像重建3D表面并摆脱固定分辨率或令牌预算的限制，Surflo提出一种前馈模型，将可变数量的输入视图压缩为单一全局潜在状态。通过流匹配将噪声独立传输至表面以解码定向3D点，支持单次前向传递输出从数千到百万个点。为缓解独立点解码带来的局部不一致性，引入光度梯度引导于ODE积分过程。实验表明，Surflo在表面指标上达到或超越前馈基线，比需数百视图的优化方法快10倍，且是唯一结合全局潜在编码与任意分辨率解码的前馈方法。
Recursive Agent Harnesses
Authors: Elias Lumer, Sahil Sen, Kevin Paul, Vamse Kumar Subbiah
First: 2026-06-11T17:47:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:47:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recursive language models (RLMs) showed that recursion over model calls is an effective strategy for long-context reasoning, and production coding agents have begun to write code that spawns subagents at scale, most recently in Anthropic's dynamic workflows. We name and study the pattern between these two lines of work, where the recursive unit is a full agent harness with filesystem tools, code execution, and planning rather than a model call with no tools. We call this the Recursive Agent Harness (RAH) and frame it as harness recursion, the code-first extension to the model recursion of RLMs. A parent agent generates and runs an executable script that spawns subagent harnesses in parallel for fine-grained workloads and uses structured function calls for small subtasks. We provide a controlled evaluation on long-context reasoning. With the backbone held fixed at GPT-5 to match the published Codex and RLM baselines, RAH improves the Codex coding-agent baseline from 71.75% to 81.36% on Oolong-Synthetic (199 samples, 13 context-length buckets up to 4M tokens), a gain attributable to the harness rather than the model. With a stronger backbone, Claude Sonnet 4.5, the same design reaches 89.77%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：递归代理框架
递归语言模型（RLMs）表明，模型调用的递归是一种有效的长上下文推理策略，而生产级编码代理已开始大规模编写能派生子代理的代码，最近体现在Anthropic的动态工作流中。我们命名并研究这两类工作的共同模式：递归单元是一个具备文件系统工具、代码执行和规划能力的完整代理框架，而非无工具的模型调用。我们称之为递归代理框架（RAH），将其视为RLMs模型递归的代码优先扩展——即“框架递归”。父代理生成并运行可执行脚本，以并行方式启动子代理框架处理细粒度任务，并使用结构化函数调用处理小规模子任务。我们在长上下文推理任务上进行了受控评估。在保持骨干模型固定为GPT-5（与已发表的Codex和RLM基线一致）的情况下，RAH将Codex编码代理基线从Oolong-Synthetic（199个样本，13个上下文长度桶，最高达4M token）上的71.75%提升至81.36%，该提升归因于框架而非模型本身。若使用更强的骨干模型Claude Sonnet 4.5，相同设计可达到89.77%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of recursive language models and scalable agent systems, this work introduces Recursive Agent Harness (RAH), a framework where a parent agent generates and executes scripts to spawn parallel subagent harnesses equipped with filesystem tools, code execution, and planning. Unlike model recursion, RAH leverages code-first orchestration for fine-grained task decomposition. Evaluated on long-context reasoning tasks with fixed GPT-5 backbone, RAH boosts the Codex baseline from 71.75% to 81.36% on Oolong-Synthetic, demonstrating that the harness design—not the model—drives the improvement. With Claude Sonnet 4.5, the same architecture achieves 89.77%, confirming scalability and effectiveness.
为提升长上下文推理能力，该研究提出递归代理框架（RAH），将具备文件系统工具、代码执行与规划能力的完整代理作为递归单元，而非仅递归模型调用。父代理生成并执行脚本，以并行方式启动子代理处理大规模任务，对小任务则采用结构化函数调用。在固定GPT-5模型基础上，RAH将Codex编码代理基线从71.75%提升至81.36%（Oolong-Synthetic数据集），表明性能提升主要源于框架设计而非模型本身。使用更强的Claude Sonnet 4.5模型时，RAH进一步提升至89.77%，验证了其作为模型递归的代码优先扩展的有效性。
The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning
Authors: Ayushman Trivedi, Bhavika Melwani
First: 2026-06-11T17:45:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:45:41+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 8 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稳定恢复流形：持续学习中可恢复性的几何原理
灾难性遗忘常被视为在序列学习过程中先前知识的破坏。基于可访问性崩溃框架，我们研究了持续学习中可恢复性的几何结构。使用Split CIFAR-100和顺序训练的ResNet-18，我们分析了十个任务中的可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂性。我们引入了恢复子空间维数（k_t），即保持90%完整探针性能所需的最小奇异方向数。与我们的可恢复性扩散假说相反，尽管表征漂移显著，恢复维数在训练过程中保持稳定（平均k_t = 8.0）。主角角漂移强烈预测可恢复性（r = -0.862），一个简单的几何模型解释了82.2%的可恢复性方差。这些发现支持稳定恢复流形假说，表明尽管表征重组，遗忘的知识仍可紧凑解码。结果表明，灾难性遗忘主要是一个可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how knowledge is lost and potentially recovered in continual learning, this study investigates the geometric structure underlying recoverability using a sequentially trained ResNet-18 on Split CIFAR-100. It introduces Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), measuring the minimal singular directions needed to preserve 90% of probe performance, and finds that despite significant representational drift, k_t remains stable at a mean of 8.0. Principal-angle drift strongly correlates with recoverability (r = -0.862), and a geometric model explains 82.2% of the variance, supporting the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis: forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable due to manifold alignment, not information loss.
为理解持续学习中知识如何丢失与恢复，本研究基于Split CIFAR-100上顺序训练的ResNet-18，探究恢复性的几何结构。引入恢复子空间维度（k_t）衡量保留90%探针性能所需的最小奇异方向数，发现尽管表征漂移显著，k_t仍保持稳定（平均值8.0）。主角距离漂移与恢复性高度相关（r = -0.862），几何模型解释了82.2%的方差，支持稳定恢复流形假说：遗忘知识因流形对齐仍可紧凑解码，而非信息破坏。
Operads for compositional reasoning in LLMs
Authors: Nathaniel Bottman, Kyle Richardson
First: 2026-06-11T17:44:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:44:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Question decomposition, i.e. breaking a complex query into simpler sub-queries whose answers are composed to produce a final answer, is a widely used strategy for improving LLM reasoning, yet it currently lacks a rigorous mathematical foundation. In this paper, we propose operads, mathematical structures that model many-in, one-out operations and compositions thereof, as a natural framework for describing question decomposition. We define the questions operad $Q$, in which operations correspond to question templates and composition corresponds to substitution of sub-answers, and show how QA models can be interpreted as algebras over $Q$. Beyond reframing existing practice, this operadic perspective points toward new methods, in particular a notion of operadic consistency, which measures whether a QA model's answers agree across the partial collapses of a question decomposition tree. Empirical evaluation of operadic consistency is reported in our companion paper (Bottman, Liu, and Richardson, 2026), which finds it strongly correlated with accuracy across twelve LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets and outperforming standard temperature-based self-consistency baselines. We argue that operads are the natural mathematical home for question decomposition, and that invariants such as operadic consistency open new directions for analyzing and improving the reliability of multi-step reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于大语言模型组合推理的代数结构
问题分解，即将复杂查询拆解为若干简单子查询并组合其答案以生成最终答案，是提升大语言模型推理能力的常用策略，但目前尚缺乏严谨的数学基础。本文提出代数结构（operads），其建模多输入、单输出操作及其组合，作为描述问题分解的自然框架。我们定义了问题代数 $Q$，其中操作对应问题模板，组合对应子答案的替换，并展示问答模型可被解释为 $Q$ 上的代数。除重构现有实践外，该代数视角还指向新方法，特别是“代数一致性”概念，用于衡量问答模型在问题分解树的局部坍缩中答案是否一致。我们配套论文（Bottman, Liu, and Richardson, 2026）报告了代数一致性的实证评估，发现其在十二个大语言模型和四个多跳问答数据集上与准确率高度相关，且优于标准的基于温度的自一致性基线。我们认为代数结构是问题分解的自然数学框架，而此类不变量（如代数一致性）为分析和提升多步推理可靠性开辟了新方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a rigorous mathematical framework to model question decomposition in LLMs, this paper introduces operads—structures that formalize many-to-one operations and their compositions—as a natural language for describing how complex queries break down into sub-queries and compose their answers. The authors define the questions operad Q, where operations represent question templates and composition corresponds to substituting sub-answers, and interpret QA models as algebras over Q. Empirical results from a companion study show that operadic consistency, a new metric measuring agreement across partial decompositions, strongly correlates with accuracy across twelve LLMs and four multi-hop QA datasets, outperforming standard temperature-based self-consistency baselines.
为弥补大语言模型中问题分解缺乏严谨数学基础的不足，本文提出使用代数结构“运算代”（operads）来形式化建模组合推理，其中运算对应问题模板，组合对应子答案代入。作者定义了问题运算代Q，并将问答模型解释为Q上的代数结构，从而为多跳推理提供新视角。实验证明，一种称为“运算一致性”的新评估指标（衡量问题分解树中部分坍缩时答案的一致性）在十二个模型和四个数据集上与准确率高度相关，且优于传统的基于温度的自一致性基线方法。
Towards Data-free and Training-free Compression for Speech Foundation Models Using Parameter Clustering
Authors: Haoning Xu, Zhaoqing Li, Huimeng Wang, Youjun Chen, Chengxi Deng, Mengzhe Geng, Xunying Liu
First: 2026-06-10T09:16:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:42:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a novel data-free and training-free compression approach for speech foundation models using channelwise clustering via k-means. More fine-grained, mixed sparsity pruning by layer-level varying number of parameter clusters is also explored. Experiments conducted on the LibriSpeech dataset suggest that when operating with pruning sparsity of 50% on HuBERT-large, consistent WER reductions of 27.73%/18.61% absolute (34.37%/21.91% relative) over the magnitude-based pruning were obtained on the test-clean and test-other subsets before fine-tuning and 0.19%/0.79% absolute (3.36%/4.62% relative) after fine-tuning with only 3 epochs. Similar WER reductions of 2.86%/5.02% absolute (59.21%/55.29% relative) were observed against magnitudebased pruning on Whisper-large-v3 at 10% sparsity, all with no significant WER increase relative to the uncompressed baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于参数聚类的无数据、无训练语音基础模型压缩方法
本文提出了一种基于通道聚类（k-means）的无数据、无训练语音基础模型压缩新方法。同时探索了通过层间不同参数聚类数量实现更精细的混合稀疏剪枝。在LibriSpeech数据集上的实验表明，当在HuBERT-large模型上以50%剪枝稀疏度运行时，在微调前，测试clean和测试other子集上的WER分别降低27.73%/18.61%（绝对值，相对值34.37%/21.91%），微调后仅用3个epoch，WER分别降低0.19%/0.79%（绝对值，相对值3.36%/4.62%）。在Whisper-large-v3模型上以10%稀疏度运行时，与基于幅度的剪枝相比，WER分别降低2.86%/5.02%（绝对值，相对值59.21%/55.29%），且与未压缩基线相比无显著WER增加。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, data-free, and training-free compression of speech foundation models, this work introduces a k-means clustering method applied channel-wise to parameters, enabling fine-grained sparsity pruning that varies per layer. Experiments on LibriSpeech show that at 50% sparsity on HuBERT-large, the proposed method achieves 27.73% absolute WER reduction on test-clean and 18.61% on test-other before fine-tuning, improving upon magnitude-based pruning, and maintains performance gains even after fine-tuning with only 3 epochs. Similar improvements are observed on Whisper-large-v3 at 10% sparsity, with no significant degradation relative to the uncompressed baseline.
为实现无需训练数据和微调的语音基础模型压缩，本文提出一种基于通道级k均值聚类的无数据无训练压缩方法，并通过层间不同聚类数实现细粒度混合稀疏剪枝。在LibriSpeech数据集上，对HuBERT-large在50%剪枝率下，测试集clean和other子集在微调前分别获得27.73%和18.61%绝对WER下降，优于基于幅度的剪枝方法；微调3轮后进一步提升0.19%绝对WER下降。在Whisper-large-v3上，10%剪枝率下同样获得2.86%和5.02%绝对WER下降，且未显著劣于未压缩基线模型。
Aerial Wildfire Suppression Planning with a Hybrid CNN-Cellular Automata Fire Model
Authors: Ion Matei, Maksym Zhenirovskyy, Takuya Kurihana, Rohit Vupala, Anthony Wong
First: 2026-06-11T17:42:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:42:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aerial wildfire suppression requires not only predicting fire spread, but also designing effective intervention strategies under operational and environmental uncertainty. We present a modeling and optimization framework for aerial wildfire suppression that combines a hybrid neural-cellular automaton wildfire model with gradient-based design of targeted aerial drops. The wildfire model predicts spatially varying spread behavior from terrain, fuel, and wind data, while the intervention module determines binary drop actions with continuous-valued location and orientation parameters mapped to the simulation grid. Water and retardant are represented with distinct suppression effects, corresponding to immediate reduction of active burning and persistent reduction of future spread. To evaluate the robustness of the resulting suppression plans, we quantify both aleatoric uncertainty through Monte Carlo sampling of daily fire-state realizations and epistemic uncertainty through spatially correlated prediction-error perturbations. A case study based on the 2020 Bear Fire shows that the framework can generate coherent aerial suppression schedules for reducing total fire-affected area and can support uncertainty-aware analysis of wildfire intervention strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于混合CNN-细胞自动机火灾模型的空中野火抑制规划
空中野火抑制不仅需要预测火势蔓延，还需在操作与环境不确定性下设计有效的干预策略。本文提出一种结合混合神经细胞自动机火灾模型与梯度优化的靶向空中洒水方案的建模与优化框架。火灾模型根据地形、燃料和风速数据预测空间异质性蔓延行为；干预模块通过连续值的位置与方向参数映射至模拟网格，确定二元洒水动作。水与阻燃剂分别对应即时降低燃烧强度与持续抑制未来蔓延的抑制效果。为评估抑制计划的鲁棒性，通过蒙特卡洛采样量化每日火情状态的随机不确定性，并通过空间相关预测误差扰动量化认知不确定性。基于2020年熊火案例研究表明，该框架可生成连贯的空中抑制调度方案，减少总受火面积，并支持不确定性感知的野火干预策略分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for effective aerial wildfire suppression under uncertain environmental conditions, this study develops a hybrid CNN-cellular automaton model to simulate fire spread and optimize targeted aerial drop interventions. The model integrates terrain, fuel, and wind data to predict spatially varying fire behavior, while a gradient-based optimization module determines drop locations and orientations to minimize fire-affected area. Experiments on the 2020 Bear Fire case study demonstrate that the framework generates coherent suppression schedules and quantifies uncertainty through Monte Carlo sampling and spatially correlated error perturbations, showing improved robustness in reducing total burned area.
为应对环境不确定性下的空中野火扑救需求，该研究将基于地形、燃料和风数据的混合CNN-细胞自动机模型与梯度优化模块结合，用于模拟火势蔓延并设计精准的空中洒水/阻燃剂投放方案。模型区分水与阻燃剂的不同抑制效果，分别模拟即时灭火与持续抑制作用。在2020年熊火案例研究中，该框架生成了有效减少火灾影响面积的扑救计划，并通过蒙特卡洛采样与空间相关误差扰动验证了其对随机与认知不确定性均具备鲁棒性。
From Tokens to Faces: Investigating Discrete Speech Representations for 3D Facial Animation
Authors: Pedro Correa, Olivier Perrotin, Samir Sadok, Paula Costa, Thomas Hueber
First: 2026-06-11T17:41:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:41:18+00:00
Comments: This work has been accepted in Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The choice of speech representation is critical in speech-driven 3D facial animation. Representations differ in what they encode: SSL features emphasize segmental and semantic cues, neural codecs yield latents optimized for acoustic reconstruction, and ASR-style objectives produce label-based spaces. We evaluate four speech representation families for 3D facial synthesis, comparing their facial reconstruction quality across two facial decoders using objective metrics and a perceptual evaluation. We additionally conduct probing analyses that relate tokenized representations to phonetic units and to articulatory deformations. We found that encoding phonetic classes is beneficial for accurate facial animation prediction on both semantic and label-based representations with comparable facial animation quality. From the latter, we introduce an Audio Visual Text-to-Speech (AVTTS) pipeline that leverages, as a shared space, discrete representations to decode speech and 3D facial motion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从音素到面部：探究离散语音表示在3D面部动画中的应用
语音表示的选择对语音驱动的3D面部动画至关重要。不同表示方式编码的内容不同：SSL特征强调分段和语义线索，神经编码器生成优化声学重建的潜在表示，ASR风格目标则生成基于标签的空间。我们评估了四种语音表示家族在3D面部合成中的表现，通过客观指标和感知评估比较其在两种面部解码器上的面部重建质量。此外，我们还进行了探针分析，将离散表示与语音音素单元及发音器官变形关联。研究发现，编码语音音类对语义和标签型表示的面部动画预测均有益，且面部动画质量相当。基于后者，我们提出了一种音频-视觉-文本到语音（AVTTS）管道，利用离散表示作为共享空间，同时解码语音与3D面部运动。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how different discrete speech representations affect 3D facial animation quality, motivated by the need to choose optimal encodings for speech-driven synthesis. The authors compare four representation families—SSL features, neural codecs, ASR-style labels, and phonetic-class tokens—using two facial decoders, evaluating them via objective metrics and perceptual tests. Their probing analyses link tokenized representations to phonetic units and articulatory movements, revealing that phonetic-class encoding improves facial animation accuracy across both semantic and label-based representations without sacrificing quality. They further propose an Audio Visual Text-to-Speech (AVTTS) pipeline that unifies discrete representations for simultaneous speech and facial motion decoding.
该研究旨在探究不同离散语音表示对3D面部动画质量的影响，动机源于语音驱动合成中选择最优编码的必要性。作者对比了四种表示方法——SSL特征、神经编码器、ASR风格标签空间和音素类令牌——在两种面部解码器上的表现，通过客观指标和感知评估进行比较。探针分析将令牌化表示与音素单元和发音变形关联，发现音素类编码能提升面部动画的准确性，且在语义和标签类表示中表现相当。主要成果是提出了一种音频视觉文本到语音（AVTTS）管道，利用离散表示作为共享空间，同时解码语音与3D面部运动。
Valid Inference with Synthetic Data via Task Exchangeability
Authors: Lezhi Tan, Tijana Zrnic
First: 2026-06-11T17:41:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:41:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
There is a proliferation of work arguing for the use of synthetic data in scientific research. For example, social scientists are arguing for the use of LLM-generated "silicon samples" in pilot studies; AI evaluations increasingly rely on "LLM-as-a-judge" outputs; and proteomics research is accelerated by generative models that produce synthetic protein structures. These developments raise an intriguing possibility: synthetic data may help researchers ask more questions, run more studies, and accelerate discovery. But they also raise a fundamental concern: synthetic data can be biased, noisy, and misspecified. In this work, we propose statistical principles for using synthetic data in scientific research with provable validity guarantees. The key insight is a new technical condition that we call task exchangeability. Informally, this is a requirement that the researcher can identify historical tasks, for which real data is available, such that their current task of interest is exchangeable with the historical tasks in an appropriate mathematical sense. We develop methods for valid inference under task exchangeability, together with extensions that provide guarantees even beyond exchangeability. We demonstrate the framework on public opinion surveys with silicon samples and AI evaluation with autoraters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过任务可交换性实现合成数据的有效推断
大量研究主张在科学研究中使用合成数据。例如，社会科学家呼吁在试点研究中使用大语言模型生成的“硅样本”；AI评估日益依赖“大语言模型作为裁判”的输出；蛋白质组学研究则借助生成模型加速合成蛋白质结构的开发。这些进展引发了一个引人入胜的可能性：合成数据可能帮助研究人员提出更多问题、开展更多研究并加速发现。但同时也引发了一个根本性担忧：合成数据可能具有偏倚、噪声和模型误设。在本研究中，我们提出了用于科学研究中使用合成数据的统计原则，并提供可证明的有效性保证。核心洞见是一种新的技术条件，我们称之为“任务可交换性”。通俗而言，这是要求研究者能够识别历史任务（这些任务有真实数据可用），使得当前研究任务在适当数学意义上与这些历史任务可交换。我们开发了在任务可交换性下进行有效推断的方法，并扩展了即使超出可交换性仍能提供保证的方法。我们在使用硅样本的公众意见调查和使用自动评分者的人工智能评估中展示了该框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of synthetic data in scientific domains such as social science, AI evaluation, and proteomics, this work addresses the risk of biased or misspecified synthetic data undermining inference validity. The authors introduce 'task exchangeability'—a statistical condition requiring that a current research task be mathematically exchangeable with historical tasks for which real data exists. Under this condition, they develop methods for valid inference and extend them to handle cases beyond strict exchangeability. Experiments on public opinion surveys using synthetic silicon samples and AI evaluations with autoraters demonstrate that their framework enables statistically sound inference even when synthetic data is imperfect.
鉴于合成数据在社会科学、AI评估和蛋白质组学等领域的广泛应用，本研究旨在解决合成数据可能存在的偏差与误设问题，提出一套具有可证明有效性的统计推断框架。核心方法引入“任务可交换性”条件，要求当前研究任务在数学上可与历史真实数据任务交换，从而支持有效推断，并扩展至可交换性之外的情形。实验在使用硅样本的民意调查和使用自动评分器的AI评估中验证，表明该框架即使在合成数据不完美时，仍能实现统计上可靠的推断。
Generative Modeling of Bach-Style Symbolic Music: A Comparative Study of Autoregressive, Latent-Variable, and Adversarial Approaches
Authors: Kyuil Lee, Dezhi Yu, Yongkang Huang
First: 2026-06-11T17:39:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:39:55+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study generative modeling of Bach-style symbolic piano music using a shared MIDI corpus and three model families: autoregressive LSTMs with attention, latent-variable models including recurrent VAEs and vector-quantized VAEs, and generative adversarial networks. We compare their ability to model polyphonic note sequences, learn useful latent representations, and generate stylistically coherent compositions. Our experiments show that the autoregressive LSTM with attention produces the most musically coherent samples, while vector quantization helps mitigate posterior collapse and yields more structured outputs than conventional recurrent VAEs. The adversarial approach captures local pitch patterns but remains difficult to train and generalizes less reliably to Bach's style. These results highlight the relative strengths and failure modes of autoregressive, latent-variable, and adversarial approaches for symbolic music generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：巴赫风格符号音乐的生成建模：自回归、潜在变量与对抗方法的比较研究
我们使用共享的MIDI语料库，研究了三种模型家族（带注意力机制的自回归LSTM、包括循环VAE和向量量化VAE的潜在变量模型、生成对抗网络）在生成巴赫风格钢琴音乐方面的表现。我们比较了它们在建模多声部音符序列、学习有用潜在表示及生成风格连贯作品方面的能力。实验表明，带注意力机制的自回归LSTM生成的音乐样本最具音乐连贯性；向量量化有助于缓解后验坍塌，比传统循环VAE生成更结构化的输出；对抗方法虽能捕捉局部音高模式，但训练困难且对巴赫风格的泛化能力较差。这些结果突显了自回归、潜在变量与对抗方法在符号音乐生成中的相对优势与失效模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate Bach-style symbolic piano music with stylistic fidelity, this study compares three generative model families—autoregressive LSTMs with attention, latent-variable models (including recurrent and vector-quantized VAEs), and adversarial networks—using a shared MIDI corpus. The autoregressive LSTM with attention produced the most musically coherent samples, while vector quantization in VAEs reduced posterior collapse and improved structural output quality over standard recurrent VAEs. Adversarial models captured local pitch patterns but suffered from training instability and poor generalization to Bach’s stylistic conventions.
为生成风格忠实的巴赫式钢琴符号音乐，本研究在统一的MIDI数据集上对比了三种生成模型家族——带注意力机制的自回归LSTM、潜在变量模型（含循环VAE与向量量化VAE）及生成对抗网络。自回归LSTM生成的样本音乐性最连贯，向量量化VAE缓解了后验坍塌问题，输出结构更清晰；而对抗网络虽能捕捉局部音高模式，但训练不稳定且难以可靠泛化至巴赫风格，凸显了不同模型架构的优势与局限。
Revisiting Vehicle Color Recognition in Long-Tailed Surveillance Scenarios
Authors: Vinícius Orrú, Bruno H. Foggiatto, Gabriel E. Lima, David Menotti, Rayson Laroca
First: 2026-06-11T17:39:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T17:39:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at the 2026 International Conference on Pattern Recognition (ICPR) - V3SC Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vehicle color recognition is an important cue for vehicle identification in surveillance systems, especially when license plates are illegible due to low resolution, occlusion, motion blur, or poor illumination. However, real-world vehicle color distributions are highly imbalanced, making overall accuracy insufficient to assess performance on rare but operationally relevant colors. This paper presents a comprehensive study of vehicle color recognition under severe class imbalance using UFPR-VeSV, a challenging real-world surveillance dataset. We investigate synthetic minority-class augmentation through two off-the-shelf generative strategies: text-conditioned image generation with RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL and image-conditioned color editing with Gemini 2.0 Flash. The curated synthetic data are combined with modern visual representations, loss reweighting, learning-rate scheduling, color-safe augmentation, foreground-aware preprocessing, and ensemble fusion. The bestperforming approach achieves 94.6% micro accuracy and 79.7% macro accuracy, improving macro accuracy by 8.2 percentage points over recent literature. A manual error analysis further shows that many remaining failures are visually ambiguous even for human annotators, highlighting the practical limits of color-based vehicle identification in unconstrained surveillance imagery. The generated images and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic

标题：长尾监控场景下车辆颜色识别的再审视
车辆颜色识别是监控系统中车辆识别的重要线索，尤其在车牌因低分辨率、遮挡、运动模糊或照明不足而无法辨识时。然而，现实世界中车辆颜色分布高度不平衡，导致整体准确率不足以评估在罕见但实际重要的颜色上的表现。本文在UFPR-VeSV这一具有挑战性的现实监控数据集上，全面研究了在严重类别不平衡下的车辆颜色识别。我们通过两种现成的生成策略——使用RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL的文本条件图像生成和使用Gemini 2.0 Flash的图像条件颜色编辑——探索合成少数类数据增强。精选的合成数据结合现代视觉表示、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合。最佳方法实现了94.6%的微准确率和79.7%的宏准确率，较近期文献提升8.2个百分点。人工错误分析进一步表明，许多剩余失败案例即使对人工标注员而言也存在视觉歧义，凸显了在无约束监控图像中基于颜色的车辆识别的实际局限性。生成图像和源代码已公开于https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic。
