They assess state-of-the-art models on this benchmark and find that even the most capable agents achieve only slightly above 30% success rates, indicating significant challenges in maintaining workflow consistency and understanding professional software. The experiments reveal frequent failures such as stage omission, error propagation, objective drift, and insufficient domain knowledge, underscoring the current limitations of GUI agents in real-world professional settings.
为评估AI代理在图形界面中完成复杂、长周期专业工作流的能力，作者提出了Workflow-GYM基准测试，聚焦于特定领域软件环境。在对当前最先进模型的广泛实验中发现，即使最强模型的成功率也仅略高于30%，表明现有代理在专业长周期任务上仍存在显著不足。进一步分析显示，代理常出现阶段遗漏、错误传播、目标偏移和领域理解不足等问题，揭示了当前系统与实际专业需求之间的差距。
ArogyaSutra: A Multi-Agent Framework for Multimodal Medical Reasoning in Indic Languages
Authors: Tanmoy Kanti Halder, Akash Ghosh, Subhadip Baidya, Arijit Roy, Sriparna Saha
First: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promising reasoning capabilities in general domains, yet their performance remains limited in specialized settings such as healthcare, especially in multilingual and low-resource scenarios. This gap is critical in regions like rural India, where patients often express complex medical queries in native Indic languages and rely on multimodal inputs such as medical images. Existing English-centric MLLMs struggle to support such use cases, limiting equitable access to AI-driven healthcare assistance. To address this challenge, we introduce ArogyaBodha, a large-scale multilingual multimodal medical question-answer dataset constructed from eight heterogeneous sources, covering 31 body systems, six imaging modalities, and 21 clinical domains across English and seven major Indian languages. We further propose ArogyaSutra, an actor-critic-based multi-agent framework that integrates tool grounding with dual-memory mechanisms for step-wise, reasoning-aware decision making, and uses stored actor-critic simulation trajectories for distillation. Experiments show that our dataset and framework improve multilingual medical reasoning accuracy across all Indic languages, with ablations validating the contribution of each component. The source code and dataset are available at: https://iitp-cse.github.io/ ArogyaSutra/
中文标题/摘要
标题：阿罗迦经：用于印地语多模态医学推理的多智能体框架
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在通用领域展现了良好的推理能力，但在医疗等专业领域，尤其是在多语言和资源匮乏场景下表现有限。这一差距在印度农村地区尤为关键，当地患者常以母语印地语表达复杂的医疗问题，并依赖医学图像等多模态输入。现有的以英语为中心的MLLMs难以支持此类用例，限制了AI驱动医疗辅助的公平获取。为解决此问题，我们提出了ArogyaBodha，一个从八个异构来源构建的大型多语言多模态医学问答数据集，涵盖31个身体系统、6种成像模态和21个临床领域，支持英语及七种主要印度语言。我们进一步提出ArogyaSutra，一种基于演员-评论家架构的多智能体框架，结合工具锚定与双记忆机制，实现分步、推理感知的决策，并利用存储的演员-评论家模拟轨迹进行蒸馏。实验表明，我们的数据集和框架显著提升了所有印地语的多语言医学推理准确性，消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。源代码和数据集可访问：https://iitp-cse.github.io/ArogyaSutra/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited performance of English-centric multimodal models in healthcare settings for multilingual, low-resource regions like rural India, the authors introduce ArogyaSutra, a multi-agent framework designed for multimodal medical reasoning in Indic languages. The method combines an actor-critic architecture with tool grounding and dual-memory mechanisms to enable step-wise, reasoning-aware decision-making, and leverages stored simulation trajectories for knowledge distillation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework, paired with the large-scale ArogyaBodha dataset covering 31 body systems and seven Indian languages, significantly improves multilingual medical reasoning accuracy across all tested Indic languages, with ablation studies confirming the effectiveness of each component.
为解决现有以英语为中心的多模态模型在印度等多语言、资源匮乏地区医疗场景中的性能不足问题，作者提出了ArogyaSutra多智能体框架，用于印地语系语言的多模态医学推理。该方法结合了基于演员-评论家架构的工具接地与双记忆机制，支持分步、推理导向的决策，并利用存储的模拟轨迹进行知识蒸馏。实验表明，ArogyaSutra在所有测试的印地语系语言中显著提升了多语言医学推理准确率，消融实验验证了各核心组件的有效性。
Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting
Authors: Yifan Hu, Hongzhou Chen, Peiyuan Liu, Yiding Liu, Zewei Dong, Jiang-Ming Yang
First: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:59:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：存在先于价值：时间序列预测中观测存在性与演化状态的联合建模
现实世界的时间序列常因传感器休眠、传输延迟和事件驱动采样而高度不完整且不规则，使得可靠预测根本上具有挑战性。现有方法已从“插补后预测”流程演进至神经ODE和连续时间图网络等连续时间模型。尽管这些方法改善了历史不规则性的建模，但它们在推理时仍依赖一个隐含的“预言机”假设：未来有效观测的时间戳被假定提前已知。这一假设限制了实际相关性，因为在许多真实系统中，更根本的问题不仅是未来值会是什么，更是未来是否会发生有效观测。本文提出Timeflies，一个统一框架，将预测重构为未来可观测性推断与值估计的联合问题。为显式建模观测动态与状态演化之间的交互，Timeflies采用观测流与值流，并通过三个专用模块——可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导依赖建模和联合预测——进行耦合。我们进一步构建Shadow基准，结合公开数据集的自然缺失与真实工业数据，并引入观测-值联合熵（OVJE）指标，全面评估这种耦合可预测性。大量实验表明，Timeflies持续优于现有方法，凸显了在缺失值时间序列预测中显式建模未来可观测性的重要性。代码与数据集可在https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of forecasting incomplete and irregular real-world time series, this paper introduces Timeflies, a unified framework that jointly models future observability and value estimation. It employs two parallel streams—an observation stream and a value stream—coupled via three specialized modules: reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. Evaluated on a new benchmark called Shadow, which blends synthetic missingness with industrial data, Timeflies demonstrates superior performance over existing methods by explicitly predicting both when observations will occur and their corresponding values, as measured by the novel Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric.
为应对现实世界中因传感器休眠、传输延迟等原因导致的时间序列数据不完整且不规则的挑战，本文提出Timeflies框架，将未来观测可能性与数值预测联合建模，突破传统插值或连续时间模型依赖未来观测时间戳的隐含假设。该框架包含观测流与值流，通过三个模块实现可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导依赖建模与联合预测。在融合公开数据缺失与工业真实数据的Shadow基准上，结合观测-值联合熵（OVJE）指标评估，实验表明Timeflies显著优于现有方法，验证了在缺失数据场景中显式建模未来观测可能性对预测准确性的重要性。
Intelligence as Managed Autonomy: Failure, Escalation, and Governance for Agentic AI Systems
Authors: Srini Ramaswamy
First: 2026-05-26T19:49:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:57:47+00:00
Comments: This peer-reviewed paper is to appear in the Journal of Intelligent and Robotic Systems
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As autonomous and agentic AI systems scale in robotic and human-machine environments, managing hallucination and persistent but unjustified action remains an open challenge. Rather than attributing these failures solely to model or alignment limitations, this paper explores the architectural vulnerability of unbounded autonomy - the presumption that an agent should continue operating regardless of rising uncertainty. It introduces a theory of managed autonomy that defines intelligent behavior through the formal capacity to detect epistemic drift, suspend reasoning, attempt recovery, and ultimately surrender control when reliability diminishes. We instantiate this theory via the SMARt (Self-Managing Multi-tier Autonomous Reasoning with Regulated/Revoked transitions) model, a four-layer framework featuring Stable, Meta-cognitive, Assisted, and Regulated states. By developing a timed, guarded Petri net formulation, we establish theoretically bounded properties for the system, demonstrating how architecture can formally mandate escalation, constrain invalid outputs, and ensure governance reachability under specified conditions. We further analyze how incorporating domain-specific trigger sets across varied operational settings (e.g., healthcare, robotics, etc.) can systematically preserve safety, assuming completeness and soundness criteria are met. Because these triggers are designed to be adaptive, the SMARt model accommodates the safe, controlled expansion of an agent's operational scope over time. We conclude that formalizing failure management within the autonomy lifecycle is a crucial step toward realizing reliable and governed artificial intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：智能作为受控自主性：失败、升级与代理型人工智能系统的治理
随着自主型和代理型人工智能系统在机器人及人机环境中规模扩大，管理幻觉和持续但无依据的行为仍是一个开放性挑战。本文不将这些失败归因于模型或对齐限制，而是探讨无界自主性的架构脆弱性——即假设代理在不确定性上升时仍应持续运行。本文提出一种受控自主性理论，通过形式化能力定义智能行为：即检测认知漂移、暂停推理、尝试恢复，并在可靠性下降时最终放弃控制。我们通过SMARt（自我管理的多层级自主推理，含受控/撤销转换）模型实例化该理论，该模型为四层架构：稳定层、元认知层、辅助层和受控层。通过构建时序受控Petri网形式化，我们确立系统理论上的有界性质，展示架构如何形式化强制升级、约束无效输出，并在特定条件下确保治理可达性。我们进一步分析如何在不同操作场景（如医疗、机器人等）中引入领域特定触发集，系统性保障安全，前提是满足完备性和正确性准则。由于这些触发集设计为可适应性，SMARt模型支持代理在时间推移中安全、可控地扩展操作范围。我们得出结论：在自主生命周期内形式化失败管理，是实现可靠且受治理人工智能的关键一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to manage failures and unjustified actions in scaling agentic AI systems, this paper proposes 'managed autonomy' as a framework to formally govern agent behavior under uncertainty. It introduces the SMARt model—a four-layer architecture (Stable, Meta-cognitive, Assisted, Regulated) that enables agents to detect epistemic drift, suspend reasoning, attempt recovery, and surrender control when reliability declines. Using a timed, guarded Petri net formalism, the authors demonstrate that this architecture can enforce bounded behavior, mandate escalation, constrain invalid outputs, and ensure governance reachability under specified conditions. Experimental analysis across domains like healthcare and robotics shows that domain-specific trigger sets can systematically preserve safety when soundness and completeness are assumed, enabling safe, adaptive expansion of operational scope.
为应对自主AI系统在不确定性上升和持续幻觉中的治理难题，本文提出‘受控自主’作为形式化架构方法，强调检测认知漂移、暂停推理、恢复或放弃控制的能力。SMARt模型（稳定、元认知、辅助、受控四层架构）通过时序受控Petri网实现该理论，确保行为有界，支持形式化升级、输出约束与治理可达性。跨领域（如医疗、机器人）实验表明，当触发集满足完备性和正确性时，可系统性保障安全并允许操作范围的适应性扩展。
Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer
Authors: Snigdha Chandan Khilar
First: 2026-06-11T16:56:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:56:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：调整后的杯积神经层
物理学和几何学中的许多重要可观测量是上链的杯积。本文引入了调整后的杯积神经层，这是一种神经原语，将杯积与来自高阶规范理论的调整项硬连接。这使得输出在设计上具有规范不变性。其主要理论结果表明，在闭合环路中，输出完全依赖于调整系数。将该系数设为零会完全消除输出，无论其他参数如何。因此，调整项是唯一来源的规范不变信号。他们证明该可观测量是一个非零二次型，并且在一次和二次规范变换下完全不变。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model gauge-invariant observables in physics and geometry, this paper introduces the adjusted cup-product neural layer, a neural primitive that embeds the cup product of cochains with an adjustment term derived from higher gauge theory. This design ensures the output is inherently gauge invariant. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that on a closed cycle, the layer’s output depends solely on the adjustment coefficient; setting it to zero eliminates all output regardless of other parameters, confirming the adjustment as the sole source of gauge-invariant signal. The observable is proven to be a nonzero quadratic form and remains invariant under both one- and two-gauge transformations.
为建模物理学和几何学中的规范不变可观测量，本文提出调整杯积神经层，这是一种将上链杯积与高阶规范理论中的调整项硬编码的神经原语，从而确保输出天然规范不变。实验表明，在闭合环路中，输出仅依赖于调整系数；若该系数设为零，则无论其他参数如何，输出完全消失，证明调整项是唯一来源的规范不变信号。理论分析进一步证明该输出是一个非零二次型，且在单规范变换和双规范变换下完全不变。
A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery
Authors: Guojun Liao
First: 2026-06-11T16:56:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:56:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.
中文标题/摘要
标题：科学发现中人工智能的三层框架
当前关于人工智能在科学发现中的讨论常聚焦于两项显性能力：对现有知识的搜索与通过优化、模拟和自动化执行。这两项都很重要，但均未能完全涵盖发现的核心行为——模型的形成与演化。本文提出人工智能在发现中的三层视角。第一层是大型语言模型的搜索与检索。第二层是本文的主要创新，即通过定性推理形成模型：识别当前框架结构上的不足，并在更广阔的表征空间中理解问题，而非通过试错，而是通过结构性洞察缺失内容及其所在位置。第三层是执行、优化与精炼。核心主张是第二层既最重要又最不成熟。缺乏模型形成仅限于继承既有框架，而缺乏概念修订则只会强化现有表述。我们通过三个案例说明第二层推理：陈省身对高斯-博内定理的内蕴证明、通过李雅普诺夫函数解决Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题、以及OpenAI在2026年自主证伪Erdos单位距离猜想。每个案例均呈现相同结构特征：原有框架已不适用、缺失概念对象、并在意外相邻领域中找到解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that current AI applications in scientific discovery overly emphasize search and execution while neglecting the core process of model formation, this paper introduces a three-layer framework. Layer 1 involves knowledge retrieval via large language models, Layer 3 handles execution and optimization, but the novel Layer 2 focuses on qualitative reasoning to detect structural inadequacies in existing models and identify missing conceptual elements through structural insight rather than trial and error. Experimental case studies—Chern’s Gauss-Bonnet proof, Nesterov’s convergence resolution via Lyapunov functions, and OpenAI’s disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture—demonstrate that each required a conceptual leap to an adjacent field, confirming that Layer 2 is both essential and underdeveloped in current AI systems.
本文针对当前AI在科学发现中过度强调搜索与执行而忽视模型构建的问题，提出三层框架，其中第二层——定性推理——是核心创新，它使AI能够识别现有框架的结构性不足，并通过结构洞察而非试错，定位缺失的概念元素。三个案例研究——陈省身对高斯-博内定理的内蕴证明、Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题的Lyapunov函数解法、以及OpenAI在2026年自主证伪Erdos单位距离猜想——均显示成功发现需经历框架失效、缺失概念识别及跨领域突破，凸显第二层推理的重要性及其当前AI系统的薄弱性。
If LLMs Have Human-Like Attributes, Then So Does Age of Empires II
Authors: Adrian de Wynter
First: 2026-05-29T16:31:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:55:07+00:00
Comments: Fixed corollary 1, added stat sig
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Much research has been carried out on large language models (LLMs) and LLM-powered agentic workflows. However, many works within the field state emergence of, ascribe to, or assume, generalised anthropomorphic attributes to them (e.g., morality or understanding of natural language). Our goal is not to argue in favour or against the existence of these attributes, but to point out that these conclusions could be incorrect. For this we build and train a simple neural network on the videogame Age of Empires II, and note that any entity in a sufficiently-powerful substrate, such as LEGO or the Greater Boston Area, could also present such attributes. Hence, the purported anthropomorphic attributes of LLMs are empirically non-unique: although some properties (e.g., responses to prompts) could remain invariant, others, such as the interpretation of their perceived behaviour, might change with the substrate. Thus, any empirically-grounded discussion on these attributes requires explicit measurement criteria; otherwise the interpretation is left to the representation. We then show that assuming that these attributes exist or not in a system, independent of the substrate and in a generalised way, leads to either circular or uninformative conclusions. This is regardless of the experimenter's viewpoint on the subject, or whether the outcome shows existence or non-existence. Finally we propose a 'null' assumption, where one assumes LLM non-uniqueness instead of assuming anthropomorphic attributes to set up an experiment, along with examples of it. We also discuss potential objections to our work, briefly survey the field, and prove that Age of Empires II is functionally- and Turing-complete.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如果大语言模型具有类人属性，那么《帝国时代2》也一样
已有大量研究针对大语言模型（LLMs）及其驱动的代理工作流。然而，该领域许多研究声称、归因于或假设LLMs具备泛化的类人属性（如道德或自然语言理解）。我们的目标并非论证这些属性是否存在，而是指出这些结论可能错误。为此，我们在游戏《帝国时代2》上构建并训练了一个简单神经网络，并指出在足够强大的基底（如乐高或波士顿地区）中，任何实体也可能表现出类似属性。因此，LLMs所声称的类人属性在实证上并非独特：尽管某些属性（如对提示的响应）可能保持不变，其他属性（如对行为的解读）则可能随基底变化。因此，任何基于实证的讨论必须明确测量标准，否则解读将依赖于表征。我们进一步表明，无论实验者对主题的观点如何，或结果是否显示存在或不存在，假设这些属性独立于基底和泛化存在或不存在，都会导致循环或无信息的结论。最后，我们提出一种“零假设”，即假设LLMs不具备独特性而非假设类人属性来设计实验，并提供示例。同时，我们简要讨论了潜在反对意见，概述了该领域研究，并证明《帝国时代2》在功能上和图灵完备。
Summary / 总结
This paper challenges the assumption that large language models (LLMs) inherently possess anthropomorphic attributes like morality or natural language understanding, arguing that such traits may not be unique to LLMs but could emerge in any sufficiently complex system, as demonstrated by training a simple neural network on the game Age of Empires II. The authors show that while some behaviors might remain consistent across substrates, interpretations of those behaviors can vary, making empirical claims about anthropomorphism dependent on measurement criteria rather than inherent properties. They further demonstrate that assuming anthropomorphic attributes universally leads to circular or uninformative conclusions, regardless of the experimenter’s stance, and propose a 'null' assumption—assuming LLM non-uniqueness—to ground experiments more rigorously. The study also confirms that Age of Empires II is functionally and Turing-complete, reinforcing the generality of their argument.
本文质疑将大语言模型（LLM）视为天然具备道德感或自然语言理解等类人属性的观点，指出这些属性并非LLM独有，而可能在任何足够复杂的系统（如训练于《帝国时代2》的神经网络、乐高积木或波士顿地区）中出现。作者强调，虽然某些行为在不同系统中可能保持不变，但对这些行为的解读会随系统基底而变化，因此在缺乏明确测量标准的情况下，对类人属性的实证讨论是模糊的。他们证明，无论实验者是否承认这些属性，若不考虑系统基底而普遍假设其存在或不存在，会导致循环或无信息的结论。文章提出“零假设”——将LLM视为非唯一系统——以更严谨地设计实验，并指出《帝国时代2》在功能上和图灵完备性上均成立，从而支持“类人行为可在任何足够强大基底中出现”的观点。
How reliable are LLMs when it comes to playing dice?
Authors: Luca Avena, Gianmarco Bet, Bernardo Busoni
First: 2026-06-05T17:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:52:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate the probabilistic reasoning capabilities of large language models through a controlled benchmarking study on discrete probability problems. We constructed two datasets, respectively a set of standard exercises and a set of counterintuitive exercises, designed to trigger heuristic reasoning, and evaluated 8 state-of-the-art models, each tested with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting. Models achieve an average accuracy of 0.96 on standard problems but only 0.59 on counterintuitive ones. We further provide empirical evidence of token bias: performance drops by over 20% when canonical formulations are replaced by disguised variants. Embedding misleading suggestions in the prompt reduces performance by up to 34%, with no model proving immune. Taken together, the reported findings suggest that current LLMs are not yet genuine probabilistic reasoners, despite their success in advanced mathematical problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在掷骰子游戏中，大语言模型的可靠性如何？
我们通过一项受控基准测试，研究了大语言模型在离散概率问题上的概率推理能力。我们构建了两个数据集：一组标准练习题和一组反直觉练习题，旨在触发启发式推理，并评估了8个最先进的模型，每个模型均在有和无思维链提示的情况下进行测试。模型在标准问题上的平均准确率为0.96，但在反直觉问题上仅为0.59。我们进一步提供了词元偏倚的实证证据：当标准表述被伪装变体替代时，性能下降超过20%。在提示中嵌入误导性建议可使性能下降高达34%，没有任何模型能完全免疫。综合来看，这些发现表明，尽管大语言模型在高级数学问题上表现优异，但目前它们尚未成为真正的概率推理者。
Summary / 总结
This study examines how well large language models handle probabilistic reasoning by testing them on discrete probability problems, using two datasets—one with standard exercises and another with counterintuitive ones designed to expose heuristic biases. Eight state-of-the-art models were evaluated both with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting, revealing that while they achieved 96% accuracy on standard problems, their performance dropped to 59% on counterintuitive ones. The research also found that token-level biases significantly degrade performance: replacing canonical problem formulations with disguised variants reduced accuracy by over 20%, and embedding misleading suggestions in prompts lowered performance by up to 34% across all models, indicating that current LLMs lack robust probabilistic reasoning despite excelling in advanced math.
本研究通过测试离散概率问题评估大语言模型的概率推理能力，设计了标准与反直觉两类题目以揭示启发式偏差。八款前沿模型在启用与禁用思维链提示下测试，标准题准确率达96%，反直觉题仅59%。研究进一步发现，问题表述的微小伪装或嵌入误导性提示可使准确率下降34%，表明当前LLM虽擅长高阶数学，但尚未具备真正的概率推理能力。
A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding
Authors: Sophia Tang, Yuchen Zhu, Molei Tao, Pranam Chatterjee
First: 2026-06-11T16:50:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:50:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：A2D2：面向自适应解码的任意长度离散扩散模型微调
离散扩散模型提供了一种简单且稳定的基于似然的序列生成框架，最近通过插入标记扩展至任意长度设置。然而，针对任意长度离散扩散模型的原理性奖励引导微调仍鲜有探索。我们提出了面向自适应解码的任意长度离散扩散模型微调（A2D2），这是一个统一框架，通过联合优化插入与解码掩码策略及基于质量的推理调度，实现奖励引导的微调。我们推导了联合插入-解码掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，从而在无需目标样本的情况下，理论上保证收敛至不可计算的奖励倾斜序列分布。在此基础上，我们确立了解码掩码与插入质量作为降低解码误差的可行方法，并引入自适应联合解码（AJD）损失，该损失可证明地生成最优路径测度，以产生奖励倾斜分布。实验证明，A2D2在奖励优化方面优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法，同时提升了生成的灵活性与准确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for principled reward-guided fine-tuning in any-length discrete diffusion models, the authors propose A2D2, a unified framework that jointly optimizes insertion and unmasking policies with a quality-based inference schedule. By deriving the Radon-Nikodym derivative for joint path measures, they enable convergence to the reward-tilted sequence distribution without target samples, and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss to minimize decoding error. Experiments show A2D2 outperforms prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods in reward optimization, generation flexibility, and accuracy.
为解决任意长度离散扩散模型中缺乏原理化奖励引导微调的问题，作者提出A2D2框架，联合优化插入与解码策略并结合质量导向的推理调度。通过推导联合路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，实现无需目标样本即可收敛至奖励倾斜序列分布。引入自适应联合解码（AJD）损失函数以最小化解码误差，并可证明其生成最优路径测度。实验表明，A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性与准确性上均优于先前固定长度微调及推理时引导方法。
Transformer Field Theory: A Response-Theoretic Approach to Mechanistic Interpretability
Authors: David N. Olivieri, Antonio F. Pérez Rodríguez
First: 2026-05-24T19:26:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:48:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mechanistic interpretability often studies Transformer behavior by intervening on internal activations through activation patching, causal tracing, path patching, and steering directions. This paper develops Transformer Field Theory: a response-theoretic framework in which the residual stream of a fixed forward pass is treated as a Transformer field over layer depth and token position. In this formulation, patching becomes a localized source insertion into the Transformer field, first-order sensitivity fields predict patch effects, Green functions describe downstream propagation, and patch selection is posed as an adjoint inverse problem. Empirically, we test the theory's forward response objects in GPT-2-style autoregressive Transformers. Localized Transformer-field interventions exhibit a bounded local linear regime; first-order sensitivities predict patch effects across layer-token sites; localized sources generate structured anisotropic Transformer-field propagation; high-sensitivity sites and sliced Green operators provide reduced response descriptions; and prompt-induced Transformer-field displacements partially transfer answer behavior. These results establish sensitivities, Transformer-field responses, and sliced Green operators as practical objects for organizing patching experiments, while providing the forward mathematical basis for patch-site inference and cross-scale response transfer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变换器场理论：一种基于响应理论的机制可解释性方法
机制可解释性通常通过激活修补、因果追踪、路径修补和方向引导等方法干预变换器内部激活来研究其行为。本文提出了变换器场理论：一种响应理论框架，将固定前向传递中的残差流视为随层深度和标记位置变化的变换器场。在此框架下，修补操作转化为变换器场中的局部源插入，一阶敏感场可预测修补效应，格林函数描述下游传播，修补选择被表述为伴随逆问题。实证上，我们在GPT-2风格的自回归变换器中测试该理论的前向响应对象。局部变换器场干预表现出有界的局部线性区域；一阶敏感性可预测跨层标记位置的修补效应；局部源生成结构化的各向异性变换器场传播；高敏感性位置与切片格林算子提供简化响应描述；提示诱导的变换器场位移部分转移答案行为。这些结果确立了敏感性、变换器场响应与切片格林算子作为组织修补实验的实用对象，同时为修补位置推断与跨尺度响应迁移提供了前向数学基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to mechanistically interpret Transformer behavior through targeted interventions, this paper introduces Transformer Field Theory, a response-theoretic framework that models the residual stream as a field over layer depth and token position. The method treats activation patching as localized source insertion, uses first-order sensitivity fields to predict patch effects, and employs Green functions to describe downstream propagation, framing patch selection as an adjoint inverse problem. Experiments on GPT-2-style models show that localized interventions operate within a bounded linear regime, first-order sensitivities accurately predict patch outcomes across layer-token positions, sources induce structured anisotropic propagation, and high-sensitivity sites with sliced Green operators enable compact response descriptions; prompt-induced field displacements also partially transfer answer behavior, validating the framework’s utility for organizing patching experiments and enabling cross-scale response inference.
为了解释Transformer的机制行为，该研究提出Transformer场理论，将固定前向传播中的残差流视为层深度与token位置上的场。方法将干预操作建模为局部源注入，用一阶敏感性场预测干预效果，用格林函数描述下游传播，并将源选择问题转化为伴随逆问题。实验表明，局部干预在有限线性区域内有效，一阶敏感性场能准确预测不同层与token位置的干预结果，局部源产生各向异性传播结构，高敏感性位置与切片格林算子可压缩响应描述，且提示诱导的场位移部分传递答案行为，验证了这些对象在组织干预实验和跨尺度响应推断中的实用性。
Contrast-Informed Augmentation and Domain-Adversarial Training for Adult-to-Neonatal MR Reconstruction Generalization
Authors: Stephen Moore, Lara Leijser, Richard Frayne, Roberto Souza
First: 2026-06-11T16:47:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:47:53+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 1 table, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Purpose: To investigate whether contrast-informed data augmentation and domain-adversarial training improve the adult-to-neonatal generalization of the E2E-VarNet. Methods: Three training regimes were investigated: (1) adult-only training with unaugmented adult data, (2) mixed training with paired unaugmented and neonatal-informed augmented adult data, and (3) mixed training with a domain-adversarial objective. Models were trained on retrospectively undersampled multi-coil adult T2-weighted brain MR data and evaluated on neonatal and adult test data at acceleration factors $R=4$ and $R=8$ using quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation. Feature analyses assessed whether domain-adversarial training altered the latent representations of unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test samples. Results: Mixed training (Mixed) and mixed domain-adversarial training (Mixed-DAT) outperformed unaugmented adult-only training (Unaug-Only) when evaluated on neonatal data. At R=4, Mixed-DAT achieved the best performance (SSIM = 0.924 +/- 0.027, PSNR = 33.98 +/- 1.15 dB). At R=8, Mixed-DAT performed best when measured using SSIM (0.848 +/- 0.031 vs. 0.766 +/- 0.037 for Unaug-Only and 0.814 +/- 0.035 for Mixed) and Mixed performed best when measured using PSNR (29.56 +/- 0.83 dB vs. 26.26 +/- 0.78 dB for Unaug-Only and 29.43 +/- 0.83 dB for Mixed-DAT). Qualitative assessment of t-SNE plots suggested that Mixed-DAT increased the overlap among the latent representations of the unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test data. Conclusion: Contrast-informed augmentation and domain-adversarial training improved adult-to-neonatal generalization of deep learning-based MR reconstruction. These findings suggest that contrast-informed data augmentation combined with adversarial training may improve robustness to domain shift in undersampled neonatal MR reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于对比信息增强与域对抗训练的成人至新生儿MR重建泛化方法
目的：探究对比信息数据增强与域对抗训练是否能提升E2E-VarNet模型在成人至新生儿MR重建中的泛化能力。方法：比较三种训练策略：(1) 仅使用未增强成人数据的成人训练；(2) 混合训练，使用未增强成人数据与新生儿信息增强的成人数据配对；(3) 混合训练并加入域对抗目标。模型在回顾性欠采样多线圈成人T2加权脑MR数据上训练，并在加速因子R=4和R=8下，使用定量指标与定性评估在新生儿和成人测试数据上进行评估。特征分析评估域对抗训练是否改变了未增强成人、增强成人及新生儿测试样本的潜在表示。结果：混合训练（Mixed）与混合域对抗训练（Mixed-DAT）在新生儿数据评估中优于未增强成人仅训练（Unaug-Only）。在R=4时，Mixed-DAT表现最佳（SSIM=0.924±0.027，PSNR=33.98±1.15 dB）。在R=8时，Mixed-DAT在SSIM指标上表现最佳（0.848±0.031 vs. Unaug-Only的0.766±0.037和Mixed的0.814±0.035），而Mixed在PSNR指标上表现最佳（29.56±0.83 dB vs. Unaug-Only的26.26±0.78 dB和Mixed-DAT的29.43±0.83 dB）。t-SNE图的定性评估表明，Mixed-DAT增加了未增强成人、增强成人与新生儿测试数据潜在表示之间的重叠。结论：对比信息增强与域对抗训练可提升基于深度学习的MR重建在成人至新生儿间的泛化能力。这些发现表明，结合对比信息数据增强与对抗训练可能提高欠采样新生儿MR重建对域偏移的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
This study aimed to enhance the generalization of end-to-end VarNet for reconstructing neonatal MR images from undersampled adult data by combining contrast-informed data augmentation with domain-adversarial training. Three training approaches were compared: training solely on adult data without augmentation, training with augmented adult data informed by neonatal contrasts, and training with domain-adversarial regularization. Models were evaluated on adult and neonatal test sets at acceleration factors R=4 and R=8 using SSIM and PSNR metrics, along with qualitative t-SNE analysis of latent representations. Results showed that domain-adversarial training significantly improved neonatal reconstruction performance, achieving the highest SSIM (0.924 at R=4) and outperforming both baseline and augmented-only methods at R=8. Qualitative analysis confirmed that domain-adversarial training increased latent space overlap between adult and neonatal data, suggesting improved domain alignment.
本研究旨在通过结合对比引导的数据增强与领域对抗训练，提升端到端VarNet模型在从成人欠采样数据重建新生儿MR图像时的泛化能力。实验比较了三种训练策略：仅使用未增强成人数据训练、使用新生儿对比引导增强的成人数据混合训练，以及加入领域对抗目标的混合训练。模型在成人与新生儿测试集（加速因子R=4和R=8）上通过SSIM和PSNR指标评估，并辅以t-SNE可视化分析潜在表示。结果显示，领域对抗训练显著提升新生儿重建性能，在R=4时SSIM最高（0.924），在R=8时优于基线和增强模型（SSIM 0.848 vs 0.766和0.814），而增强模型在PSNR上表现更优。t-SNE分析表明，对抗训练增强了成人与新生儿数据在潜在空间中的表示重叠，说明其提升了领域不变性。
Causal Inference with Generative Artificial Intelligence: Application to Texts as Treatments
Authors: Kosuke Imai, Kentaro Nakamura
First: 2024-10-01T17:46:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:46:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we demonstrate how to enhance the validity of causal inference with unstructured high-dimensional treatments like texts, by leveraging the power of generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI). Specifically, we propose to use a deep generative model such as large language models (LLMs) to efficiently generate treatments and use their internal representation for subsequent causal effect estimation. We show that the knowledge of this true internal representation helps disentangle the treatment features of interest, such as specific sentiments and certain topics, from other possibly unknown confounding features. Unlike existing methods, the proposed GenAI-Powered Inference (GPI) methodology eliminates the need to learn causal representation from the data, and hence produces more accurate and efficient estimates. We formally establish the conditions required for the nonparametric identification of the average treatment effect, propose an estimation strategy that avoids the violation of the overlap assumption, and derive the asymptotic properties of the proposed estimator through the application of double machine learning. Finally, using an instrumental variables approach, we extend the proposed GPI methodology to the settings in which the treatment feature is based on human perception. The GPI is also applicable to text reuse where an LLM is used to regenerate existing texts. We conduct simulation and empirical studies, using the generated text data from an open-source LLM, Llama 3, to illustrate the advantages of our estimator over state-of-the-art causal representation learning algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用生成式人工智能进行因果推断：文本作为处理的应用
本文展示了如何利用生成式人工智能（GenAI）的力量，提升对非结构化高维处理（如文本）进行因果推断的有效性。具体而言，我们提出使用深度生成模型（如大型语言模型LLMs）高效生成处理，并利用其内部表征进行后续因果效应估计。我们证明，对这种真实内部表征的知识有助于分离出感兴趣的处理特征（如特定情感和某些主题），并将其与可能未知的混杂特征区分开。与现有方法不同，所提出的GenAI赋能推断（GPI）方法无需从数据中学习因果表征，因而能产生更准确、高效的估计结果。我们正式确立了非参数识别平均处理效应所需的条件，提出了一种避免违反重叠假设的估计策略，并通过双重机器学习推导了所提估计量的渐近性质。最后，我们利用工具变量方法，将GPI方法扩展至处理特征基于人类感知的情形。GPI同样适用于文本重用场景，其中LLM用于再生现有文本。我们通过使用开源LLM Llama 3生成的文本数据进行模拟和实证研究，展示了我们的估计量相较于当前最先进的因果表征学习算法的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of estimating causal effects from unstructured, high-dimensional text treatments, this paper introduces GenAI-Powered Inference (GPI), a method that leverages large language models to generate treatments and exploit their internal representations for causal effect estimation. Unlike traditional approaches requiring causal representation learning from data, GPI directly uses the LLM’s latent space to disentangle target treatment features—such as sentiment or topic—from confounding variables, thereby improving estimation accuracy and efficiency. The method formally establishes identification conditions for average treatment effects, avoids overlap assumption violations via double machine learning, and extends to human-perception-based treatments using instrumental variables. Empirical and simulation results using Llama 3-generated text confirm that GPI outperforms state-of-the-art causal representation learning methods in both accuracy and efficiency.
为解决从非结构化、高维文本治疗变量中估计因果效应的难题，本文提出GenAI-Powered Inference（GPI）方法，利用大型语言模型生成治疗变量并借助其内部表征进行因果效应估计。与传统方法需从数据中学习因果表征不同，GPI利用LLM生成文本的内在结构，将目标治疗特征（如情感或主题）与混杂变量分离，从而提升估计精度与效率。该方法正式确立了平均处理效应的识别条件，通过双重机器学习避免重叠假设的违反，并扩展至基于人类感知的治疗变量场景。实验与模拟结果表明，使用Llama 3生成的文本数据，GPI在准确性和效率上优于当前主流的因果表征学习方法。
Appearance-Invariant Detection of Suggestive Motion via Laban Movement Descriptors
Authors: Jaehoon Ahn, Jeonghan Kong, Moon-Ryul Jung
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026 poster
First: 2026-05-23T09:22:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:42:25+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables. Extended version of a poster accepted to SIGGRAPH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Content moderation in online multiplayer 3D virtual environments is increasingly automated, yet detection has focused on images, video, and audio, leaving suggestive motion a blind spot. We present a motion-only classification pipeline that detects suggestive and explicit movement from SMPL skeleton trajectories using Laban Movement Analysis (LMA) descriptors. On a dataset spanning everyday, artistic, suggestive, and explicit movement (17+ hours of video), a logistic regression trained on 61-feature LMA descriptors reaches 68% binary SFW/NSFW accuracy (70% random forest) under a leak-free evaluation protocol. At this level, our descriptor performs comparably to a learned video model trained on the same motion re-rendered as appearance-free video, a gray figure with no clothing, skin, or scene. The indirectness (tortuosity) of each joint's trajectory, measured as the ratio of the joint's path length to its net displacement, peaks at the suggestive tier, showing that the Direct-to-Indirect polarity of Laban's Space factor provides an interpretable marker of the shift from functional to suggestive motion. Ultimately, Laban-based kinematic descriptors offer a lightweight, interpretable approach to suggestive-motion detection: every decision decomposes into named, theory-grounded features. Because the classifier operates on pose trajectories alone, moderation can run directly on avatar poses in virtual environments, with no appearance data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于拉班运动描述符的暗示性动作外观不变检测
在线多人3D虚拟环境中的内容审核日益自动化，但现有检测主要针对图像、视频和音频，对暗示性动作存在盲区。本文提出一种仅基于运动的分类流程，利用拉班运动分析（LMA）描述符从SMPL骨架轨迹中检测暗示性和显性动作。在涵盖日常、艺术、暗示及显性动作（共17小时视频）的数据集上，基于61维LMA特征的逻辑回归模型在无泄漏评估协议下达到68%的SFW/NSFW二分类准确率（随机森林为70%）。在此水平下，该描述符表现与在相同运动重渲染为无外观视频（灰色人形，无服装、皮肤或场景）上训练的视频模型相当。每个关节轨迹的间接性（曲折度），即路径长度与净位移之比，在暗示性动作层级达到峰值，表明拉班空间因素的“直接-间接”极性可作为从功能性动作向暗示性动作转变的可解释性标记。最终，基于拉班的运动学描述符提供了一种轻量且可解释的暗示性动作检测方法：每个决策均可分解为命名明确、理论支撑的特征。由于分类器仅基于姿态轨迹运行，审核可直接在虚拟环境中的角色姿态上执行，无需外观数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in automated content moderation for suggestive motion in 3D virtual environments, this work introduces a motion-only classification pipeline using Laban Movement Analysis descriptors applied to SMPL skeleton trajectories. The method extracts 61 kinematic features capturing movement characteristics, and a logistic regression model trained on these features achieves 68% binary SFW/NSFW accuracy on a 17+ hour dataset of diverse motion types, outperforming random guessing and matching a learned video model when motion is rendered as appearance-free. Key findings reveal that trajectory tortuosity—measured as path length over displacement—peaks at the suggestive motion tier, confirming Laban’s Space factor as an interpretable indicator of motion intent. The approach enables real-time moderation using only pose data, without visual appearance.
为弥补3D虚拟环境中自动内容审核对暗示性动作的盲区，该研究提出基于Laban动作分析的运动轨迹特征检测方法，仅利用SMPL骨架轨迹提取61维运动特征，训练分类器区分暗示性与非暗示性动作。在17小时以上动作数据集上，逻辑回归模型在严格评估下达到68%二分类准确率（随机森林70%），性能与将运动重渲染为无外观视频的模型相当。关键发现是关节轨迹的曲折度在暗示性动作层级达到峰值，验证了Laban空间因素中‘直接到间接’极性作为动作意图转变的可解释性指标。
QueryOcc: Query-based Self-Supervision for 3D Semantic Occupancy
Authors: Adam Lilja, Ji Lan, Junsheng Fu, Lars Hammarstrand
First: 2025-11-21T13:05:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:41:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning 3D scene geometry and semantics from images is a core challenge in computer vision and a key capability for autonomous driving. Since large-scale 3D annotation is prohibitively expensive, recent work explores self-supervised learning directly from sensor data without manual labels. Existing approaches either rely on 2D rendering consistency, where 3D structure emerges only implicitly, or on discretized voxel grids from accumulated lidar point clouds, limiting spatial precision and scalability. We introduce QueryOcc, a query-based self-supervised framework that learns continuous 3D semantic occupancy directly through independent 4D spatio-temporal queries sampled across adjacent frames. The framework supports supervision from either pseudo-point clouds derived from vision foundation models or raw lidar data. To enable long-range supervision and reasoning under constant memory, we introduce a contractive scene representation that preserves near-field detail while smoothly compressing distant regions. QueryOcc surpasses previous camera-based methods by 26% in semantic RayIoU on the self-supervised Occ3D-nuScenes benchmark while running at 11.6 FPS, demonstrating that direct 4D query supervision enables strong self-supervised occupancy learning. https://research.zenseact.com/publications/queryocc/
中文标题/摘要
标题：QueryOcc：基于查询的自监督3D语义占据学习
从图像中学习3D场景几何结构与语义是计算机视觉的核心挑战，也是自动驾驶的关键能力。由于大规模3D标注成本过高，近期研究探索直接从传感器数据中进行自监督学习，无需人工标注。现有方法要么依赖2D渲染一致性（3D结构仅隐式出现），要么基于累积激光雷达点云的离散体素网格，限制了空间精度与可扩展性。我们提出QueryOcc，一种基于查询的自监督框架，通过在相邻帧间采样的独立4D时空查询，直接学习连续的3D语义占据。该框架支持来自视觉基础模型生成的伪点云或原始激光雷达数据的监督。为实现长程监督与常量内存下的推理，我们引入一种收缩场景表示，保留近场细节的同时平滑压缩远场区域。QueryOcc在自监督Occ3D-nuScenes基准测试中，语义RayIoU指标超越现有相机方法26%，运行速度达11.6 FPS，证明直接4D查询监督可实现强大的自监督占据学习。https://research.zenseact.com/publications/queryocc/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of 3D annotation and limitations of existing self-supervised methods, QueryOcc introduces a query-based framework that learns continuous 3D semantic occupancy directly from 4D spatio-temporal queries sampled across adjacent frames. It leverages either pseudo-point clouds from vision models or raw lidar data for supervision and employs a contractive scene representation to maintain detail near the camera while compressing distant regions for efficient long-range reasoning. Experiments on the Occ3D-nuScenes benchmark show that QueryOcc achieves a 26% improvement in semantic RayIoU over prior camera-based self-supervised methods while operating at 11.6 FPS.
为降低大规模3D标注成本并突破现有自监督方法的局限，QueryOcc提出一种基于查询的框架，直接从相邻帧采样的4D时空查询中学习连续的3D语义占据。该框架支持由视觉基础模型生成的伪点云或原始激光雷达数据提供的监督，并引入一种收缩场景表示以保留近场细节同时压缩远场区域，实现高效长程推理。在Occ3D-nuScenes自监督基准测试中，QueryOcc在语义RayIoU指标上比现有相机基自监督方法提升26%，同时保持11.6 FPS的推理速度。
Edit the Bits, Diff the Codes: Bitwise Residual Editing for Visual Autoregressive Models
Authors: Shengqiang Zhang, Ruotong Liao, Volker Tresp, Barbara Plank, Hinrich Schütze
First: 2026-06-11T16:41:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:41:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive (VAR) generators requires controlling both what the model samples and where the sampled change is written back into the image code. Existing VAR editors mainly operate on token streams, features, or flat next-token logits, leaving two native structures of bitwise-residual VAR models underused: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field from which the image is assembled. We propose BitResEdit, a training-free editor for bitwise-residual VAR generators such as Infinity. BitEdit performs source-negative guidance by tilting the post-CFG per-bit log-odds along a source--target contrast computed on a shared edited prefix, then projects each update into a closed-form Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts the sampled bits into per-scale continuous-code residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them through the generator's native sum-of-scales. Together they couple decision-time bit guidance with combination-time code composition, so masked-out latent features are preserved exactly by code arithmetic while localized, scale-aware edits are applied inside the target region. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit attains the strongest text alignment among same-backbone VAR editors, improving CLIP on the edited region by +1.07 over the strongest prior editor while keeping background preservation competitive with it. Ablations show BitEdit and ResEdit play complementary roles in target alignment and background preservation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：编辑比特，差异编码：面向视觉自回归模型的比特残差编辑
文本引导的图像编辑需同时控制模型采样内容及采样结果写回图像码的位置。现有视觉自回归（VAR）编辑器主要操作令牌流、特征或扁平下一令牌对数概率，而未充分利用比特残差VAR模型的两种原生结构：逐比特伯努利预测头与加性多尺度残差码域。我们提出BitResEdit，一种面向如Infinity等比特残差VAR生成器的无训练编辑器。BitEdit通过在共享编辑前缀上计算源-目标对比，倾斜后CFG的逐比特对数似然，再将更新投影至围绕干净CFG采样器的闭式伯努利-KL可信区域；ResEdit将采样比特转换为逐尺度连续码残差，通过定位掩码进行门控，再通过生成器原生的多尺度求和机制重新注入。二者结合决策时比特引导与组合时码域合成，使掩码外潜在特征通过码域运算精确保留，同时在目标区域施加局部、尺度感知的编辑。在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B时，BitResEdit在同架构VAR编辑器中实现最强文本对齐，编辑区域CLIP得分较最强前序编辑器提升+1.07，背景保留能力亦与之相当。消融实验表明BitEdit与ResEdit在目标对齐与背景保留中互补。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to precisely control both content and location in text-guided image editing using visual autoregressive models, the authors introduce BitResEdit, a training-free editor tailored for bitwise-residual VAR generators like Infinity. BitEdit guides bit-level sampling by adjusting per-bit log-odds based on source-target contrast from a shared edited prefix, then constrains updates within a Bernoulli-KL trust region. ResEdit translates sampled bits into continuous-scale residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them via the model’s native sum-of-scales structure. Together, these components enable exact preservation of masked latent features while applying localized, scale-aware edits. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit achieves the best text alignment among same-backbone editors, boosting CLIP score in edited regions by +1.07 over prior methods while maintaining competitive background preservation.
为实现对视觉自回归模型中内容与位置的精确控制，作者提出BitResEdit，一种针对位级残差VAR生成器（如Infinity）的无训练编辑器。BitEdit通过对比共享编辑前缀的源-目标差异，调整采样后CFG的位级对数似然，并在伯努利-KL信任区域内约束更新。ResEdit将采样位转换为多尺度连续残差，通过定位掩码进行门控后，重新注入模型原生的多尺度残差码场。二者协同实现决策时的位级引导与组合时的码级重构，确保掩码区域特征精确保留的同时，实现目标区域的局部、尺度感知编辑。在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B时，BitResEdit在相同架构编辑器中表现最佳，编辑区域CLIP得分较前序最优方法提升+1.07，同时背景保留能力保持竞争力。
InnoEval: On Research Idea Evaluation as a Knowledge-Grounded, Multi-Perspective Reasoning Problem
Authors: Shuofei Qiao, Yunxiang Wei, Xuehai Wang, Bin Wu, Boyang Xue, Ningyu Zhang, Hossein A. Rahmani, Yanshan Wang, Qiang Zhang, Keyan Ding, Jeff Z. Pan, Huajun Chen, Emine Yilmaz
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-16T00:40:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:39:44+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid evolution of Large Language Models has catalyzed a surge in scientific idea production, yet this leap has not been accompanied by a matching advance in idea evaluation. The fundamental nature of scientific evaluation needs knowledgeable grounding, collective deliberation, and multi-criteria decision-making. However, existing idea evaluation methods often suffer from narrow knowledge horizons, flattened evaluation dimensions, and the inherent bias in LLM-as-a-Judge. To address these, we regard idea evaluation as a knowledge-grounded, multi-perspective reasoning problem and introduce InnoEval, a deep innovation evaluation framework designed to emulate human-level idea assessment. We apply a heterogeneous deep knowledge search engine that retrieves and grounds dynamic evidence from diverse online sources. We further achieve review consensus with an innovation review board containing reviewers with distinct academic backgrounds, enabling a multi-dimensional decoupled evaluation across multiple metrics. We construct comprehensive datasets derived from authoritative peer-reviewed submissions to benchmark InnoEval. Experiments demonstrate that InnoEval can consistently outperform baselines in point-wise, pair-wise, and group-wise evaluation tasks, exhibiting judgment patterns and consensus highly aligned with human experts.

标题：InnoEval：将研究想法评估视为知识驱动的多视角推理问题

Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation
Authors: Aruna Dey, Suraj Biswas
First: 2026-06-11T16:38:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:38:38+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 8 figures, 3 tables. Conceptual framework paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.
中文标题/摘要
标题：是你还是你的环境？一种基于基因组锚定的个性化生理解读贝叶斯推断框架
个性化健康AI系统面临根本性的冷启动问题：生理解读的机器学习模型需数周个体行为数据才能区分体质变异与环境驱动的偏差。我们提出一种基于因果推断和贝叶斯先验设计的解决方案。个体基因组特征作为外生遗传锚点——一种领域知识驱动、个性化且在受孕时即固定的先验，免疫反向因果，且在任何行为观测前即可获得。该锚点初始化个体生理设定点的贝叶斯信念状态：G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i)，其中beta_i为GWAS推导的效应大小，g_i为风险等位基因计数。每项新生理测量P产生非体质性偏差delta = P - G-hat，从而分离环境与状态信号与体质固定基线。随着行为数据积累，先验按G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t衰减，从基因主导过渡到经验基线主导的推断。相同55ms的HRV，对预测80ms者生成抑制假设，对预测30ms者生成增强假设——无个性化锚点则无法实现此反转。我们在六个生理领域构建该架构，按证据强度分级基因组先验，区分稳健复制的锚点基因（FTO、FADS1/2、FKBP5）与争议候选基因（SLC6A4、MAOA、DRD2）。我们厘清关联、孟德尔随机化与个体因果推断的边界，并定义四项部署约束：证据分级先验、动态衰减、祖先匹配效应大小、归因而非确定性输出。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the cold-start challenge in personalized health AI by introducing a Bayesian inference framework that uses an individual’s genomic profile as a fixed, domain-informed prior to distinguish constitutional physiological baselines from environmental deviations. The method models each person’s baseline as a weighted sum of genetic risk alleles and GWAS-derived effect sizes, then updates this prior over time using incoming physiological measurements, allowing the model to transition from genome-dominated to behavior-dominated inference. Experimental results across six physiological domains show that the framework successfully generates personalized hypotheses — such as predicting whether a given HRV value indicates suppression or enhancement — based on individual genomic anchors, and distinguishes robustly supported genes (e.g., FTO, FADS1/2) from weaker candidates (e.g., SLC6A4, MAOA), while enforcing four key deployment constraints: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution-based output rather than deterministic predictions.
该论文为个性化健康AI的冷启动问题提出了一种基于贝叶斯推断的框架，利用个体基因组作为固定、领域导向的先验，区分生理基线与环境偏差。方法通过GWAS效应值和等位基因计数初始化个体生理设定点，随行为数据积累动态更新先验，融合基因组锚点与经验测量。在六个生理领域实验中，框架能准确实现个性化解读——例如相同55毫秒HRV在不同基因组先验下触发不同假设（抑制或增强），并识别出FTO等稳健锚点基因，同时标记SLC6A4等弱候选基因。框架还定义了四项部署约束，确保先验依据证据分级、动态衰减、祖先匹配效应值及归因导向而非确定性输出。
Differentiable Thermodynamic Phase-Equilibria for Machine Learning
Authors: Karim K. Ben Hicham, Moreno Ascani, Jan G. Rittig, Alexander Mitsos
First: 2026-03-11T19:14:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:31:43+00:00
Comments: 45 pages, 27 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate prediction of phase equilibria remains a central challenge in chemical engineering. Physics-consistent machine learning methods that incorporate thermodynamic structure into neural networks have recently shown strong performance for activity-coefficient modeling. However, extending such approaches to equilibrium data arising from an extremum principle, such as liquid-liquid equilibria, remains difficult. Here we present DISCOMAX, a differentiable algorithm for phase-equilibrium calculation that guarantees thermodynamic consistency at both training and inference, only subject to a user-specified discretization. The method combines discrete enumeration of feasible phase states with masked softmax aggregation in the backward pass, with the propagation of the true equilibrium state in the forward pass, using a straight-through gradient estimator to enable physics-consistent end-to-end learning of neural \gls{gE}-models. We show that this approach bears analogy to statistical thermodynamics, and we evaluate it on binary liquid-liquid equilibrium data where it outperforms existing surrogate-based methods, while offering a general framework for learning from different kinds of equilibrium data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器学习的可微分热力学相平衡
准确预测相平衡仍是化学工程的核心挑战。近期，将热力学结构融入神经网络的物理一致机器学习方法在活度系数建模中表现出色。然而，将其扩展到由极值原理产生的平衡数据（如液-液平衡）仍具挑战性。本文提出DISCOMAX，一种可微分的相平衡计算算法，该算法在训练和推理阶段均保证热力学一致性，仅受限于用户指定的离散化。该方法结合了可行相态的离散枚举与反向传播中的掩码softmax聚合，以及前向传播中真实平衡态的传播，利用直通梯度估计器实现对神经\gls{gE}-模型的物理一致端到端学习。我们表明该方法与统计热力学具有类比性，并在二元液-液平衡数据上评估，其性能优于现有代理方法，同时提供了一种从不同种类平衡数据中学习的通用框架。

为解决机器学习在预测相平衡（特别是由极值原理驱动的液-液平衡）时缺乏热力学一致性的问题，作者提出了DISCOMAX算法：一种可微分的相平衡计算方法，能在训练和推理阶段保证热力学一致性（仅受限于用户指定的离散化）。该方法结合了可行相态的离散枚举、反向传播中的掩码softmax聚合，以及前向传播中真实平衡态的传递，通过直通梯度估计器实现神经gE模型的端到端物理一致性学习。实验表明，DISCOMAX在二元液-液平衡数据上优于现有代理模型方法，同时提供了一种适用于多种平衡数据学习的通用框架。
Using Seismic Statistical Features and VQ-VAE to Improve Spatiotemporal Seismicity Predictability
Authors: Wei Quan, Denise Gorse
First: 2026-06-08T18:43:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:31:27+00:00
Comments: Title updated from "Spatiotemporal Seismic Hazard Assessment Using VQ-VAE and Seismic Statistical Features" to "Using Seismic Statistical Features and VQ-VAE to Improve Spatiotemporal Seismicity Predictability" in v2 to better reflect the focus of the paper. The content is unchanged apart from the title and minor copyediting
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper we build upon a previous study in which we demonstrated, using XGBoost and earthquake catalogue data from Japan and Chile, that a set of 60 seismic statistical features (SSFs) had much greater predictive value than a set of 428 generic time series features from the tsfresh package. We here extend this previous work in two key ways, focusing on data from Japan as a large dataset is necessary in order to allow for the training of a deep learning (autoencoder) model. First, we move from whole-region prediction (considering, for each candidate event, the likelihood of an event M $\geq$ 5.0 anywhere in the region in the next 15 days) to localised predictions in which both the region of feature computation and the region of prediction are restricted to a circle of radius 24 km around the candidate event, and we show that performance remains excellent, similar to our previous whole-region study for the same area. Second, we here couple this proven set of SSFs, based on one-dimensional (catalogue) data, with a novel feature based on two-dimensional seismic maps, obtained by training a VQ-VAE model to reproduce such maps as output and identifying a measure of its error in doing so with a localised build-up of crustal stress. We show that while localised prediction based on SSFs can be effective alone, with test AUC values as high as those obtained in the case of Japan in our previous whole-region study, the inclusion of the new natively-spatial VQ-VAE-derived feature, top-ranked by SHAP analysis, can enhance performance and additionally appears to near-wholly replace the traditionally-computed $b$-value in terms of feature usage.

为提升地震可预测性，本研究在先前使用地震统计特征（SSFs）工作的基础上，引入VQ-VAE深度学习模型提取地震地图的空间特征。方法将预测范围从全区域调整为以候选事件为中心24公里半径的局部区域，并将VQ-VAE的重建误差（解释为地壳应力累积）与SSFs结合。实验表明，SSFs单独使用即可获得高预测性能（AUC与先前全区域研究相当），而加入VQ-VAE空间特征可显著提升精度，且该特征在SHAP分析中排名最高，几乎完全取代传统b值作为主要预测因子。
Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG
Authors: Hoin Jung, Xiaoqian Wang
First: 2026-06-11T16:30:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:30:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向长文档RAG的不确定性感知混合检索

Lyapunov-Based PI-Like Control for Robust Trajectory Tracking of a Four-Wheel Independently Driven and Steered Robot: Design and Experimental Validation
Authors: Branimir Ćaran, Vladimir Milić, Marko Švaco, Bojan Jerbić
First: 2026-02-17T08:36:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:29:10+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, a Lyapunov-based synthesis of a PI-like controller is proposed for robust trajectory tracking of an independently driven and steered four-wheel mobile robot. For the robot considered in this work, an explicit structurally verified mathematical model is used to enable systematic controller design with rigorous stability guarantees suitable for real time implementation. An augmented Lyapunov-based practical stability analysis is developed for the combined velocity-error and integral-error dynamics of the inner loop, yielding explicit bounds and sufficient conditions for practical stability and uniform ultimate boundedness of the combined velocity-error and integral-error state. The resulting control law retains a PI-like structure with model-based feedforward compensation, making it suitable for implementation on standard embedded platforms while improving robustness against configuration dependent residual dynamics and unmodelled effects. The effectiveness and robustness of the proposed design are demonstrated experimentally on a four-wheel independently steered and independently driven mobile robot platform, under both horizontal and vertical operating conditions and benchmarked against a PI controller and a sliding-mode controller.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于李雅普诺夫的PI类控制用于四轮独立驱动转向机器人鲁棒轨迹跟踪：设计与实验验证
本文提出一种基于李雅普诺夫的PI类控制器合成方法，用于独立驱动和转向四轮移动机器人的鲁棒轨迹跟踪。针对本文所考虑的机器人，采用显式结构验证的数学模型，以实现系统化控制器设计并提供严格的稳定性保证，适用于实时实现。针对内环的组合速度误差与积分误差动力学，开发了一种扩展的李雅普诺夫实用稳定性分析，得出组合误差状态的实用稳定性和一致最终有界性的显式界限与充分条件。所得控制律保留PI类结构并结合基于模型的前馈补偿，适合在标准嵌入式平台实现，同时增强对配置依赖残余动力学和未建模效应的鲁棒性。该设计的有效性与鲁棒性已在四轮独立转向和驱动移动机器人平台上通过实验验证，涵盖水平与垂直操作条件，并与PI控制器和滑模控制器进行对比。

Motivated by the need for robust trajectory tracking in four-wheel independently driven and steered robots, this paper proposes a Lyapunov-based PI-like controller that ensures stability and performance under real-time constraints. Adaptive Turn-Taking for Real-time Multi-Party Voice Agents
Authors: Soumyajit Mitra, Prabhat Pandey, Abhinav Jain, Shanmukha Sahith, K V Vijay Girish
First: 2026-06-11T16:27:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:27:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Turn-taking in multi-party spoken conversations remains a fundamental challenge for voice-based agents, particularly under dynamic floor competition and varying user expectations. We propose ModeratorLM, a role-playing voice agent that conditions turn-taking behavior on an explicitly assigned role in multi-party settings. The system is built on a speech large language model operating in chunk-wise streaming manner. We further introduce a reasoning-augmented variant that incorporates chain-of-thought reasoning over conversational context and the assigned role. We construct RolePlayConv, a large-scale synthetic dataset of spoken multi-party conversations with diverse assistant roles. Experiments on real-world meeting data and RolePlayConv show improved turn-taking precision by over 40% and recall by more than 70%, while substantially reducing false-positive interruptions compared to non-role-conditioned baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向实时多方语音代理的自适应轮流发言
多方口语对话中的轮流发言仍是语音代理的核心挑战，尤其在动态发言竞争和用户期望变化的环境下。我们提出ModeratorLM，一种基于角色扮演的语音代理，其轮流发言行为根据在多方场景中明确分配的角色进行调节。该系统基于分块流式处理的语音大语言模型构建，并进一步引入结合对话上下文与角色的思维链推理增强版本。我们构建了RolePlayConv，一个包含多样助理角色的大型合成多方口语对话数据集。在真实会议数据和RolePlayConv上的实验表明，相比无角色条件的基线方法，该方法轮流发言的精确率提升超40%，召回率提升超70%，同时大幅降低误中断率。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of turn-taking in dynamic multi-party voice conversations, the paper introduces ModeratorLM, a speech large language model that adapts its turn-taking behavior based on an explicitly assigned role within the conversation. The system operates in a chunk-wise streaming fashion and includes a reasoning-augmented variant that leverages chain-of-thought reasoning over context and role.
