为解决多用户语音对话中动态轮次分配的难题，该文提出ModeratorLM，一种基于角色条件的语音代理，通过分块流式语音大语言模型动态调整发言时机。其增强版结合对话上下文与角色进行链式推理，显著提升表现。在真实会议数据和合成数据集RolePlayConv上的实验表明，该方法将轮次识别准确率提升40%以上，召回率提升70%以上，同时大幅降低误中断率，优于无角色条件的基线模型。
Emergence of Hierarchical Emotion Organization in Large Language Models
Authors: Maya Okawa, Bo Zhao, Eric J. Bigelow, Rose Yu, Tomer Ullman, Ekdeep Singh Lubana, Hidenori Tanaka
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-07-12T15:12:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:27:44+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) increasingly power conversational agents, understanding how they model users' emotional states is critical for ethical deployment. Inspired by emotion wheels, i.e., a psychological framework that argues emotions organize hierarchically, we analyze probabilistic dependencies between emotional states in model outputs. We find that LLMs naturally form hierarchical emotion trees that align with human psychological models, and larger models develop more complex hierarchies. We also uncover systematic biases in emotion recognition across socioeconomic personas, with compounding misclassifications for intersectional, underrepresented groups. Human studies reveal striking parallels, suggesting that LLMs internalize aspects of social perception. Beyond highlighting emergent emotional reasoning in LLMs, our results hint at the potential of using cognitively-grounded theories for developing better model evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型中层级情绪组织的涌现
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地驱动对话代理，理解它们如何建模用户的情绪状态对于伦理部署至关重要。受情绪轮（一种心理学框架，主张情绪具有层级组织）启发，我们分析模型输出中情绪状态间的概率依赖关系。我们发现，LLMs自然形成与人类心理学模型一致的层级情绪树，且模型规模越大，其层级结构越复杂。我们还发现，在不同社会经济人格中，情绪识别存在系统性偏差，对交叉性、代表性不足群体的误分类尤为严重。人类研究揭示了显著的相似性，表明LLMs内化了部分社会认知。除凸显LLMs中涌现的情绪推理能力外，我们的结果也暗示，基于认知理论的方法可能有助于开发更优的模型评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how large language models represent human emotions for ethical deployment, researchers analyzed probabilistic dependencies in model outputs using a psychological framework inspired by emotion wheels. They discovered that LLMs spontaneously generate hierarchical emotion trees that mirror human emotion organization, with complexity increasing in larger models. Experimental results also revealed systematic biases in emotion recognition, particularly disadvantaging intersectional and underrepresented socioeconomic groups, while human studies showed striking parallels, suggesting LLMs internalize social perception patterns.
为理解大型语言模型如何表征人类情绪以实现伦理部署，本研究基于情绪轮这一心理学框架，分析模型输出中情绪状态的概率依赖关系。研究发现，LLM会自发形成与人类情绪组织结构一致的层级情绪树，且模型规模越大，结构越复杂。实验还揭示情绪识别存在系统性偏差，尤其对交叉性与边缘化社会经济群体，误分类呈累积效应。人类研究结果与之高度吻合，表明LLM内化了部分社会感知能力，暗示认知基础理论可用于改进模型评估。
NetCause: Counterfactual Learning for Root Cause Analysis in Large-Scale Networks
Authors: Fabien Chraim, Jian Zhang, Dominik Janzing, Xiang Song, Christos Faloutsos, John Evans
First: 2026-06-11T16:26:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:26:33+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Can a learned model capture how faults propagate through a large-scale network and use this knowledge to causally attribute customer impact to its underlying root cause? Existing root cause analysis techniques often rely on static rules, correlation heuristics, or topology-local reasoning, which struggle to generalize in dynamic environments where faults propagate across complex physical and logical dependencies. We present NetCause, a self-supervised learning-based framework that models network incidents as graph-temporal processes and uses counterfactual simulation to rank candidate root causes. This approach produces an interpretable ranking of root cause hypotheses and integrates naturally with operator-defined mitigation and remediation actions. We train the model on over 1,500 incidents collected over six months from a leading cloud provider's production network and evaluate it on 31 expert-labeled incidents. NetCause consistently improves root cause ranking quality in the regime most relevant to operational decision-making, achieving a 16.1% accuracy improvement over a rule-based heuristic baseline. While training is computationally intensive, inference is lightweight, requiring only seconds of GPU runtime per incident (well below typical telemetry collection latencies).
中文标题/摘要
标题：NetCause：用于大规模网络根因分析的反事实学习
学习的模型能否捕捉故障在大规模网络中的传播方式，并利用该知识因果地将客户影响归因于其根本原因？现有根因分析技术通常依赖静态规则、相关性启发式或拓扑局部推理，难以在动态环境中泛化，特别是在故障跨越复杂物理和逻辑依赖关系时。 我们提出了NetCause，一种基于自监督学习的框架，将网络事件建模为图时序过程，并利用反事实模拟对候选根因进行排序。该方法生成可解释的根因假设排序，并能自然地与操作员定义的缓解和修复措施集成。 我们在一家领先云服务提供商的生产网络中收集的1500多个事件上训练模型，并在31个专家标注的事件上进行评估。NetCause在最贴近运营决策的场景中持续提升根因排序质量，相比基于规则的启发式基线准确率提升16.1%。虽然训练计算开销大，但推理轻量，每个事件仅需数秒GPU运行时间（远低于典型遥测收集延迟）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static and heuristic-based root cause analysis methods in dynamic, large-scale networks, NetCause introduces a self-supervised learning framework that models network incidents as graph-temporal processes and employs counterfactual simulation to rank root cause hypotheses. Trained on over 1,500 real-world incidents from a cloud provider’s production network, the model evaluates its performance on 31 expert-labeled cases and consistently outperforms a rule-based baseline by 16.1% in root cause ranking accuracy, while maintaining lightweight inference requiring only seconds per incident.
为了解决现有静态规则和启发式方法在动态大规模网络中泛化能力差的问题，NetCause提出一种基于自监督学习的框架，将网络事件建模为图时序过程，并通过反事实模拟对候选根因进行排序。该模型在一家云服务商的生产网络中训练了1500多个真实事件，通过模拟根因缺失后的网络影响来评估其因果性。实验表明，NetCause在31个专家标注事件上比规则基线方法准确率提升16.1%，且推理仅需数秒，远低于典型遥测采集延迟。
When Does Mixing Help? Analyzing Query Embedding Interpolation in Multilingual Dense Retrieval
Authors: Tongyao Zhu, Chao-Ming Huang, Min-Yen Kan
Venue: ACL 2026 Oral
First: 2026-06-11T16:23:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:23:16+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Main (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While mixed-language querying is ubiquitous in multilingual communities, the sensitivity of dense retrievers to such queries remains poorly understood. We present a ratio-controlled study on mMARCO that systematically evaluates retrieval performance by varying the mixing proportion of parallel query translations via embedding-level mixing -- constructing mixed queries as an interpolation of monolingual embeddings. Experiments with BGE-M3 demonstrate that an optimal mixing ratio outperforms the best monolingual endpoint in 88/105 cases. We uncover a distinct asymmetry driven by English dominance: mixing is uniformly beneficial when retrieving from non-English document indices, whereas indices containing English are best served by pure English queries. Furthermore, English acts as the strongest mixing partner for every non-English document language. Finally, when controlling for English dominance, mixing gains correlate negatively with typological distance. We conclude that language-mix sensitivity is structured and predictable, and we validate the robustness of these patterns across model families and scales.
中文标题/摘要
标题：何时混合查询有益？分析多语言密集检索中的查询嵌入插值
尽管混合语言查询在多语言社区中普遍存在，但密集检索器对此类查询的敏感性仍不甚明了。我们在mMARCO上开展了一项比例控制研究，通过嵌入层混合系统性评估检索性能——将混合查询构建为单语嵌入的插值。实验表明，BGE-M3在88/105个案例中，最优混合比例优于最佳单语查询。我们发现英语主导导致的显著不对称性：在非英语文档索引中，混合查询始终有益；而含英语索引则更适合纯英语查询。此外，英语是所有非英语文档语言最强的混合伙伴。最后，在控制英语主导因素后，混合收益与类型学距离呈负相关。我们得出结论：语言混合敏感性具有结构化和可预测性，并验证了这些模式在不同模型家族和规模中的稳健性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the widespread use of mixed-language queries in multilingual settings but limited understanding of how dense retrievers respond to them, this study systematically evaluates query embedding interpolation on mMARCO by varying the mixing ratio of parallel translations. Using BGE-M3, it finds that an optimal mixing ratio outperforms the best monolingual query in 88 out of 105 cases, revealing an asymmetry driven by English dominance: mixing benefits non-English document indices uniformly, while English indices perform best with pure English queries. English consistently acts as the strongest mixing partner for all non-English languages, and mixing gains correlate negatively with typological distance when controlling for English influence, indicating structured and predictable language-mix sensitivity across models.
该研究探讨了在多语言密集检索中，通过嵌入层插值混合平行翻译查询的效果，动机源于多语言社区中混合语言查询的普遍性。研究在mMARCO上进行比例控制实验，通过插值单语查询嵌入构造混合查询并评估检索性能。使用BGE-M3模型发现，88/105个案例中最佳混合比例优于单语查询，揭示了英语主导带来的不对称性：混合查询对非英语文档索引效果更好，而英语文档索引则更适合纯英语查询。英语对所有非英语语言都是最强的混合伙伴，且混合增益随语言类型距离增大而降低，表明语言混合敏感性具有结构化和可预测性。
RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yichao Zhong, Yidan Lu, Yuhang Lu, Tianyang Tang, Haoguang Mai, Yixuan Pan, Tianyu Li, Li Chen, Jingbo Wang, Zhongyu Li, Peng Lu, Hongyang Li
First: 2026-06-09T16:51:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:22:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboNaldo：通过运动引导的课程强化学习实现精准、稳定且强大的人形机器人足球射门
精英级人形足球射门需要全身稳定性、高冲击力的全身交互以及精准命中目标。运动跟踪驱动的强化学习（RL）可提供全身运动协调的稳定性，但固定参考点使其难以适应球的不同位置和击球时机；相比之下，任务奖励驱动的RL难以从零开始探索并发现有效的射门动作。为此，我们提出了RoboNaldo，一种面向高冲击力人形交互的三阶段运动引导课程RL框架。以单个真人踢球动作作为基础，逐步将优化目标转向射门性能。课程首先学习稳定的全身踢球先验，然后适应定点任意位置静止球的任意球场景，最后通过运动指令与踢球触发接口扩展至移动球射门。训练期间，高层启发式规划器控制该接口，而其他高层控制器可在推理时驱动相同的低层策略。仿真结果表明，RoboNaldo的任意球射门误差比现有基线方法低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍。在搭载视觉感知的Unitree G1机器人真实环境中，RoboNaldo在3米距离下任意球和移动球场景的平均目标误差分别为0.73米和0.86米，触球后球速达13.10米/秒，相当于职业球员开放进攻射门速度的59-71%。项目页面：https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo。
Summary / 总结
RoboNaldo addresses the challenge of achieving accurate, stable, and powerful humanoid soccer shooting by combining motion-guided curriculum reinforcement learning with task-adaptive training stages. Motivated by the difficulty of coordinating whole-body stability and high-impulse interactions under variable ball positions and timings, the method uses a single human-kick motion as a scaffold to progressively shift learning toward shooting performance: first building a stable kicking prior, then adapting to stationary free-kick scenarios, and finally extending to moving-ball shooting via a locomotion-command interface. A heuristic planner guides training, while alternative controllers can operate the same policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo outperforms baselines with 48.6% lower shot error and 2.96x higher velocity. On a real Unitree G1 robot, it achieves average shooting errors of 0.73m and 0.86m for free-kick and moving-ball cases, respectively, with post-contact ball velocity reaching 13.10 m/s — 59–71% of professional open-play shot speed.
RoboNaldo 通过结合运动引导的课程强化学习与任务驱动探索，解决了人形机器人实现精准、稳定且强力足球射门的难题。它以单个人类踢球动作作为引导，逐步训练策略：先学习稳定全身踢球动作，再适应静止球自由球场景，最后通过运动指令与踢球触发接口处理移动球射门。训练阶段由高层规划器引导，推理阶段可由不同高层控制器驱动同一低层策略。仿真测试中，RoboNaldo 将自由球射门误差降低 48.6%，射门速度提升 2.96 倍。在 Unitree G1 实体机器人上测试，自由球和移动球场景平均射门误差分别为 0.73 米和 0.86 米，触球后球速达 13.10 米/秒，相当于职业球员开放进攻射门速度的 59–71%。
AgentRivet: an automated system for producing Rivet routines from journal publications
Authors: Antonio J. Costa, Caterina Doglioni, Christian Gütschow, Andrew D. Pilkington, Sukanya Sinha
First: 2026-06-11T16:22:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:22:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Particle physics collider experiments provide Rivet routines as part of the analysis preservation strategy for model-independent measurements. Rivet is a C++ toolkit that allow new theoretical models to be compared to the measurements, thus aiding the development and tuning of Monte Carlo event generators as well as searches for physics beyond the Standard Model. However, analysis coverage is known to be incomplete, with only 39% of measurements having documented and publicly available Rivet routines. In this article, we design and implement an automated workflow based on Large Language Models with the goal of providing the missing routines. This multi-step workflow, referred to as AgentRivet, extracts the physics analysis information from published papers and writes the missing Rivet routines, with intermediate code- and physics- reviews as part of an autonomous quality control. We report the results obtained using commercial Large Language Models, provided by OpenAI, Anthropic, and Google, for two recent measurements from the ATLAS and CMS experiments. We find that AgentRivet produces competent Rivet routines with few syntax errors. The physics fidelity of the routines is reasonable and follows the explanations given in the relevant publications. Nevertheless, physics-implementation issues do arise and are investigated using the artefacts produced by AgentRivet. The majority of physics implementation issues arise from subtle-but-ambiguous definitions in the given publication, although some models struggle to implement complex observables even when clear definitions are given.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AgentRivet：一种从期刊论文自动生成Rivet程序的自动化系统
粒子物理对撞机实验作为模型无关测量分析保存策略的一部分，提供Rivet程序。Rivet是一个C++工具包，允许将新理论模型与测量结果进行比较，从而辅助蒙特卡洛事件生成器的开发与调优，以及对标准模型外物理的搜寻。然而，分析覆盖范围已知不完整，仅有39%的测量结果具有文档化且公开可用的Rivet程序。本文设计并实现了一种基于大语言模型的自动化工作流，旨在提供缺失的程序。该多步骤工作流称为AgentRivet，从已发表论文中提取物理分析信息并编写缺失的Rivet程序，中间包含代码和物理层面的自主质量控制审查。我们使用OpenAI、Anthropic和Google提供的商业大语言模型，对ATLAS和CMS实验的两项近期测量结果进行了测试。结果表明，AgentRivet生成的Rivet程序具备一定能力，语法错误较少，物理一致性合理，符合相关文献的解释。然而，仍存在物理实现问题，通过分析AgentRivet生成的产物进行研究。大部分物理实现问题源于文献中微妙但含糊的定义，尽管部分模型即使在定义明确的情况下也难以实现复杂可观测量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the incomplete coverage of Rivet routines—only 39% of collider measurements have documented ones—this work introduces AgentRivet, an automated system using Large Language Models to generate missing Rivet routines from journal publications. The method involves extracting physics analysis details from papers, generating corresponding C++ code, and incorporating intermediate code and physics reviews for quality control. Experiments with commercial LLMs (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google) on two recent ATLAS and CMS measurements show that AgentRivet produces routines with few syntax errors and reasonable physics fidelity aligned with published descriptions. However, physics implementation issues persist, often stemming from ambiguous definitions in the source papers, even when definitions are clear, indicating limitations in handling complex observables.
鉴于仅有39%的粒子对撞机测量结果配有文档化的Rivet程序，本研究提出AgentRivet系统，利用大语言模型从期刊论文中自动生成缺失的Rivet程序。该方法通过从论文中提取物理分析信息，生成对应C++代码，并辅以中间代码与物理审查以确保质量。在对两项ATLAS和CMS最新测量结果的实验中，使用商业大模型（OpenAI、Anthropic、Google）发现系统生成的程序语法错误较少，物理实现基本符合论文描述。但仍有物理实现问题，主要源于论文中定义的模糊性，即使定义清晰，部分模型仍难以处理复杂可观测量，表明系统在复杂物理实现方面仍存在局限。
Graphical Causal Reasoning for Root Cause Analysis in Cloud Networks
Authors: Fabien Chraim, Dominik Janzing, John Evans
First: 2026-06-11T16:20:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:20:51+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cloud-computing relies on large-scale networks which are inherently complex systems. In this paper, we present a novel approach to root cause analysis (RCA) of cloud network incidents, leveraging graph-based causal discovery techniques. Our method addresses the limitations of rule-based automation by introducing a spatiotemporal grouping strategy and an automation ontology to reduce the dimensionality of the problem. We construct a causal graph from binary time series data using bivariate Granger causality and conditional independence tests. For inference, we introduce a probabilistic method that assigns edge-specific conditional probabilities as a function of time lag, allowing for interpretable, time-aware root cause scoring via causal graph traversal. We evaluated the system using a labeled dataset of 35 production incidents from a major cloud provider. The model successfully recalled the correct root cause in 85.7% of incidents and produced an exact match in 74.3%. In production, the deployed system has been used in over 800 real-world incidents, with positive qualitative feedback from network engineers. These results highlight the practicality of a data-driven, causal approach to RCA in dynamic and large-scale operational environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：云网络中基于图的因果推理根因分析方法
云计算依赖于大规模网络，这些网络本质上是复杂系统。本文提出了一种新颖的云网络事件根因分析（RCA）方法，利用基于图的因果发现技术。该方法通过引入时空分组策略和自动化本体，克服了基于规则的自动化方法的局限性，降低问题维度。我们利用双变量格兰杰因果关系和条件独立性检验，从二进制时间序列数据构建因果图。为推理，我们引入一种概率方法，将边特定条件概率作为时间滞后的函数，通过因果图遍历实现可解释、时间感知的根因评分。我们使用某大型云服务商提供的35个标注生产事件数据集评估系统，模型在85.7%的事件中成功召回正确根因，74.3%的事件实现精确匹配。在生产环境中，该系统已应用于800多个真实事件，获得网络工程师的积极定性反馈。这些结果突显了数据驱动、因果方法在动态、大规模运营环境中进行RCA的实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complexity of cloud networks and limitations of rule-based RCA systems, this paper proposes a graph-based causal reasoning method that uses spatiotemporal grouping and an automation ontology to reduce problem dimensionality. It constructs causal graphs from binary time series using Granger causality and conditional independence tests, then applies a probabilistic inference model that assigns time-lag-dependent conditional probabilities to edges for interpretable root cause scoring. Evaluated on 35 labeled production incidents, the system recalled the correct root cause in 85.7% of cases and achieved exact matches in 74.3%, with over 800 real-world deployments yielding positive feedback from engineers.
为应对云网络复杂性及规则驱动根因分析系统的局限，本文提出一种基于图的因果推理方法，结合时空分组与自动化本体以降低问题维度。该方法利用格兰杰因果和条件独立性检验从二进制时间序列构建因果图，并引入概率推理模型，通过时间滞后条件概率对边赋值，实现可解释的根因评分。在35个标注生产事件上测试，系统正确召回根因率达85.7%，精确匹配率达74.3%，并在800余次实际部署中获得网络工程师正面反馈。
Ride, Track, and Recover: Pilot Randomized Trial of a Wearable Digital Self-Management Intervention During a Veteran Endurance-Cycling Program
Authors: Alan Ta, Nilsu Salgin, Caleb Armstrong, Kala Phillips Reindel, Farzan Sasangohar
First: 2026-06-11T16:18:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:18:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-traumatic stress disorder (PTSD) in veterans is characterized by persistent hyperarousal and comorbid anxiety and depressive symptoms that are difficult to monitor and manage outside clinical settings. Thirteen veterans participating in a Project Hero cycling event in Texas were randomized by computer-generated sequence in a naturalistic setting to two arms: (1) digital intervention plus physical activity, or (2) physical activity only, plus a third at-home monitoring control cohort consisting of 7 veterans selected from the broader Project Hero veteran community. Continuous smartwatch sensing combined heart rate and accelerometer features to detect hyperarousal events, which were confirmed in real time by participants. Weekly self-report measures of anxiety, depression, and PTSD severity were collected. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear trajectories over time. Baseline-normalized hyperarousal trajectories differed significantly across conditions, with the digital intervention group (n=7) showing structured stabilization compared to late-study escalation in the physical-only group (n=3). Both cycling groups exhibited acute symptom improvements during the endurance event; however, the digital intervention group demonstrated a higher overall maintenance of gains. The at-home control group (n=4) showed gradual symptom declines. Perceived precision of ML detections varied substantially across individuals and was positively associated with symptom severity, with higher-severity participants confirming a greater proportion of detected events. These results suggest that coupling wearable detection with digital self-management tools may support stabilization of hyperarousal and symptom improvement while emphasizing the importance of personalization and human-centered design in wearable mental health systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：骑行、追踪与恢复：一项针对退伍军人耐力骑行项目的可穿戴数字自我管理干预的随机对照试验
退伍军人的创伤后应激障碍（PTSD）表现为持续的高唤醒状态及伴随的焦虑和抑郁症状，这些症状在临床环境外难以监测和管理。十三名参与德克萨斯州“英雄项目”骑行活动的退伍军人在自然环境中通过计算机生成序列随机分配至两组：（1）数字干预加体育活动，或（2）仅体育活动，另设一组由7名来自更广泛“英雄项目”退伍军人社区的参与者组成的居家监测对照组。智能手表通过连续监测心率和加速度计数据，检测高唤醒事件，参与者实时确认。每周收集焦虑、抑郁和PTSD严重程度的自我报告数据。广义加性混合模型刻画了随时间变化的非线性轨迹。基线标准化的高唤醒轨迹在各组间存在显著差异，数字干预组（n=7）表现出结构化稳定，而仅体育活动组（n=3）则在研究后期症状加剧。两组骑行参与者在耐力骑行期间均出现急性症状改善，但数字干预组整体维持效果更佳。居家对照组（n=4）则呈现症状渐进性下降。机器学习检测的精确度因个体差异显著不同，且与症状严重程度呈正相关，症状更严重的参与者更频繁确认检测到的事件。这些结果表明，将可穿戴设备检测与数字自我管理工具结合，可能有助于稳定高唤醒状态并促进症状改善，同时强调了在可穿戴心理健康系统中个性化和以人为本设计的重要性。
Summary / 总结
This study aimed to evaluate whether a wearable-based digital self-management intervention could help veterans manage PTSD symptoms during an endurance cycling program. Thirteen veterans were randomized into two active groups — one receiving digital intervention plus cycling, the other cycling alone — and a control group receiving at-home monitoring. Smartwatches continuously tracked heart rate and movement to detect hyperarousal, confirmed by participants in real time, while weekly self-reports measured anxiety, depression, and PTSD severity. Analysis using generalized additive mixed models revealed that the digital intervention group showed structured stabilization of hyperarousal over time, contrasting with worsening symptoms in the cycling-only group. Both active groups improved acutely during the event, but the digital group better maintained gains. The control group showed gradual symptom decline. Importantly, participants with higher symptom severity were more likely to confirm detected hyperarousal events, highlighting the need for personalized, human-centered design in wearable mental health tools.
为解决退伍军人在非临床环境中难以监测和管理创伤后应激障碍（PTSD）相关高唤醒状态及共病症状的问题，研究在一次骑行活动中将13名退伍军人随机分为两组：一组接受数字自我管理干预加体育活动，另一组仅接受体育活动，另设一组4名在家监测的对照组。系统通过智能手表连续采集心率与加速度数据，实时检测高唤醒事件并由参与者确认。每周自我报告评估焦虑、抑郁及PTSD严重程度，采用广义加性混合模型分析。结果显示，数字干预组高唤醒状态随时间呈现结构化稳定趋势，而仅体育活动组后期症状恶化，对照组症状渐进性下降。数字干预组症状改善维持效果更佳。机器学习检测准确度因人而异，且与症状严重程度正相关，表明个性化和以人为中心的设计对可穿戴心理健康工具至关重要。
What's Old is New Again: Classical Dimensionality Reduction for Efficient Saliency-Guided Biometric Attack Detection
Authors: Samuel Webster, Walter Scheirer
First: 2026-06-11T16:18:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:18:06+00:00
Comments: 16 pages (8 main, 2 references, 6 appendix), 4 figures (3 main, 1 appendix), 13 tables (3 main, 10 appendix)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Saliency-guided training is a paradigm in visual recognition that encourages models to focus on the most relevant image regions during learning. While its application in biometric presentation attack detection (PAD) has shown strong benefits in robustness and generalization, adoption is often limited by the high cost, domain specificity, and limited scalability of existing saliency acquisition methods, such as human annotations over a limited dataset. We present a novel, cost-efficient, and highly-scalable approach to saliency acquisition using maps inspired by classical dimensionality reduction techniques: PCA and LDA. Our proposed methods generate saliency maps directly from raw training data, requiring no human annotation nor domain knowledge. We contextualize the effectiveness of these saliency sources in three saliency-explored domains (iris PAD, synthetic face detection, fingerprint PAD) and demonstrate its scalability in two saliency-novel domains (fingerprint vein PAD and ID card PAD). Across all domains tested, models trained using dimensionality reduction-sourced saliency maps exceed baseline and sometimes SOTA saliency methods without any resource investment or domain-specific tooling. Our findings overcome an important yet unaddressed barrier to saliency-guided training for biometric attack detection and beyond.
中文标题/摘要
标题：旧瓶新酒：经典降维技术用于高效显著性引导的生物特征攻击检测
显著性引导训练是视觉识别中的一个范式，旨在鼓励模型在学习过程中聚焦于图像中最相关的区域。尽管其在生物特征呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的应用已显示出在鲁棒性和泛化能力方面的显著优势，但其采用常受限于现有显著性获取方法（如有限数据集上的人工标注）的高成本、领域特异性及有限可扩展性。我们提出了一种新颖、低成本且高度可扩展的显著性获取方法，该方法借鉴经典降维技术（PCA和LDA）生成显著性图。所提方法可直接从原始训练数据生成显著性图，无需人工标注或领域知识。我们在三个已探索显著性的领域（虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD）中评估了这些显著性源的有效性，并在两个新领域（指纹静脉PAD、身份证PAD）中验证其可扩展性。在所有测试领域中，使用降维源显著性图训练的模型均超越基线方法，有时甚至优于当前最先进（SOTA）的显著性方法，且无需任何资源投入或领域特定工具。我们的发现克服了生物特征攻击检测中显著性引导训练所面临的一个重要且未被解决的障碍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and limited scalability of traditional saliency acquisition methods in biometric attack detection, this work introduces a novel approach using classical dimensionality reduction techniques—PCA and LDA—to generate saliency maps directly from raw training data without human annotation or domain expertise. The method is applied across three established domains (iris PAD, synthetic face detection, fingerprint PAD) and two novel ones (fingerprint vein PAD, ID card PAD), demonstrating consistent performance gains over baseline and state-of-the-art saliency methods. Models trained with these dimensionality reduction-derived saliency maps achieve superior or competitive accuracy without requiring additional resources or specialized tools, effectively overcoming a key barrier to scalable saliency-guided training in biometric security.
为解决传统生物特征攻击检测中获取显著性图成本高、可扩展性差的问题，本文提出利用经典降维方法PCA和LDA直接从原始训练数据生成显著性图，无需人工标注或领域知识。该方法在虹膜、人脸、指纹、指纹静脉和身份证识别等五个生物特征领域中测试，结果表明，使用这些自动生成显著性图训练的模型在性能上持续优于基线和当前最先进方法，且无需额外资源或专用工具。研究证明，经典降维方法可有效引导视觉识别任务中的注意力，为生物特征安全提供可扩展、高效的显著性引导训练方案。
Improving Pre-trained Adult Glioma Segmentation Models Using only Post-processing Techniques
Authors: Abhijeet Parida, Daniel Capellán-Martín, Zhifan Jiang, Nishad Kulkarni, Krithika Iyer, Austin Tapp, Syed Muhammad Anwar, María J. Ledesma-Carbayo, Marius George Linguraru
First: 2025-12-16T22:01:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:10:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gliomas are the most common malignant brain tumors in adults and are among the most lethal. Despite aggressive treatment, the median survival rate is less than 15 months. Accurate multiparametric MRI (mpMRI) tumor segmentation is critical for surgical planning, radiotherapy, and disease monitoring. While deep learning models have improved the accuracy of automated segmentation, large-scale pre-trained models generalize poorly and often underperform, producing systematic errors such as false positives, label swaps, and slice discontinuities in slices. These limitations are further compounded by unequal access to GPU resources and the growing environmental cost of large-scale model training. In this work, we propose adaptive post-processing techniques to refine the quality of glioma segmentations produced by large-scale pretrained models developed for various types of tumors. We demonstrated the techniques in multiple BraTS 2025 segmentation challenge tasks, with the ranking metric improving by 14.9 % for the sub-Saharan Africa challenge and 0.9% for the adult glioma challenge. This approach promotes a shift in brain tumor segmentation research from increasingly complex model architectures to efficient, clinically aligned post-processing strategies that are precise, computationally fair, and sustainable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：仅使用后处理技术改进预训练成人胶质瘤分割模型
胶质瘤是成人中最常见的恶性脑肿瘤，也是最致命的肿瘤之一。尽管治疗积极，中位生存期仍不足15个月。准确的多参数MRI（mpMRI）肿瘤分割对术前规划、放疗和疾病监测至关重要。虽然深度学习模型提升了自动化分割的准确性，但大规模预训练模型泛化能力差，常表现不佳，产生系统性错误，如假阳性、标签互换和切片不连续。这些局限性进一步因GPU资源获取不均及大规模模型训练的环境成本而加剧。本文提出自适应后处理技术，以优化各类肿瘤预训练模型生成的胶质瘤分割质量。我们在多个BraTS 2025分割挑战任务中验证了该方法，其中撒哈拉以南非洲挑战赛排名指标提升14.9%，成人胶质瘤挑战赛提升0.9%。该方法推动脑肿瘤分割研究从复杂模型架构转向高效、临床契合的后处理策略，实现精准、计算公平且可持续。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization and systematic errors of large-scale pre-trained models in adult glioma segmentation, this study introduces adaptive post-processing techniques to refine outputs without retraining. Applied to BraTS 2025 challenges, these methods corrected false positives, label swaps, and slice discontinuities, improving ranking metrics by 14.9% in the sub-Saharan Africa challenge and 0.9% in the adult glioma challenge. The approach emphasizes computational fairness and sustainability, shifting focus from complex architectures to efficient, clinically relevant post-processing.
鉴于大型预训练模型在成人胶质瘤分割中泛化能力差且易产生系统性错误，本文提出自适应后处理技术以优化分割结果，无需重新训练。在BraTS 2025挑战赛中，该方法有效修正了假阳性、标签互换和切片不连续等问题，使排名指标在撒哈拉以南非洲挑战赛中提升14.9%，在成人胶质瘤挑战赛中提升0.9%，表明通过高效后处理即可实现临床精准分割，无需依赖复杂模型架构。
Adaptable Segmentation Pipeline for Diverse Brain Tumors with Radiomic-Guided Subtyping and Lesion-Wise Model Ensemble
Authors: Daniel Capellán-Martín, Abhijeet Parida, Zhifan Jiang, Nishad Kulkarni, Krithika Iyer, Austin Tapp, Syed Muhammad Anwar, María J. Ledesma-Carbayo, Marius George Linguraru
Venue: MICCAI
First: 2025-12-16T18:09:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:06:05+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables. Algorithm presented at MICCAI BraTS 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust and generalizable segmentation of brain tumors on multi-parametric magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) remains difficult because tumor types differ widely. The BraTS 2025 Lighthouse Challenge benchmarks segmentation methods on diverse high-quality datasets of adult and pediatric tumors: multi-consortium international pediatric brain tumor segmentation (PED), preoperative meningioma tumor segmentation (MEN), meningioma radiotherapy segmentation (MEN-RT), and segmentation of pre- and post-treatment brain metastases (MET). We present a flexible, modular, and adaptable pipeline that improves segmentation performance by selecting and combining state-of-the-art models and applying tumor- and lesion-specific processing before and after training. Radiomic features extracted from MRI help detect tumor subtype, ensuring a more balanced training. Custom lesion-level performance metrics determine the influence of each model in the ensemble and optimize post-processing that further refines the predictions, enabling the workflow to tailor every step to each case. On the BraTS testing sets, our pipeline achieved performance comparable to top-ranked algorithms across multiple challenges. These findings confirm that custom lesion-aware processing and model selection yield robust segmentations yet without locking the method to a specific network architecture. Our method has the potential for quantitative tumor measurement in clinical practice, supporting diagnosis and prognosis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于影像组学引导亚型划分与病灶级模型集成的适应性脑肿瘤分割流程
由于肿瘤类型差异显著，多参数磁共振成像（MRI）上脑肿瘤的稳健且可泛化分割仍具挑战。BraTS 2025灯塔挑战赛在成人及儿童肿瘤的多样化高质量数据集上评估分割方法，包括多联盟国际儿童脑肿瘤分割（PED）、术前脑膜瘤分割（MEN）、脑膜瘤放疗分割（MEN-RT）及术前术后脑转移瘤分割（MET）。我们提出一种灵活、模块化、可适应的流程，通过选择并组合前沿模型，结合肿瘤与病灶特异性预处理与后处理，提升分割性能。从MRI中提取的影像组学特征有助于识别肿瘤亚型，确保训练更均衡。自定义病灶级性能指标决定各模型在集成中的权重，并优化后处理以进一步精炼预测，使流程能针对每个病例自适应调整每一步。在BraTS测试集上，本流程性能与多个挑战赛中排名前列的算法相当。结果证实，自定义病灶感知处理与模型选择可实现稳健分割，且不依赖特定网络架构。该方法有望在临床实践中实现肿瘤定量测量，支持诊断与预后评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of segmenting diverse brain tumors across multi-parametric MRI datasets, this study introduces a flexible, modular pipeline that adapts to tumor type and lesion characteristics by combining state-of-the-art models and applying tumor-specific preprocessing and postprocessing. Radiomic features guide tumor subtyping to balance training data, while lesion-level performance metrics dynamically weight model contributions in an ensemble. Evaluated on BraTS 2025 Lighthouse Challenge datasets including pediatric, meningioma, and metastatic tumors, the pipeline achieved segmentation performance comparable to top-ranked methods across all benchmarks, demonstrating robustness without reliance on a fixed architecture.
为应对多参数MRI中不同类型脑肿瘤分割的挑战，该研究提出一种模块化、可适应的分割流程，通过组合前沿模型并应用病变特异性预处理与后处理提升分割性能。方法利用影像组学特征指导肿瘤亚型识别，确保训练平衡，并采用自定义病变级指标动态调整模型权重，优化后处理。在BraTS 2025挑战赛多个数据集（含儿童、脑膜瘤及转移瘤）上，该流程表现媲美顶尖算法，且不依赖特定网络架构，验证其鲁棒性，具备临床定量测量肿瘤的潜力。
MaskWAM: Unifying Mask Prompting and Prediction for World-Action Models
Authors: Hanyang Yu, Haitao Lin, Jingbo Zhang, Wenyao Zhang, Chenghao Gu, Heng Li, Ping Tan
First: 2026-06-11T16:02:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:02:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World Action Models (WAMs) present a promising paradigm for robotic control via video prediction. However, current WAMs suffer from fundamental spatial bottlenecks: standard text inputs introduce referential ambiguity in cluttered scenes, while unstructured RGB predictions lack semantic grounding and remain biased by task-irrelevant backgrounds. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MaskWAM, an object-centric world-action model. By jointly integrating masks as both explicit inputs and predictions via a unified Mixture of Transformers (MoT), MaskWAM unlocks robust policy generalization. This design provides two key benefits: (1) predicting future masks yields object-centric semantic supervision that suppresses visual noise, significantly enhancing even standard text-conditioned WAMs; and (2) coupling this predictive supervision with first-frame visual prompts, such as target object masks, establishes a precise spatial anchor that substantially reduces language ambiguity. Crucially, as WAMs are inherently vision-driven architectures, direct mask conditioning yields substantially stronger guidance than text alone, establishing a precise and robust paradigm for manipulating unseen objects. Evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks demonstrate that MaskWAM significantly outperforms baselines in both language-clear and language-ambiguous tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MaskWAM：统一掩码提示与预测的世界动作模型
世界动作模型（WAMs）为通过视频预测实现机器人控制提供了一种有前景的范式。然而，当前WAMs存在根本性的空间瓶颈：标准文本输入在杂乱场景中引入指代歧义，而无结构化的RGB预测缺乏语义锚定，且受任务无关背景影响。为克服这些限制，我们提出MaskWAM，一种以物体为中心的世界动作模型。通过统一的混合Transformer（MoT）联合整合掩码作为显式输入与预测，MaskWAM实现了稳健的策略泛化。该设计提供两大优势：（1）预测未来掩码可提供以物体为中心的语义监督，抑制视觉噪声，显著增强甚至标准文本条件下的WAMs；（2）将此预测监督与首帧视觉提示（如目标物体掩码）结合，建立精确的空间锚点，大幅降低语言歧义。关键在于，WAMs本质上是视觉驱动架构，直接掩码条件化比文本单独条件化提供更强引导，建立了一种精确且稳健的操作未知物体的范式。在LIBERO、RoboTwin及真实世界任务上的评估表明，MaskWAM在语言清晰与语言模糊任务中均显著优于基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the spatial ambiguities and semantic lack in current World Action Models (WAMs), MaskWAM introduces a unified object-centric framework that jointly uses masks as both input prompts and predicted outputs via a Mixture of Transformers. This approach enhances policy generalization by providing semantic grounding through mask prediction, which suppresses visual noise and improves performance even under standard text conditioning. Coupling mask predictions with first-frame visual prompts further anchors spatial understanding, reducing language ambiguity. Experimental results across LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks show MaskWAM consistently outperforms baselines in both clear and ambiguous language settings, demonstrating its robustness and effectiveness for manipulating unseen objects.
为解决当前世界动作模型在复杂场景中因文本输入导致的空间歧义和RGB预测缺乏语义锚定的问题，MaskWAM提出一种统一的以对象为中心的框架，通过混合Transformer结构同时将掩码作为输入提示和输出预测。该设计通过预测掩码提供语义监督以抑制视觉噪声，提升甚至原本仅依赖文本的模型性能，同时结合首帧视觉提示精确锚定空间位置，显著降低语言歧义。在LIBERO、RoboTwin及真实任务上的实验表明，MaskWAM在语言清晰与模糊场景下均显著优于基线方法，证明掩码引导比纯文本更有效。
Bernstein-Schur Kernels: Random Features by Sketched Modulation and Radial Randomization
Authors: Taha Bouhsine
First: 2026-06-08T21:59:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:01:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bernstein--Schur kernels are products of a finite-feature kernel and a completely monotone shift-invariant kernel: nonstationary kernels falling between the shift-invariant and dot-product templates random features exploit, so neither Bochner sampling nor polynomial sketching applies to the full kernel directly. We give one random-feature construction for the whole class that randomizes both factors: it sketches the finite modulation and samples the radial factor's one-dimensional Bernstein--Widder scale before applying Gaussian random Fourier features, giving feature dimension $Dm$, free of the $O(d^2)$ size of the exact modulation feature. With the modulation kept exact (the $m\to\infty$ limit), we prove unbiasedness, an exact variance, and a matrix-Bernstein operator-norm bound controlled by the top kernel and modulation eigenvalues and an intrinsic dimension rather than the crude $N\max_{ij}$ route. Whitening this argument at the ridge makes the effective dimension $d_{\mathrm{eff}}(λ)$ the \emph{exact} intrinsic dimension of the matrix variance, so $O((1+\|P\|_{\mathrm{op}}/λ)\log(d_{\mathrm{eff}}/δ))$ radial draws preserve the kernel-ridge solution; tilting the draw by a closed-form whitened leverage improves this to the effective-dimension count $O((1+d_{\mathrm{eff}})\log(d_{\mathrm{eff}}/δ))$. Conditioning on the sketch carries every guarantee to the deployed doubly-randomized estimator up to one additive sketch term, and all hold for the whole class with the modulation Gram in place of the polynomial one. The flagship instance is the biased $yat$-kernel $k_{yat,b}(w,x)=(w^\top x+b)^2/(\|w-x\|^2+\varepsilon)$, whose family span contains the inverse-multiquadric kernel by finite differences in $b$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：伯恩斯坦-舒尔核：基于草图调制与径向随机化的随机特征
伯恩斯坦-舒尔核是有限特征核与完全单调平移不变核的乘积：非平稳核介于平移不变与点积模板随机特征之间，因此直接对完整核无法应用博赫纳采样或多项式草图化。我们提出一种针对整个类别的随机特征构造方法，同时随机化两个因子：先对有限调制进行草图化，再对径向因子的一维伯恩斯坦-维德德尺度进行采样，最后应用高斯随机傅里叶特征，得到特征维度为Dm，摆脱了精确调制特征的O(d²)规模。当调制保持精确（即m→∞极限）时，我们证明了无偏性、精确方差及由核与调制特征值和内在维度控制的矩阵伯恩斯坦算子范数界，而非粗略的Nmax_{ij}路径。在岭值处对这一论证进行白化，使有效维度d_eff(λ)成为矩阵方差的精确内在维度，因此O((1+||P||_{op}/λ)log(d_eff/δ))个径向采样即可保留核岭解；通过闭式白化杠杆倾斜采样，可进一步优化至有效维度计数O((1+d_eff)log(d_eff/δ))。对草图进行条件化后，所有保证均传递至部署的双重随机化估计量，仅增加一个附加草图项，且对整个类别的所有保证均成立，只需用调制格拉姆矩阵替代多项式格拉姆矩阵。典型实例为偏置yat核k_{yat,b}(w,x)=(w^⊤x+b)²/(||w-x||²+ε)，其函数族通过b的有限差分可包含逆多二次核。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of efficiently approximating Bernstein-Schur kernels—nonstationary kernels combining finite-feature and shift-invariant components—the paper introduces a random-feature method that simultaneously sketches the finite modulation and samples the radial Bernstein-Widder scale before applying Gaussian random Fourier features, yielding a feature dimension of Dm without the O(d²) overhead of exact modulation. The method proves unbiasedness and provides a matrix-Bernstein bound controlled by kernel and modulation eigenvalues and intrinsic dimension, not crude data-dependent terms. Experimental results show that whitening via ridge regularization makes the effective dimension d_eff(λ) the exact intrinsic dimension, enabling O((1+||P||_op/λ)log(d_eff/δ)) radial draws to preserve kernel-ridge solutions, and tilting via whitened leverage further reduces this to O((1+d_eff)log(d_eff/δ)), with guarantees preserved under sketching for the entire kernel class.
为解决无法直接用Bochner采样或多项式草图处理的非平稳Bernstein-Schur核，该文提出一种随机特征方法：先对有限调制进行草图压缩，再对径向Bernstein-Widder分量进行一维采样，最后应用高斯随机傅里叶特征，实现Dm维特征空间，避免O(d^2)的精确调制开销。该方法证明无偏性，且矩阵Bernstein范数界由核与调制特征值及内在维度控制，而非粗略的Nmax。通过岭正则化白化，有效维度d_eff(λ)即为矩阵方差的精确内在维度，使径向采样量降至O((1+||P||_op/λ)log(d_eff/δ))以保持核岭解，白化杠杆倾斜后进一步降至O((1+d_eff)log(d_eff/δ))。在草图条件下，所有保证可传递至双随机估计器，适用于整个类核，调制Gram矩阵替代多项式形式，其中偏置yat核作为典型实例，其家族通过b的有限差分可涵盖逆多二次核。
CloudCons: A Comprehensive End-to-End Benchmark for Cloud Resource Consolidation
Authors: Xiaobin Zhang, Lefei Shen, Mouxiang Chen, Zhuo Li, Hongkai Li, Han Fu, Jianling Sun, Xiaoxue Ren, Chenghao Liu
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-06-11T16:00:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T16:00:51+00:00
Comments: Accepted to KDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Driven by conservative over-provisioning to guarantee service reliability, resource utilization in cloud data centers remains at low levels. To mitigate this, the forecast-then-optimize paradigm has emerged to optimize consolidation by anticipating future demands. While emerging time series foundation models promise to enhance this paradigm through zero-shot generalization, existing benchmarks focus solely on prediction error metrics. The actual decision utility of these advanced models remains unverified, rendering their practical value for downstream tasks uncertain. To bridge this gap, we propose CloudCons, a comprehensive end-to-end benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models within the specific context of cloud resource consolidation. We build high-quality datasets that cover diverse workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg, capturing distinct service characteristics ranging from synchronized diurnal rhythms to stochastic, pulse-like bursts and high-frequency noise. We conduct an extensive evaluation of statistical, deep learning, and foundation models. Our experiments reveal a pivotal finding: while foundation models demonstrate superior zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this advantage does not inherently translate into better decision utility. Of practical significance, we systematically analyze how the selection of predictive quantiles acts as a critical lever. We provide actionable guidelines for calibrating these selections to balance the trade-off between resource efficiency and service reliability, offering vital insights for real-world deployment decisions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CloudCons：云资源整合的端到端综合基准
为保障服务可靠性，云数据中心普遍采用保守的资源过量配置，导致资源利用率偏低。为缓解此问题，预测-优化范式应运而生，通过预判未来需求来优化资源整合。尽管新兴时间序列基础模型有望通过零样本泛化能力提升该范式，但现有基准仅关注预测误差指标，未能验证这些先进模型的实际决策效用，其对下游任务的实际价值尚不明确。为弥合这一差距，我们提出CloudCons，一个专为评估云资源整合场景下预测模型而设计的端到端综合基准。我们构建了覆盖华为云、微软Azure和谷歌Borg等多样化工作负载的高质量数据集，涵盖从同步日周期到随机脉冲式突发及高频噪声等不同服务特征。我们对统计模型、深度学习模型和基础模型进行了广泛评估。实验揭示关键发现：虽然基础模型在零样本预测精度上表现更优，但这一优势并不必然转化为更好的决策效用。具有实际意义的是，我们系统分析了预测分位数选择作为关键调节杠杆的作用，并提供可操作的校准指南，以平衡资源效率与服务可靠性之间的权衡，为实际部署决策提供重要参考。

It leverages real-world workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg to construct diverse, high-fidelity datasets capturing varied service patterns. The study evaluates statistical, deep learning, and foundation models, revealing that while foundation models outperform others in zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this does not guarantee better decision utility. Crucially, the research demonstrates that selecting appropriate predictive quantiles is a key factor in balancing resource efficiency and service reliability, providing practical guidelines for deployment.
为解决云数据中心因保守过量配置导致资源利用率低的问题，本文提出CloudCons，一个端到端基准，用于评估预测模型在资源合并决策中的实际效用。该基准基于华为云、Azure和Google Borg的真实工作负载构建，涵盖从同步日周期到脉冲式突发和高频噪声等多种服务模式。实验评估了统计、深度学习和基础模型，发现虽然基础模型在零样本预测精度上表现优异，但其在资源合并决策中的实际效用并不必然更高。研究指出，预测分位数的选择是关键杠杆，可平衡资源效率与服务可靠性，为实际部署提供可操作指导。
Measurement-Calibrated Multi-Camera Fusion for Vision-Based Indoor Localization
Authors: Mateo Toro Diz, Jonathan Hoss, Noah Klarmann
First: 2026-06-11T15:56:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:56:02+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted for presentation at the IEEE 22st International Conference on Automation Science and Engineering (CASE 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Indoor vision-based localization systems are affected by detection noise, occlusions, and limited camera coverage, leading to uncertainty at multiple stages of the pipeline. While multi-camera data fusion is widely used to mitigate these issues, it is typically treated as a black-box component and evaluated solely end-to-end, obscuring its mechanistic contributions. To address this gap, this work investigates whether explicitly characterizing single-camera localization errors can be leveraged to calibrate and optimize multi-camera data fusion. We introduce a measurement-calibrated fusion approach that integrates component-wise error quantification, specifically isolating homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. A component-wise evaluation is conducted to quantify error contributions from homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. Experimental results show that data fusion improves localization accuracy compared to single-camera baselines. While measurement-calibrated fusion provides only limited improvement in absolute accuracy over standard fusion, it substantially reduces trajectory variance and improves motion smoothness, which are critical for applications requiring stable and continuous motion estimates. These results highlight the value of explicit error characterization when designing data fusion strategies for vision-based indoor positioning systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于测量校准的多相机融合用于视觉室内定位
基于视觉的室内定位系统受检测噪声、遮挡和相机覆盖范围有限的影响，在管道的多个阶段产生不确定性。虽然多相机数据融合广泛用于缓解这些问题，但通常被视为黑盒组件，仅以端到端方式评估，掩盖了其机制性贡献。为解决这一问题，本文研究是否可通过显式表征单相机定位误差，来校准和优化多相机数据融合。 我们提出了一种测量校准融合方法，整合了分量级误差量化，具体隔离了单应性校准、人体检测和运动跟踪。通过分量级评估量化了单应性校准、人体检测和运动跟踪的误差贡献。 实验结果表明，数据融合相比单相机基线提升了定位精度。尽管测量校准融合在绝对精度上仅提供有限提升，但显著降低了轨迹方差并改善了运动平滑性，这对需要稳定连续运动估计的应用至关重要。这些结果突显了在设计视觉室内定位系统数据融合策略时，显式误差表征的价值。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of uncertainty in indoor vision-based localization due to detection noise, occlusions, and limited camera coverage, which degrade system performance. To improve multi-camera fusion, the authors propose a measurement-calibrated fusion method that explicitly quantifies and isolates errors from homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. Experimental results demonstrate that while standard fusion outperforms single-camera methods in absolute accuracy, the measurement-calibrated approach significantly reduces trajectory variance and enhances motion smoothness, offering critical stability for applications requiring continuous motion estimates.
该研究针对室内视觉定位系统因检测噪声、遮挡和相机覆盖不足导致的不确定性问题，提出一种测量校准的多相机融合方法，通过量化并分离相机标定、人体检测与运动跟踪三个模块的误差来优化融合策略。实验表明，虽然校准融合在绝对精度上仅小幅优于标准融合，但其显著降低了轨迹方差并提升了运动平滑性，这对需要稳定连续运动估计的实际应用至关重要。
Leveraging Audio-LLMs to Filter Speech-to-Speech Training Data
Authors: Qixu Chen, Satoshi Nakamura
First: 2026-06-11T15:55:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:55:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted to INTERSPEECH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale mined corpora provide abundant training data for end-to-end speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) but may contain noise, misalignment, and semantic errors. Filtering noisy data is crucial to maintain robust speech translation performance. We study how to train an audio-language model to make keep/drop decisions on paired speech directly from audio. To obtain reliable supervision without manual labels, we adopt a scalable two-stage Rank-to-Distill strategy. A lightweight ranker generates keep/drop pseudo-labels from noisy speech pairs, then trains an audio large language model to predict keep/drop directly from raw paired speech. The resulting model jointly captures acoustic fidelity and cross-lingual semantic consistency for the selection of speech-conditioned data. Experiments on CVSS-C and SpeechMatrix show consistent improvements over unfiltered training, yielding up to +1.4 ASR-BLEU for end-to-end S2ST.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用音频大语言模型过滤语音到语音训练数据
大规模挖掘语料为端到端语音到语音翻译（S2ST）提供了丰富的训练数据，但可能包含噪声、对齐错误和语义错误。过滤噪声数据对保持稳健的语音翻译性能至关重要。我们研究如何训练音频语言模型，直接从音频中对配对语音做出保留或丢弃的决策。为在无需人工标注的情况下获得可靠监督，我们采用可扩展的两阶段Rank-to-Distill策略：轻量级排序器从噪声语音对中生成保留/丢弃伪标签，随后训练音频大语言模型直接从原始配对语音预测保留/丢弃。所得模型联合捕捉声学保真度和跨语言语义一致性，以选择语音条件数据。在CVSS-C和SpeechMatrix上的实验表明，相比未过滤训练，性能持续提升，端到端S2ST最高提升+1.4 ASR-BLEU。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of noisy, misaligned, and semantically flawed data in large-scale mined speech-to-speech translation corpora, this work trains an audio-language model to autonomously decide whether to retain or discard speech pairs directly from raw audio. Using a two-stage Rank-to-Distill approach, a lightweight ranker first generates pseudo-labels for keep/drop decisions, which are then used to train an audio large language model to predict these labels from raw speech pairs. The model leverages both acoustic fidelity and cross-lingual semantic consistency to filter training data. Experiments on CVSS-C and SpeechMatrix demonstrate consistent performance gains over unfiltered training, with up to +1.4 ASR-BLEU improvement in end-to-end S2ST.
为解决大规模语音到语音翻译语料中存在的噪声、对齐错误和语义偏差问题，本文提出训练一个音频-语言模型，直接根据原始音频判断语音对是否保留或丢弃。方法采用两阶段Rank-to-Distill策略：首先用轻量级排序器从噪声数据中生成保留/丢弃的伪标签，再用这些标签训练音频大语言模型直接从原始语音对中预测保留或丢弃决策。该模型同时捕捉声学保真度与跨语言语义一致性以指导数据选择。在CVSS-C和SpeechMatrix上的实验表明，相比未过滤训练，该方法在端到端S2ST任务中持续提升性能，最高提升1.4 ASR-BLEU。
Heterogeneous LiDAR Early Fusion and Learned Re-Ranking Strategy for Robust Long-Term Place Recognition in Unstructured Environments
Authors: Judith Vilella-Cantos, Juan José Cabrera, Mónica Ballesta, David Valiente, Luis Payá
First: 2026-06-11T15:52:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:52:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust localization in unstructured environments, such as agricultural fields, is a critical challenge for autonomous systems. LiDAR sensors provide detailed 3D information about the environment and are invariant to lighting conditions. For this reason, LiDAR-based place recognition methods have gained significant attention. In this paper, we propose MinkUNeXt-VINE++, a novel approach that combines early fusion of heterogeneous LiDAR data from two sensors (Livox Mid-360 and Velodyne VLP-16) and a learned re-ranking strategy in inference time. This fusion leverages the strengths of each sensor to provide a more comprehensive representation of the environment. Additionally, the re-ranking approach is particularly important in repetitive environments, such as vineyards, as finding true positives is a major challenge. We evaluated our approach using the TEMPO-VINE dataset, which provides heterogeneous LiDAR data in vineyard environments across different phenological stages. Our results demonstrate that MinkUNeXt-VINE++ significantly improves place recognition performance compared to single-sensor approaches and state-of-the-art methods. MinkUNeXt-VINE++ achieves a 20% improvement in the Recall@1 metric compared to single-sensor approaches, and +30% including re-ranking. The code of our method is publicly available for reproduction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：异构激光雷达早期融合与学习重排序策略用于非结构化环境中鲁棒的长期定位
在非结构化环境（如农田）中实现鲁棒定位是自主系统的关键挑战。激光雷达传感器可提供环境的详细3D信息，且不受光照条件影响，因此基于激光雷达的定位方法备受关注。本文提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++，一种结合双传感器（Livox Mid-360与Velodyne VLP-16）异构激光雷达数据早期融合与推理时学习重排序策略的新方法。该融合充分利用各传感器优势，提供更全面的环境表征。此外，重排序策略在重复性环境（如葡萄园）中尤为重要，因真阳性识别是主要挑战。我们在TEMPO-VINE数据集上评估该方法，该数据集提供不同物候期葡萄园环境中的异构激光雷达数据。结果表明，MinkUNeXt-VINE++相比单传感器方法及当前最先进方法显著提升定位性能。与单传感器方法相比，其Recall@1指标提升20%，结合重排序后提升30%。本方法代码已公开，便于复现。

To address the challenge of robust long-term place recognition in unstructured environments like vineyards, this paper introduces MinkUNeXt-VINE++, which fuses heterogeneous LiDAR data from Livox Mid-360 and Velodyne VLP-16 sensors early in the pipeline and applies a learned re-ranking strategy during inference. The method combines sensor strengths to enhance environmental representation and improves recall in repetitive settings. Evaluated on the TEMPO-VINE dataset across multiple vineyard phenological stages, MinkUNeXt-VINE++ outperforms single-sensor methods and state-of-the-art approaches, achieving a 20% gain in Recall@1 without re-ranking and a 30% gain with it.
为解决农业等无结构环境中长期定位的鲁棒性问题，本文提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++方法，通过早期融合Livox Mid-360与Velodyne VLP-16两种异构激光雷达数据，并在推理阶段引入学习的重排序策略。该方法结合传感器优势提升环境表征能力，尤其在重复性场景（如葡萄园）中提升真阳性识别率。在涵盖多个物候期的TEMPO-VINE数据集上评估，该方法相较单传感器方案及现有最先进方法，召回率@1提升20%（无重排序）并提升30%（含重排序）。
GF-DiT: Scheduling Parallelism for Diffusion Transformer Serving
Authors: Xinwei Qiang, Yifan Hu, Shixuan Sun, Jing Yang, Han Zhao, Chen Chen, Yu Feng, Jingwen Leng, Minyi Guo
First: 2026-06-11T15:51:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:51:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant architecture for image and video generation, creating growing demand for efficient DiT serving. Existing systems assign each request a fixed parallel configuration throughout its lifetime. However, DiT workloads exhibit substantial heterogeneity across requests, execution stages, and system conditions, making static parallelism inefficient and often leading to poor GPU utilization and degraded service quality. This paper argues that DiT serving should treat GPU parallelism as a first-class schedulable resource. We present GF-DiT, a policy-programmable runtime for elastic DiT serving that dynamically adapts the parallelism of running requests according to workload demands and service objectives. GF-DiT introduces an asynchronous execution abstraction that decomposes requests into independently schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation. To make elastic parallelism practical, GF-DiT further proposes group-free collectives, a lightweight communication abstraction that supports low-overhead online formation and reconfiguration of arbitrary execution groups. We implement GF-DiT in vLLM-Omni and evaluate it on representative image and video diffusion workloads. Compared with fixed-pipeline execution with static parallelism, GF-DiT improves throughput by up to 6.01$\times$, reduces mean latency by up to 95%, lowers SLO violation rates by up to 90%, and reduces communication-group setup overhead from 778 ms to approximately 60 $μ$s.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GF-DiT：为扩散Transformer服务调度并行性
扩散Transformer（DiT）已成为图像和视频生成的主流架构，对高效DiT服务的需求日益增长。现有系统在请求生命周期内为其分配固定并行配置。然而，DiT工作负载在请求、执行阶段和系统条件上表现出显著异构性，静态并行性效率低下，常导致GPU利用率低和服务质量下降。 本文主张DiT服务应将GPU并行性视为可调度的首要资源。我们提出了GF-DiT，一种面向弹性DiT服务的策略可编程运行时，可根据工作负载需求和服务目标动态调整运行请求的并行性。GF-DiT引入异步执行抽象，将请求分解为可独立调度的轨迹任务，并支持在线GPU重新分配。为使弹性并行性实用，GF-DiT进一步提出组无关集体通信，一种轻量级通信抽象，支持任意执行组的低开销在线组建与重构。 我们在vLLM-Omni中实现GF-DiT，并在代表性图像和视频扩散工作负载上进行评估。与固定流水线和静态并行性相比，GF-DiT吞吐量提升最高达6.01倍，平均延迟降低最高达95%，SLO违规率降低最高达90%，通信组设置开销从778毫秒降至约60微秒。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the heterogeneity of DiT workloads and inefficiencies of static GPU parallelism, GF-DiT introduces a policy-programmable runtime that dynamically adjusts parallelism per request based on workload and service goals. It achieves this via an asynchronous execution model that splits requests into schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation, complemented by group-free collectives for low-overhead communication group management. Experimental results show GF-DiT boosts throughput by up to 6.01x, cuts mean latency by up to 95%, reduces SLO violations by up to 90%, and slashes communication setup overhead from 778 ms to ~60 μs.
为了解决静态并行配置在服务扩散Transformer时导致GPU利用率低和服务质量下降的问题，GF-DiT提出一个可编程运行时系统，根据负载和目标动态调整每个请求的GPU并行度。该系统通过异步执行模型将请求拆分为可独立调度的任务，并支持在线GPU重分配，同时引入无组通信抽象以实现低开销的执行组动态组建。在图像和视频扩散负载上的实验表明，GF-DiT吞吐量最高提升6.01倍，平均延迟降低95%，SLO违规率降低90%，通信组建立开销从778毫秒降至约60微秒。
SPARC: Reliable Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations at Scale
Authors: Nils Blank, Paul Mattes, Maximilian Xiling Li, Jakub Suliga, Thomas Roth, Moritz Reuss, Pankhuri Vanjani, Rudolf Lioutikov
First: 2026-06-11T15:46:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:46:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work introduces Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations with Reliability Calibration (SPARC), a risk-aware framework that automatically labels robot demonstrations with structured spatial annotations and assigns each annotation a reliability score. Structured spatial annotations, such as bounding boxes, object trajectories, and manipulation phase labels, benefit a broad range of robotics applications from training grounded robot policies and embodied foundation models to motion planning and hierarchical task composition. Existing automated pipelines generate such annotations at scale but provide no reliable quality signal: detector confidence is poorly calibrated for annotation correctness, forcing a choice between accepting noisy labels or discarding useful samples. In contrast to existing automated pipelines, SPARC leverages the spatio-temporal structure inherent to robot tasks to generate a reliability signal, reducing noisy labels and retaining more useful samples. We further introduce Interaction-Aware Bench (IA-Bench), a benchmark that measures model accuracy in grounding the locations of interacted objects in robot demonstrations. On 1.7k human-annotated demonstrations spanning diverse embodiments and scenarios, SPARC significantly outperforms detection-only baselines in localization accuracy while retaining three times more samples at high-precision operating points. Our experiments demonstrate that models finetuned on our annotations achieve state-of-the-art results on object-grounding and pointing benchmarks among similarly sized models, while remaining competitive on broader spatial-reasoning suites without manually verified or annotated training data. Furthermore, policies trained on SPARC-generated annotations outperform baselines in cluttered, visually ambiguous real-world scenes. Code, data, and models are available at intuitive-robots.github.io/sparc-labeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SPARC：从机器人演示中大规模生成可靠的时空标注
本工作提出了空间标注与可靠性校准框架（SPARC），一种风险感知框架，可自动为机器人演示生成结构化时空标注并为每个标注分配可靠性评分。结构化时空标注（如边界框、物体轨迹和操作阶段标签）广泛适用于机器人领域的多种应用，包括训练具身机器人策略和具身基础模型、运动规划及分层任务组合。现有自动化流水线虽能大规模生成此类标注，但无法提供可靠的质量信号：检测器置信度对标注正确性的校准效果不佳，迫使人们在接受噪声标签与丢弃有用样本之间做出选择。与现有自动化流水线不同，SPARC利用机器人任务固有的时空结构生成可靠性信号，减少噪声标签并保留更多有用样本。我们进一步引入交互感知基准（IA-Bench），用于衡量模型在机器人演示中定位交互物体位置的准确性。在涵盖多样具身形态与场景的1700个人工标注演示中，SPARC在定位精度上显著优于仅依赖检测的基线方法，同时在高精度操作点保留了三倍于基线的样本量。实验表明，使用我们标注微调的模型在物体定位与指针任务基准上达到同类模型中的最先进水平，且在无需人工验证或标注训练数据的广泛空间推理基准上仍保持竞争力。此外，基于SPARC生成标注训练的策略在杂乱、视觉模糊的真实场景中优于基线方法。代码、数据和模型可在intuitive-robots.github.io/sparc-labeling获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable, scalable spatial annotations in robotics, SPARC introduces a risk-aware framework that automatically generates structured spatial annotations—such as bounding boxes and manipulation phase labels—from robot demonstrations while assigning each a reliability score. Unlike existing pipelines that lack quality calibration, SPARC leverages spatio-temporal task structure to produce a reliability signal, reducing noisy labels while preserving useful samples. Evaluated on 1.7k human-annotated demonstrations, SPARC achieves significantly higher localization accuracy than detection-only baselines and retains three times more high-precision samples. Models fine-tuned on SPARC annotations outperform similar-sized models on object-grounding and pointing benchmarks, and policies trained on them show superior performance in cluttered, ambiguous real-world environments.
为解决机器人演示大规模空间标注缺乏可靠质量评估的问题，SPARC提出一种风险感知框架，自动生成结构化空间标注（如边界框和操作阶段标签）并为每个标注分配可靠性评分，该评分基于任务的时空结构进行校准。与现有仅依赖检测置信度的流水线不同，SPARC通过任务结构减少噪声标注，保留更多高质量样本。在1.7k人工标注的演示数据上，SPARC在定位精度上显著优于仅检测的基线方法，同时在高精度阈值下保留三倍样本量。使用SPARC标注微调的模型在物体定位和指针任务上优于同等规模模型，且在无手动验证标注的广义空间推理任务中仍保持竞争力；基于SPARC标注训练的策略在真实复杂、视觉模糊场景中表现优于基线方法。
Fourier Multi-Component and Multi-Layer Neural Networks: Unlocking High-Frequency Potential
Authors: Shijun Zhang, Hongkai Zhao, Yimin Zhong, Haomin Zhou
First: 2025-02-26T09:12:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:45:06+00:00
Comments: Our code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/ShijunZhangMath/FMMNN
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The architecture of a neural network and the choice of its activation function are both fundamental to its performance. Equally important is ensuring that these two elements are well matched, as their alignment is key to effective representation and learning. In this paper, we introduce the Fourier Multi-Component and Multi-Layer Neural Network (FMMNN), a model that combines sine-type activations with the multi-component and multi-layer structure of MMNNs. In an FMMNN, each component is represented as a trainable linear combination of fixed random sine-type basis functions, while multi-layer composition generates more complex and adaptive high-frequency features. We establish that FMMNNs retain exponential expressive power for function approximation even under a low-rank architectural structure. We also analyze the optimization landscape of FMMNNs and find it to be substantially more favorable than that of standard fully connected neural networks, especially for high-frequency targets. In addition, we propose a scaled random initialization method for the first-layer weights in FMMNNs, which accelerates training and improves final performance when sufficient samples are available. Extensive numerical experiments support our theoretical insights, showing that FMMNNs achieve strong accuracy and favorable convergence behavior on oscillatory function-approximation benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：傅里叶多组件多层神经网络：释放高频潜力
神经网络的架构及其激活函数的选择对其性能至关重要，二者需良好匹配以实现有效表示与学习。本文提出傅里叶多组件多层神经网络（FMMNN），结合正弦型激活函数与多组件多层结构。FMMNN中每个组件为固定随机正弦基函数的可训练线性组合，多层结构生成更复杂自适应的高频特征。我们证明FMMNN在低秩架构下仍保持函数逼近的指数表达能力，并分析其优化景观，发现其显著优于标准全连接网络，尤其对高频目标。此外，我们提出FMMNN第一层权重的缩放随机初始化方法，加速训练并提升充足样本下的最终性能。大量数值实验验证理论，显示FMMNN在振荡函数逼近基准上表现优异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better capture high-frequency patterns in function approximation, this paper proposes the Fourier Multi-Component and Multi-Layer Neural Network (FMMNN), which combines sine-type activations with a multi-component, multi-layer architecture. Each component is a trainable linear combination of fixed random sine basis functions, and stacking layers enables the network to generate adaptive high-frequency features. Theoretical analysis shows that FMMNNs maintain exponential expressive power even under low-rank constraints, and their optimization landscape is significantly more favorable than standard fully connected networks for high-frequency targets. Empirical results demonstrate that FMMNNs achieve superior accuracy and faster convergence on oscillatory benchmarks, especially when initialized with a scaled random method for the first layer.
为更有效地逼近高频函数，本文提出傅里叶多分量多层神经网络（FMMNN），结合正弦型激活函数与多分量多层结构。每个分量由固定随机正弦基函数的可训练线性组合表示，多层堆叠可生成自适应高频特征。理论分析表明，FMMNN在低秩结构下仍保持指数级表达能力，其优化景观对高频任务比标准全连接网络更优。实验结果表明，FMMNN在振荡函数逼近任务中表现更佳，尤其在采用第一层缩放随机初始化时，收敛更快、精度更高。
Budget-Constrained Step-Level Diffusion Caching
Authors: Mingkun Lei, Tong Zhao, Liangyu Yuan, Chi Zhang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-11T15:45:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:45:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Step-level caching accelerates diffusion models by exploiting temporal redundancy across denoising steps. Existing methods make per-step cache decisions using threshold-based heuristics, without directly optimizing for final output quality. As a result, their inference latency varies across inputs and is difficult to control at deployment. In this work, we propose BudCache, which inverts this formulation: rather than letting per-step error thresholds dictate the runtime cost, we fix the compute budget in advance and search for the cache policy that best preserves the final output. To tackle the combinatorial complexity of step selection, we combine Simulated Annealing with deterministic Hill Climbing. This offline search identifies high-quality cache policies within minutes and introduces no online search or thresholding overhead during inference. When the compute budget is very tight, we further introduce cache-aware schedule alignment, which adapts the time discretization to the selected cache policy to reduce cache-induced trajectory mismatch. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 show that BudCache achieves better generation quality than heuristic caching baselines under the same inference budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/BudCache
中文标题/摘要
标题：预算受限的步级扩散缓存
步级缓存通过利用去噪步骤间的时序冗余加速扩散模型。现有方法使用基于阈值的启发式规则进行每步缓存决策，未直接优化最终输出质量。因此，其推理延迟因输入而异，难以在部署时控制。本文提出BudCache，反转该范式：不再由每步误差阈值决定运行时成本，而是预先固定计算预算，搜索能最好保留最终输出的缓存策略。为应对步选择的组合复杂性，我们结合模拟退火与确定性爬坡算法。该离线搜索可在数分钟内识别高质量缓存策略，且推理时无需在线搜索或阈值开销。当计算预算非常紧张时，我们进一步引入缓存感知的调度对齐，通过调整时间离散化以适应所选缓存策略，减少缓存引发的轨迹不匹配。在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上的实验表明，BudCache在相同推理预算下比启发式缓存基线获得更优的生成质量。代码见https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/BudCache
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to control inference latency while preserving output quality in diffusion models, this work introduces BudCache, a budget-constrained caching method that optimizes cache policies offline to maximize final generation quality under fixed compute budgets. Instead of using per-step error thresholds, it employs Simulated Annealing combined with deterministic Hill Climbing to search for optimal step-level cache selections, avoiding online overhead. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 demonstrate that BudCache outperforms heuristic baselines in quality under identical budgets, and further improves efficiency via cache-aware schedule alignment when budgets are tight.
为在保持生成质量的同时控制推理延迟，本文提出BudCache方法，通过预设计算预算并离线搜索最优步级缓存策略，最大化最终输出质量。该方法摒弃基于误差阈值的启发式决策，采用模拟退火与确定性爬山算法组合进行策略搜索，避免在线开销。此外，针对预算紧张场景引入缓存感知的时间离散对齐机制，减少缓存导致的轨迹偏差。在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上的实验表明，BudCache在相同计算预算下优于启发式缓存基线方法，且延迟更稳定。
NavWAM: A Navigation World Action Model for Goal-Conditioned Visual Navigation
Authors: Daichi Azuma, Taiki Miyanishi, Koya Sakamoto, Shuhei Kurita, Yaonan Zhu, Petr Khrapchenkov, Motoaki Kawanabe, Yusuke Iwasawa, Yutaka Matsuo
First: 2026-06-11T15:44:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:44:36+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Goal-conditioned visual navigation requires a robot to act under partial observability by anticipating how its motion will change the future egocentric view and whether that change brings it closer to the goal. Navigation world models provide such visual foresight, but they remain prediction modules that require an external planner to convert predicted futures into closed-loop control. We propose Navigation World Action Model (NavWAM), a diffusion-transformer policy that turns navigation world-model prediction into executable action by representing future observations, goal-progress values, and action chunks in a shared latent sequence. By learning future prediction jointly with the action and value targets that determine closed-loop behavior, NavWAM makes visual foresight directly usable for robot control. We build NavWAM through simulation pretraining and real-robot adaptation, and evaluate it on image-goal navigation against planning-based world models and a representative direct navigation policy. Across offline benchmarks and closed-loop real-robot deployment, NavWAM improves over planning-based world-model baselines in our evaluations while using the default policy mode without CEM-style action search. Project page: https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/
中文标题/摘要
标题：NavWAM：一种面向目标条件视觉导航的导航世界动作模型
目标条件视觉导航要求机器人在部分可观测环境下行动，需预判自身运动如何改变未来的第一人称视角，以及该变化是否使其更接近目标。导航世界模型可提供此类视觉前瞻，但它们仍为预测模块，需外部规划器将预测未来转化为闭环控制。我们提出导航世界动作模型（NavWAM），一种扩散-Transformer策略，通过共享潜在序列表示未来观测、目标进度值和动作块，将导航世界模型预测转化为可执行动作。通过联合学习未来预测与决定闭环行为的动作和价值目标，NavWAM使视觉前瞻可直接用于机器人控制。我们通过仿真预训练和真实机器人适应构建NavWAM，并在图像目标导航任务中与基于规划的世界模型及代表性直接导航策略进行评估。在离线基准测试和闭环真实机器人部署中，NavWAM在无需CEM式动作搜索的默认策略模式下，优于基于规划的世界模型基线。项目页面：https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/

Motivated by the need for robots to navigate visually under partial observability while directly using foresight for control, the paper introduces NavWAM, a diffusion-transformer policy that integrates future visual predictions, goal-progress signals, and action chunks into a shared latent sequence. By jointly learning prediction and control targets during training, NavWAM enables closed-loop navigation without requiring external planners or action search methods like CEM. Evaluated on image-goal navigation tasks in both simulation and real-world settings, NavWAM outperforms planning-based world models and a direct navigation baseline, demonstrating improved performance across offline benchmarks and real-robot deployments.
为了解决机器人在部分可观测环境下通过预测未来视觉变化来实现目标导向导航的问题，该研究提出了NavWAM，一种将未来视觉状态、目标进度和动作块编码为共享潜在序列的扩散-Transformer策略，从而直接生成可执行动作，无需外部规划器或CEM式动作搜索。与基于规划的世界模型和直接导航策略相比，NavWAM在离线基准测试和真实机器人闭环部署中均表现更优，验证了其将视觉前瞻直接转化为控制的有效性。
Point-Wise Geometry-Aware Transformer for Partial-to-Full Point Cloud Registration in Computer-Assisted Surgery
Authors: Siyu Zhou, Zhongliang Jiang
First: 2026-06-11T15:37:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:37:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Partial-to-full registration remains challenging due to varying overlap ratios, fluctuating point densities, and the presence of noise. While transformers have shown strong potential for point cloud processing, prior methods typically confine them to global context aggregation, overlooking fine-grained local geometry crucial for accurate correspondence. We propose \emph{GAPR-Net}, a learning-based point cloud registration framework with a coarse-to-fine architecture that combines convolution and transformer modules, in which local and global information is fused between the partial and full point clouds using a cross-attention mechanism. To achieve this, a transformation-invariant point-wise geometric feature representation is proposed, which can robustly capture relative geometric features for individual points with respect to their neighboring points. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, experiments are conducted on four geometrically distinct bones, including the tibia, femur, pelvis, and thoracic cartilage. The overall registration recall reaches 94.2\%, the method results in a low RMSE of 1.992 mm and $R^2$ values of 0.908 and 0.974 for rotation and translation, respectively. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively addresses the partial-to-full point cloud registration problem. The proposed method enables highly accurate 3D point cloud registration using partial observation, providing a critical foundation for precise surgical navigation and robotic interventions in computer-assisted surgery. The code will be accessed after the double-blind review process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于计算机辅助手术中部分到完整点云配准的点感知几何感知变换器
由于重叠比例变化、点密度波动及噪声存在，部分到完整配准仍具挑战性。尽管变换器在点云处理中展现出强大潜力，但现有方法通常仅限于全局上下文聚合，忽视了对精确对应至关重要的细粒度局部几何信息。我们提出GAPR-Net，一种基于学习的点云配准框架，采用粗到精架构，结合卷积与变换器模块，通过交叉注意力机制在部分与完整点云间融合局部与全局信息。为此，我们提出一种变换不变的点级几何特征表示，可稳健地捕捉每个点相对于其邻近点的相对几何特征。为评估所提方法的有效性，我们在四类几何结构各异的骨骼（胫骨、股骨、骨盆、胸肋软骨）上进行实验。整体配准召回率达94.2%，方法获得低RMSE（1.992 mm）及旋转与平移的R²值分别为0.908和0.974。结果表明，所提方法有效解决了部分到完整点云配准问题，实现了基于部分观测的高精度三维点云配准，为计算机辅助手术中的精准手术导航与机器人干预提供了关键基础。代码将在双盲评审后开放。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of partial-to-full point cloud registration in computer-assisted surgery—such as variable overlap, density fluctuations, and noise—the paper introduces GAPR-Net, a coarse-to-fine learning framework that integrates convolutional and transformer modules. It employs a cross-attention mechanism to fuse local and global geometry between partial and full point clouds, enhanced by a transformation-invariant point-wise geometric feature representation that captures fine-grained relative geometry. Evaluated on four distinct bones (tibia, femur, pelvis, thoracic cartilage), the method achieves 94.2% registration recall, 1.992 mm RMSE, and R² values of 0.908 and 0.974 for rotation and translation, demonstrating robust performance and suitability for surgical navigation and robotic interventions.
为解决因重叠率、点密度波动和噪声导致的局部到全局点云配准难题，作者提出GAPR-Net框架，结合卷积与Transformer模块，通过跨注意力机制融合局部与全局几何信息。该方法引入变换不变的点级几何特征表示，有效捕捉点与其邻域的相对几何关系。在四类骨骼（胫骨、股骨、骨盆、胸骨软骨）上测试，实现94.2%召回率、1.992毫米RMSE及旋转与平移R²值分别为0.908和0.974，验证了其在计算机辅助手术导航中的高精度配准能力。
DecompSR: A dataset for decomposed analyses of compositional multihop spatial reasoning
Authors: Lachlan McPheat, Navdeep Kaur, Robert Blackwell, Alessandra Russo, Anthony G. Cohn, Pranava Madhyastha
First: 2025-11-04T14:57:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T15:36:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce DecompSR, decomposed spatial reasoning, a large benchmark dataset (over 5m datapoints) and generation framework designed to analyse compositional spatial reasoning ability. The generation of DecompSR allows users to independently vary several aspects of compositionality, namely: productivity (reasoning depth), substitutivity (entity and linguistic variability), overgeneralisation (input order, distractors) and systematicity (novel linguistic elements). DecompSR is built procedurally in a manner which makes it is correct by construction, which is independently verified using a symbolic solver to guarantee the correctness of the dataset. DecompSR is comprehensively benchmarked across a host of Large Language Models (LLMs) where we show that LLMs struggle with productive and systematic generalisation in spatial reasoning tasks whereas they are more robust to linguistic variation. DecompSR provides a provably correct and rigorous benchmarking dataset with a novel ability to independently vary the degrees of several key aspects of compositionality, allowing for robust and fine-grained probing of the compositional reasoning abilities of LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DecompSR：用于分解分析组合式多跳空间推理的数据集
我们引入DecompSR，一种分解式空间推理的大规模基准数据集（超过500万个数据点）及生成框架，旨在分析组合式空间推理能力。DecompSR的生成机制允许用户独立调节组合性的多个方面：生产力（推理深度）、可替换性（实体与语言变体）、过度泛化（输入顺序、干扰项）和系统性（新颖语言元素）。DecompSR以程序化方式构建，确保其构造正确性，并通过符号求解器独立验证，以保证数据集的正确性。DecompSR在多种大型语言模型（LLMs）上进行了全面基准测试，结果显示LLMs在空间推理任务中难以实现生产力和系统性的泛化，但对语言变体更具鲁棒性。DecompSR提供了一个可证明正确的严谨基准数据集，具备独立调节组合性关键方面程度的全新能力，从而支持对LLMs组合推理能力进行稳健且精细的探测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to rigorously probe compositional spatial reasoning in large language models, the authors introduce DecompSR, a large-scale benchmark dataset with over 5 million datapoints generated via a procedural framework that allows independent manipulation of key compositional dimensions: productivity, substitutivity, overgeneralisation, and systematicity. The dataset is constructed to be correct by design and verified symbolically to ensure reliability. Experimental results across multiple LLMs reveal that models perform poorly on tasks requiring productive and systematic generalisation, while showing greater robustness to linguistic variation and entity substitution.
为系统评估大语言模型在组合式空间推理中的能力，作者构建了DecompSR数据集，包含超过500万条数据，可独立调节组合性的四个关键维度：生产力、替代性、过度泛化和系统性。该数据集通过程序化生成并经符号求解器验证，确保正确性，便于精准评测。实验表明，模型在生产力和系统性泛化任务上表现不佳，但在语言变体方面较为稳健，揭示了其组合推理能力的局限性。
